Chapter 651: Fishman Youngster – Cang Xu?!

Lady Sea Snake channeled her magic into the scroll, which then slipped from her hands and floated slowly before her.

The scroll emitted a faint blue glow.

She deliberately bit her finger, releasing a trickle of blood.

Guided by her will, the blood seeped into the scroll's surface, staining only the serpentine totem crimson, leaving the other patterns and runes untouched.

In truth, the serpentine totem itself had been drawn primarily with Lady Sea Snake's blood.

Thus, the blood swiftly merged with the serpentine totem, activating it.

For this magic contract scroll, Lady Sea Snake's blood served as the key to its activation.

Even if someone were to steal this scroll, without Lady Sea Snake's blood, it would be nothing more than a worthless piece of paper.

The first step was crucial. Only after Lady Sea Snake completed it did she proceed with confidence, channeling her magic to fully activate the scroll.

The primary magic formation within the scroll began to radiate a deep blue glow. The light flickered and swirled ceaselessly, gradually illuminating each magic rune.

When every rune and magic line was lit, the blue light reached its peak intensity.

The blinding blue light shone upon the gold level sea serpent, simultaneously illuminating Lady Sea Snake, binding their minds together in a spiritual connection.

At that moment, Lady Sea Snake's brow furrowed deeply, her face contorted with agony. Her blood seemed to boil throughout her body, her head felt as though struck by an invisible iron hammer, her spirit subjected to a grueling ordeal.

After all, this was a silver level being forcibly resisting a gold level, an act of defiance against a superior.

As for the gold level sea serpent, its entire body convulsed violently, as if being savagely electrocuted.

Ba Qi firmly restrained the serpent, ensuring it could not truly resist.

After several seconds, the blinding blue light gradually stabilized and softened. Lady Sea Snake's expression of agony eased considerably, allowing the contract process to proceed smoothly.

But at that very moment, a powerful surge of magic power erupted violently from the nearby sea!

In the blink of an eye, the level of this magic fluctuation skyrocketed from bronze level to gold level.

Necromancy - Soul Scream Cannon!

On the deck of the ghost ship, countless ghosts swirled and danced behind a hastily deployed silver level cannon.

The dense mass of ghosts coalesced into a circular, pitch-black vortex. A gold level ghost plunged into its center, its form compressed and reshaped by the vortex into a cannonball.

Once loaded into the cannon, it was immediately fired.

The undead, already close to the battlefield, had long since brought Lady Sea Snake within effective range. This cannon shot caught the pirates completely off guard!

“I've never seen such a tactic before!” Cang Xu closely monitored the results of the bombardment.

He noticed that the gold level ghost transformed into the cannonball clearly bore signs of modification.

Despite being hampered by the silver level cannon, the gold level ghost's role as projectile, enhanced by the gold level Jian Gui's spellcasting and amplified by the gold level ghost ship, delivered far greater destructive force than the Deep Sea Monster Fish's own gold level cannon and gold level cannonballs combined! Factoring in the undead's new faction advantage only amplified this superiority.

Faced with such bombardment, Ba Qi and his companions were shocked.

“A gold level spell!”

“Those are necromancers!”

Amidst the collective shouts, Lady Sea Snake swiftly ascended into the air, attempting to evade the cannon fire.

Having clashed with Jian Gui and others before, she was the most prepared for this sudden turn of events, making her reaction remarkably swift.

But the artillery fire wasn't aimed at Lady Sea Snake, nor at the Fluffy Cloud. Its target was the immobilized gold level sea serpent.

“No!”

Amid Lady Sea Snake's shrieks, the cannonball struck the sea serpent with pinpoint accuracy, blowing it into a pile of flesh and bone fragments. The sea serpent's gold level soul was utterly obliterated in an instant.

Crucially, Lady Sea Snake was midway through establishing a magic contract with the serpent—a process that hadn't halted. A psychic link bound them.

One's brutal death instantly inflicted severe damage on the other.

Lady Sea Snake spat blood, her body staggering violently. She nearly tumbled into the sea, barely managing to stabilize and remain suspended in the air.

Ruan Tang Ren let out a startled cry, intending to assist her. But as he rose to his feet, his legs buckled, and he collapsed to his knees.

His energy was utterly depleted. He was powerless, his combat strength reduced to nothing.

“Necromancers! You're too arrogant!” Ba Qi roared, leaping from the Fluffy Cloud and crashing into the sea surface.

Boom!

A massive column of water erupted as Ba Qi charged toward the ghost ship like a mountain tiger.

This was a shrewd tactical choice; only close combat could fully leverage a fighter's inherent advantage against a magician.

However, Jian Gui was prepared.

He had been biding his time all this while, not wasting his efforts, but quietly brewing his spell in silence.

Black soul chains coiled around Ba Qi.

The Soul Scream Cannon fired once more, striking the restrained Ba Qi.

Boom!

A deafening explosion sent the sea surging upward like a mountain.

Two gold level ghost “shells” were fired. The silver level cannon on the ghost ship's deck dimmed completely, as if struck by a petrification spell. It turned entirely grayish white, losing all metallic luster. It had been so severely corroded by negative energy that it was utterly useless now.

Lady Sea Snake, her body heavy with wounds, attempted to fly back to the Fluffy Cloud.

During this process, bone spears flew toward her, and ghosts whirled into the air, blocking her path.

“Those damned necromancers! Qi Ling, act now!” With her life threatened, Lady Sea Snake abandoned all pretense and issued a direct command.

Qi Ling immediately descended from the Fluffy Cloud, staff in hand.

As she formally unleashed necromancy magic, her true identity was exposed.

Shock rippled through the Fluffy Cloud. No one had imagined that Qi Ling, who had lived among them for so long, always quiet and unassuming, was actually a necromancer!

“This is troublesome.” Cang Xu, who had been attacking her remotely with spells, immediately felt conflicted. He found it difficult to strike Qi Ling with full force.

Their mission was to save her, not kill her.

Qi Ling was bound by her pact with Lady Sea Snake, she had no choice but to fight with everything she had.

With Qi Ling holding them off, Lady Sea Snake made it safely back onto the Fluffy Cloud.

Ruan Tang Ren hurried to support her, his heart aching for the gravely wounded Lady Sea Snake. He paid no heed to Qi Ling, who had suddenly revealed her true identity.

Lady Sea Snake scanned the deck and called out, "Everyone, rest assured. I have Qi Ling under my control. She may be a necromancer, but she is my slave!"

The pirates aboard the Fluffy Cloud stirred once more at these words.

Seeing Qi Ling “loyally” battling the unexpected enemy, they began to believe Lady Sea Snake's claim.

“Don't be anxious,” Cang Xu advised, his hand resting on Xiao Wang Zi's shoulder from within the ghost ship's cabin, “air combat favors them. Lure Qi Ling into the sea first.”

A wise man would choose his own battlefield.

Xiao Wang Zi nodded in agreement with Cang Xu's suggestion.

As for Ku Feng, he guarded the two magicians.

Qi Ling understood “cooperation” well. She plunged directly into the sea, commanding the water ghosts to attack the ghost ship.

These water ghosts could indeed unleash their full combat power underwater, ensuring that Qi Ling didn't violate her magic pact with Lady Sea Snake.

“Chaos!”

“Who knew there was a group of necromancers too?”

The fishmen of the Da Ji Clan, already retreating to a distance, witnessed the sudden outbreak of battle and retreated further in fright.

Qi Ling fought alone against the combined forces of Xiao Wang Zi and Cang Xu, while the water ghosts tangled with the death heads and the skeleton army.

“What the hell are these flying death heads?” Qi Ling wondered in alarm.

“Too bad I can't deploy my skeleton turtle cavalry. Otherwise, these water ghosts wouldn't stand a chance!” Xiao Wang Zi scoffed.

The ghost ship, carrying the necromancers, engaged in a retreating battle against Ba Qi. Meanwhile, vast numbers of undead creatures, commanded by the necromancers, filled the gaps across the battlefield. They made extensive contact and began a fierce struggle.

Ba Qi bore minor wounds.

That cannon blast had indeed packed a punch. Despite deploying his defensive battle skill, he couldn't shield himself completely.

Yet such injuries didn't hinder his combat prowess. Ba Qi accelerated relentlessly, closing in on the ghost ship with full force.

The ghost ship fought while retreating. Merging with Jian Gui, it shed its usual rigidity, becoming remarkably agile and alert.

A dozen or so gold level ghosts, each modified to varying degrees, circled and swirled around the ghost ship, forming the main force hindering Ba Qi's approach.

Ba Qi continuously unleashed his battle skills, while the dozen gold level ghosts, under the command of Jian Gui, advanced and retreated with precision, their coordination seamless.

The situation reached a brief stalemate, and Lady Sea Snake aboard the Fluffy Cloud grew impatient.

“Fire the cannons!” she ordered on behalf of Ruan Tang Ren.

But the other pirates ignored her, all eyes fixed on Ruan Tang Ren.

Ruan Tang Ren, however, stared back at Lady Sea Snake, his face filled with worry and concern. Realizing the situation, he quickly issued the command: “Aim at the ghost ship! Fire hard!”

The Fluffy Cloud carried numerous silver level cannons and a substantial supply of ammunition.

Silver level cannons firing silver level shells pounded the sea in rapid succession, sending a dozen water spouts skyward.

Upon impact, the shells trailed long, bubbly white tails through the water, hurtling toward the ghost ship.

The ghost ship moved swiftly, evading most of the cannonballs. Then Ku Feng personally intervened, slicing two solid cannonballs clean in half.

Yet to Lady Sea Snake's eyes, this scene appeared entirely different.

She saw nearly every cannonball strike the ghost ship, which was shattered beyond recognition amid the explosions.

Jian Gui was forced to reveal himself. His aura weakened, the ghost ship's severe damage had also affected him.

Ba Qi charged toward the necromancers.

The magicians scattered like birds and beasts.

As a fighter, Ku Feng stayed behind to cover their retreat. He was the first to fall in battle.

Next were Cang Xu and Xiao Wang Zi.

Finally, Jian Gui was also slain by Ba Qi!

“Truly worthy of Lord Ba Qi!!” Lady Sea Snake watched, her heart racing with exhilaration.

“The only pity is my sea serpent... wait, what's that?” She suddenly spotted a gold level sea serpent charging into the battlefield, seemingly drawn by the scent of blood.

“Launch the ship! Fly above that sea serpent!” she commanded.

The pirates heard and saw Ruan Tang Ren shouting, and immediately obeyed, maneuvering the Fluffy Cloud slowly into position.

Ba Qi unleashed his battle skills, charging forward relentlessly, sweeping away the gold level ghosts like withered leaves.

Not only that, but with the Fluffy Cloud's cannons providing support, he successfully sank the ghost ship.

After a final fierce battle, he also eliminated Jian Gui and his crew.

"Necromancer? Hmph, this time you'll die completely." Just as Ba Qi was feeling triumphant, sudden agony struck him, causing him to spit out a mouthful of blood.

He abruptly looked down at his midsection, where thick black smoke billowed, emanating a potent undead aura.

“Necromancy magic?”

“When?”

He immediately recalled the battle moments ago. He had taken a mysterious spell from Jian Gui head-on, seizing the opportunity to kill him.

“So that was the gold level necromancer's last-ditch counterattack!”

The next instant, Ba Qi's body shook violently again.

He discovered two more areas on his body erupting with darkness.

Ba Qi hastily activated defensive battle skills while downing potions to heal his wounds.

The reality was that the gold level octopusman fighter, Zhang Yu Wan, wielded axes, halberds, swords, and spears, relentlessly stabbing at Ba Qi's sides.

The dark smoke Ba Qi perceived was actually his own blood spurting forth.

Ba Qi reached for a potion to drink, only for Zhang Yu Wan to snatch it away. Unwittingly, he gulped down a mouthful of seawater instead.

Divine spell—Opportunistic Deception!

Secretly cast by the saint level octopusman Zhou Zhang, the divine spell's fluctuations went undetected. Yet every enemy sensing the waves was simultaneously deceived, instinctively ignoring it.

Zhang Yu Wan circled Ba Qi, stabbing relentlessly with his weapons until the latter was gravely wounded. Then, he clenched his fist and drove it into the back of Ba Qi's neck with a powerful hammer blow, knocking him unconscious.

Meanwhile, the Fluffy Cloud landed on the ocean surface. Another gold level octopusman, Jiu Hua Bi, used his four thick tentacles to bind the key pirates: Lady Sea Snake, Ruan Tang Ren, and the first mate.

Moments later, Cang Xu and the others also fell captive to the undersea army, all taken alive.

Ocean Mother Nest.

“They're back?” The fishman youngster had already detected the army's movements outside the Mother Nest through his scouting puppet.

He watched closely.

When the commotion began, temptation flickered in the fishman youngster's heart.

Could the departure of Zhou Zhang and the others be his chance to escape?

After a moment's hesitation, he dismissed the thought.

The risk was too great.

Though Zhou Zhang and the others had departed, many remained behind, the three gold levels who were troublesome enough. There were numerous amounts of silver levels and iron levels too.

Staying here, however, yielded astonishing rewards for the fishman youngster - the Pearl Bubbles from the Mermaid Fairy Tale kept multiplying, nearly overflowing!

Crucially, the fishman youngster couldn't be certain of further intelligence. What if Zhou Zhang and the others had suddenly departed merely to rendezvous with a large-scale sea beast horde?

Such scenarios have occurred multiple times before.

Only Zhou Zhang rarely went out alone; most missions involved the other two gold level octopusman fighters.

This time, all three octopusmen had mobilized, signaling something unusual to the fishman youngster.

The fishman youngster remained stationed in the cockpit, closely monitoring the activity outside the mother nest through the magic projection.

He eagerly anticipated a powerful beast tide disrupting the order outside the nest, giving him an opportunity to take advantage of the chaos.

But his hopes were quickly dashed.

He witnessed Zhou Zhang and the others returning.

“They came back so quickly, thankfully I didn’t risk it.” The fishman youngster felt a sense of relief.

"This time, they actually captured so many human prisoners? Judging by their attire, are they pirates?"

“There's even a gold level fighter...” The fishman youngster saw the unconscious Ba Qi for the first time.

“Wait, Lady Sea Snake?” The fishman youngster spotted Lady Sea Snake. He had crossed paths with her during the Weeping Pine Naval Battle.

“Who'd have thought that the Fire Beard Pirates would meet their end here today,” the fishman youngster murmured with a faint smile.

These human prisoners had all been spellbound, their lives temporarily preserved in the sea.

But the fishman youngster understood Zhou Zhang and the others' purpose: to keep them alive temporarily before sacrificing them to the Charming Blue Goddess. Living sacrifices were naturally best for blood offerings.

“Clearly, Lady Sea Snake's faith shouldn't be in the Charming Blue Goddess.” Regarding his former enemy, the fishman youngster naturally delighted in seeing her meet a gruesome end at the hands of the saint level sea beast of the Ocean Mother Nest.

"Perhaps I can make the first move? Acquiring a human magic bloodline wouldn't be bad either.” The fishman youngster schemed inwardly.

"Oh? A gold level necromancer... Seems vaguely familiar." The fishman youngster spotted Jian Gui.

However, Jian Gui wore a full black robe with a hood, lacking any particularly distinctive features.

The next moment, the fishman youngster's eyes widened in shock.

What was he seeing?

"Cang Xu?

How did he get captured by Zhou Zhang!”

“Wait, so that means the one in front of him is Jian Gui?” Then, the fishman youngster spotted familiar faces: Xiao Wang Zi, Ku Feng, and Qing Xin.

The fishman youngster: "…"

Chapter 652: Converting to Another Religion

The fishman youngster never imagined he would meet Cang Xu under such circumstances.

“This is truly troublesome,” the fishman youngster murmured, his expression heavy with worry.

Even saving himself was difficult now, and Cang Xu had become a prisoner of the saint level priestess Zhou Zhang. Therefore, the fishman youngster not only had to save himself but also rescue Cang Xu.

“What am I to do?” The fishman youngster once again felt the inadequacy of his own strength.

He was merely a silver level. Yet before him, even the gold level fighter and necromancer had been easily captured by Zhou Zhang.

The might of the saint level was too formidable for a gold level to withstand, far surpassing the difficulty of a silver level slaying a gold level!

Besides Zhou Zhang, there were at least five other gold level enemies. Silver level and iron level foes were even more numerous.

If the fishman youngster attempted a direct confrontation, he would have to face an entire undersea army!

Seeing the fate of the necromancers, he felt fortunate he hadn't taken the risk earlier.

Even with his Bloodcore absorbing undead bloodlines, allowing him to transform into an undead, acting alone would still be extremely dangerous.

The moment he became a ghost, passing through the walls of the Ocean Mother Nest to the outside world, would be the moment of his complete destruction. Or he'd suffer the same fate as Cang Xu and the others, captured.

From these prisoners, the fishman youngster keenly sensed Zhou Zhang's formidable power. Even without witnessing the battle firsthand.

Zhou Zhang had captured alive Ba Qi, Lady Sea Snake, Ruan Tang Ren, and the necromancers led by Jian Gui. Such a feat was terrifying.

After all, killing and capturing alive were entirely different levels of difficulty.

Especially when the fishman youngster calculated the time, Zhou Zhang had departed the altar and returned in mere moments. To capture so many powerful foes in such a short span... Such combat prowess was truly terrifying upon reflection.

The prisoners were escorted beneath the altar.

The octopusman escorting the captured Ba Qi was named Zhang Yu Wan, he cursed under his breath before delivering a vicious kick to Ba Qi's shin.

Ba Qi's combat power had been completely sealed.

Zhou Zhang had subsequently cast a divine spell upon him, allowing him to breathe freely underwater and withstand the immense pressure of the sea, yet leaving him utterly powerless to resist.

The brutal kick sent Ba Qi crashing to his knees.

It must be said, this battle was undoubtedly the most humiliating for Ba Qi.

He possessed the Bad Weather bloodline, cultivated the Air Pressure battle qi, and mastered the racial magic Air Element Transformation.

His trump card made him virtually devoid of any fatal structural weaknesses in his body.

Yet he was enveloped by the Opportunistic Deception Spell, completely unaware he was under attack. Even when he thought he was consuming healing potions, his enemies had stolen them, yet he remained oblivious.

His formidable racial magic Air Element Transformation remained entirely unused.

It could be said Zhou Zhang had completely countered him, rendering his formidable power utterly useless.

But victory was victory, defeat was defeat. Ba Qi's face darkened, yet he acknowledged the truth. He knelt on both knees, utterly still, offering no resistance.

Soon after, Lady Sea Snake was dragged to Ba Qi's side and forced to kneel.

Perhaps the kneeling motion was too violent, for she coughed up a mouthful of blood. Her expression was utterly exhausted, her gaze dim and lifeless, stripped of her usual radiance.

Her current predicament was an utterly crushing blow!

Lady Sea Snake had considered Jian Gui and others but never imagined encountering a being like Zhou Zhang.

Success had been within her grasp, only to slip away at the last moment. Not only was the forcibly contracted gold level sea serpent utterly destroyed, but she herself had become a prisoner.

The fall from grace was staggering.

Ruan Tang Ren followed closely behind, forced to his knees.

His condition was equally dire. To subdue the gold level sea serpent, he had not only expended every ounce of strength but also downed two potent potions.

Now, his entire body felt like jelly, utterly devoid of resistance. He couldn't even maintain his kneeling posture, soon collapsing face-down onto the ground.

Even in this state, he hadn't forgotten to care for his beloved.

Ruan Tang Ren dragged himself forward inch by inch, his chin scraping the floor as he moved toward Lady Sea Snake.

“No, don't be afraid. At least we're still alive,” he managed to say, his words strained.

Lady Sea Snake remained unresponsive, her expression vacant.

Seeing his beloved in this state, Ruan Tang Ren felt a sharp pang of sorrow.

Jian Gui soon knelt beside them, joining the line formed by Ba Qi and the others.

Xiao Wang Zi sobbed quietly while being pressed close against him.

Ku Feng had lost one arm. This was the result of his reaction mid-battle, after the effect of the Opportunistic Deception Spell faded, as he fought back with all his might.

His expression was as hard as iron. His combat prowess was at the peak of the silver level, but his opponent was Zhang Yu Wan of the gold level. He had managed to survive due to Zhang Yu Wan deliberately holding back.

Cang Xu, however, suffered little damage.

Of course, this was because he had regained his senses and deliberately ceased resisting.

Cang Xu possessed the weakest strength. In such circumstances, the weakest often fared better than the stronger.

Cang Xu raised his eyes to observe Zhou Zhang ascending the altar, surrounded by the vast undersea army, his heart filled with bitter resentment.

The necromancers had sought to play the role of the third party, only for the true victors to be Zhou Zhang's faction, who had struck last.

This was a crushing defeat in every sense!

"Damn it! Now a prisoner, facing enemies too powerful to hope of escape."

“Must I truly meet my end here this time?”

Cang Xu felt profound resentment, especially knowing the Bloodcore existed.

Yet he also silently urged himself to remain calm: "Since these octopusman didn't kill me outright on the battlefield but instead captured us all and even kindly cast divine magic to keep us alive underwater for now... if they truly intended to kill us, this wouldn't be the moment."

Qing Xin was eventually escorted to Cang Xu's side.

She knelt down of her own accord.

The expression on this wind magician's face was numb.

Her situation had already been dire aboard the ghost ship.

She never imagined that after this battle, she would become a prisoner.

For Qing Xin, it truly felt like there was no worst living environment, only worse ones!

Qi Ling was not spared either, joining the ranks of kneeling captives.

Yet among all the prisoners, her situation might be the least dire.

For she had connections to the Da Ji Clan. After their retreat, the Da Ji Clan had now reunited with Zhou Zhang and the others. This offered Qi Ling a glimmer of hope.

If Zhou Zhang had accepted the Da Ji Clan, perhaps she might also accept her - this special fishman who had always prioritized the tribe, who even after becoming a necromancer, secretly protected them?

Besides those kneeling in the front row like Ba Qi and Jian Gui, every pirate aboard the Fluffy Cloud, and every crew member of the Heroism Pirates, had been rounded up.

But they weren't worthy of kneeling before the altar.

The fishmen of the Da Ji Clan were not treated as prisoners.

Qi Ren, Qi Shu, and Qi Miao scanned their surroundings.

The immense magic formation sprawling across the mountain wall left them awestruck. The sight of the gold level beast bone altar filled them with awe.

Seeing their enemies reduced to captives, a sense of satisfaction and excitement at vengeance filled their hearts.

The clan priestess, Qi Miao, whispered to the clan leader, Qi Ren: “Lord Qi Ling has also been taken captive. Should we report this situation to Lord Zhou Zhang?”

Qi Ren's face darkened as he shook his head slightly. "Now is not the time. We must endure for now. For the moment, they should not be in mortal danger. My greater concern is that we hold no standing to plead for a necromancer before Lord Zhou Zhang. Haven't you seen the looks on the others' faces?"

The vast undersea army encircling the altar fixed their gaze upon the necromancers, their eyes brimming with intense loathing, hatred, fear, and murderous intent.

Compared to the human Ba Qi and Lady Sea Snake, the necromancers were clearly far less welcome.

Zhou Zhang had ascended the altar, surveying everyone below.

Her gaze swept lightly over the others before settling on the captives like Ba Qi.

Zhou Zhang spoke in the Universal Language, introducing herself: "I am Zhou Zhang, devoted entirely to serving our deity, Charming Blue Goddess. As one of the archbishops of the Charming Blue Church, your capture by my hand must be the will of the gods, the arrangement of fate."

“Now, you have a choice.”

“Choose to convert to our deity, the Charming Blue Goddess, and you may gain the chance to survive, even freedom.”

“But if you cling to your former faith, utter annihilation will be your only fate.”

As a saint level archbishop of the Charming Blue Church, spreading the goddess's faith had always been her primary duty.

Hearing her words, the captives revealed expressions of shock.

“Charming Blue?”

“Wasn't she already destroyed?”

“No. As long as a single believer remains, the deity is immortal, at most, she has merely fallen into slumber.”

“So that's why the saint level priestess didn't exterminate us outright...”

After the initial shock, the captives fell into silence.

Convert?

For them, this was an extremely weighty and difficult decision.

In this world, nearly everyone held onto their own beliefs. Many devout believers would rather die than renounce their faith. To these believers, death held no terror. By holding fast to their faith, even in death, they might earn the deity's favor, be guided to the divine domain, and thereafter enjoy the blissful life of heaven.

Zhou Zhang's threat of life and death seemed forceful, but in truth, for some deeply devout believers, it was a negligible threat.

The difficulty of conversion also signifies the difficulty of proselytizing.

Typically, the more powerful the deity, the easier it was to spread their faith. But Charming Blue Goddess was clearly not a powerful deity, and her situation was dire. After that divine war, she had remained in slumber.

Given sufficient time, Zhou Zhang might have employed gentler, more measured methods for proselytizing. But now, tasked with presiding over a blood sacrifice, she possessed neither the time nor the patience. Thus, she resorted to threatening life and death, forcing conversion through sheer force.

Chapter 653: Rescued Due to Unexpected Events

Seeing the captives remain silent, Zhou Zhang said nothing further. Instead, she signaled with her eyes to the gold level octopusman, Jiu Hua Bi, standing nearby.

Jiu Hua Bi immediately understood Zhou Zhang's intent. With a wave of his hand, he directed the others to escort the sea beast herd onto the altar.

Thus, under the prisoners' gaze, a blood sacrifice commenced.

Though the fishman youngster hiding within the Ocean Mother Nest had witnessed such rituals countless times, it was the prisoners' first encounter.

They watched the entire process from start to finish, their eyes wide with astonishment, feeling the immense power of this hybrid formation blending magic and divine text.

The captives finally understood why such immense power lay concealed within White Hair Trench.

“As a saint level archbishop of the Charming Blue Church, she intends to hunt sea beasts here on a massive scale, sacrificing them to her deity.”

“The beast tide in White Hair Trench is their perfect opportunity to gather ample offerings for the blood sacrifice.”

"We're utterly doomed. Captured by these people, we're being forced to convert. Refuse, and we'll likely be sacrificed!"

“Life or death?”

“Convert or not?”

The captives faced an agonizing choice.

At that moment, one captive spoke up first: “I’ll convert!”

All eyes instantly focused on him.

People quickly recognized the one declaring conversion as the highest level necromancer among them -the gold level Jian Gui.

Zhou Zhang's expression remained unchanged as she tilted her chin slightly. The octopusman behind Jian Gui immediately moved to support him to his feet.

Jian Gui was finally free from kneeling.

He raised his head to look at Zhou Zhang on the altar, bowing slowly as he introduced himself: “Archbishop of the Charming Blue Goddess, Zhou Zhang of the saint level... I am Jian Gui, the direct heir to the orthodox necromancer lineage...”

Jian Gui stated his identity and revealed his teacher was one of the Twelve Saints. Finally, he invoked the name of You Shen. This legendary figure, renowned throughout the world for founding the necromancer faction, was a true superstar!

Jian Gui passionately expressed his viewpoint: the necromancer orthodoxy and the Charming Blue Church were not adversaries; cooperation between the two was entirely possible.

Yet these words did nothing to soften the expressions of his enemies. Instead, the disgust and suspicion on many faces only deepened.

Zhang Yu Wan snorted coldly, fixing a piercing stare on Jian Gui: "Necromancer orthodoxy? So, you're a blasphemous priest, are you?"

With those words, a torrent of murderous intent surged toward Jian Gui from all directions.

The hatred between blasphemous priests and the various religions and their believers ran deep, their conflict utterly irreconcilable, a complete and utter enmity!

Jian Gui hastily clarified that while he belonged to the orthodox necromancer faction, he was not a blasphemous priest.

He further emphasized: While many blasphemous priests existed within the orthodox undead faction, not a single one had ever dared to offend the Charming Blue Goddess.

This much was true.

At least to Zhou Zhang's knowledge, no necromancer had ever desecrated her sacred faith.

Jian Gui concluded with a solemn oath: Even if he gained such power in the future, he would never dare to offend the Charming Blue Goddess. To prove his sincerity, he declared he would convert on the spot, join the Charming Blue Church, and become the world's first gold level necromancer to worship the goddess! Moreover, when the opportunity arose, he would preach to other necromancers.

These words moved many believers.

Zhou Zhang stared at Jian Gui for a long moment before speaking in the Universal Language: “All things should worship my Lord, and necromancers are no exception. Jian Gui, if you truly can spread the Goddess's faith among necromancers, I shall reward you according to your merits.”

"I will believe you for now, but you must prove your faith to us later. Do not be a cunning fox or jackal."

"I hope you truly believe in my Lord. Should we discover you deceiving us, even with You Shen standing behind you, you shall pay an unimaginably heavy price."

Jian Gui hastily pledged he would keep his word and devote himself wholeheartedly to the worship of the Charming Blue Goddess. He was already eager to learn the relevant prayers and perform his very first prayer.

Yet deep within, Jian Gui sneered inwardly: "If Founder You Shen truly stood behind me, would you dare repeat those words just now? Zhou Zhang!"

Circumstances dictated his attitude. Jian Gui chose outward obedience while secretly seeking an opportunity to call for aid.

During the previous battle, his focus had been entirely drawn to Ba Qi, leaving him utterly unprepared for the Opportunistic Deception Spell. This left him unable to even make the gesture to send a distress signal.

Even if he had sent one, the chances of receiving rescue at the level of the Twelve Saints were slim.

That battle on the Underworld River had been more a matter of good fortune for Jian Gui. One of the Twelve Saints, Doctor Sui Shi, happened to be nearby and could receive his distress signal.

With Jian Gui as an example, Xiao Wang Zi and the others immediately understood what they must do.

It had to be said, necromancers possessed remarkably flexible faith boundaries.

Xiao Wang Zi, whimpering softly, feared death intensely. He dreaded never seeing his parents again.

But before Xiao Wang Zi could speak, another voice piped up first: “I too shall convert to the great Charming Blue Goddess!”

All turned to see who had spoken. The speaker was unexpected; it was Ba Qi.

Though Ba Qi held his own beliefs, his faith ran shallow, at best, he was a casual believer.

After becoming a prisoner, Ba Qi swiftly accepted his reality and chose submission. This likely stemmed from his past experience being imprisoned in the Abyss Hanging Tower.

With the two most powerful gold level captives having both declared their conversion and demonstrated their allegiance, the remaining prisoners swiftly followed suit: each expressed their willingness to convert.

Thus, these individuals were helped to their feet, though the shackles around their wrists and ankles remained fastened.

Zhou Zhang issued further orders. She instructed priests of the Charming Blue Church to lead the captives, teaching them the church's prayers and ritual protocols. They were also tasked with guiding the captives to settle here, overseeing and managing their daily lives.

To be spared like this, freed from the threat of death, felt almost unbelievable to Cang Xu and the others.

“They just let us go like that?”

“She actually believes we've converted?”

“The Charming Blue believers around us are looking at us strangely. There must be something fishy going on!”

If he could speak to them directly, the fishman youngster would tell them: 'You're thinking far too simplistically! Next, you'll be transported to the Ocean Mother Nest. The saint level sea beast within the Mother Nest will be the most rigorous test of your faith. The slightest deficiency in faith will see you ruthlessly slaughtered, becoming one of the blood sacrifices.'

But for now, the fishman youngster heaved a huge sigh of relief.

If Zhou Zhang had tried to execute the prisoners in public, the fishman youngster would be forced to break out of the Ocean Mother Nest and attack them with the Deep Sea Monster Fish in an attempt to save Cang Xu.

He could never stand by and watch his comrade die horribly.

That was utterly beyond him.

For the fishman youngster, this turn of events was undoubtedly the best possible outcome!

Thanks to the intelligence he'd gathered earlier, he had already guessed Zhou Zhang's next move.

“Once Cang Xu is teleported in, I can rescue him.”

“When he's brought aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish and sees me, he'll be shocked, right?” The fishman youngster smiled, anticipating that moment.

"I set out from Snow Bird Harbor originally to rescue Lan Zao. I failed to save Lan Zao and ended up trapped in Ocean Mother Nest myself. Now, by some twist of fate, I'm going to save Cang Xu."

In that moment, the fishman youngster felt the capriciousness of destiny.

“Ah, I wonder how Lan Zao is doing now?”

Lan Zao was doing quite well.

He swam freely through the ocean.

Through days of training, he had grown quite familiar with his new life form.

He constantly practiced breathing through his gills, striving to make the action instinctive.

Before this, he had “accidentally” encountered Ba Qi, enduring a trial. After falling into the water, Lan Zao had fallen unconscious, ceasing his gill breathing.

This was a weakness. Following Shuang Tu's reminder, he was now actively working to improve it.

Ba Qi's infused battle qi allowed Lan Zao to complete his energy transformation in minimal time.

Now, Lan Zao practiced the Air Pressure battle qi technique.

Battle skill -Air Cannon.

Lan Zao slowed his swimming pace, covertly contracting his muscles to compress the air sac within his body. The stored compressed air was immediately forced out in a mass, traveling through his windpipe into his mouth.

From the outside, Lan Zao's Adam's apple rolled, followed by his cheeks suddenly bulging.

Seizing the moment, Lan Zao opened his mouth and fired an air cannonball.

The projectile flew dozens of meters before exploding with a boom.

Lan Zao assessed that its destructive force should rival that of an iron level cannonball.

However, since the test was conducted deep underwater, the sea surface remained perfectly calm.

After fully converting his battle qi, Lan Zao's previous injuries had completely healed.

Currently, he was focusing on mastering the Air Cannon battle skill.

It must be said that the study materials provided by Shuang Tu have been immensely beneficial to him. Combined with his bloodline and battle qi technique being highly compatible, Lan Zao's cultivation of this battle skill has progressed rapidly.

He has now fully grasped it. Immersed in his training, he gradually lost track of time until Shuang Tu teleported over to find him.

Shuang Tu addressed Lan Zao: “Your training session is over, Hen Fa.”

“Lunch is ready for you.”

Lan Zao nodded his thanks to Shuang Tu.

He understood clearly: his rapid progress these past days stemmed partly from his bloodline and diligence, but equally from Shuang Tu's comprehensive support. This included daily nutritional feasts, magic cuisine. Not only were they delicious, but they were perfectly suited to his current needs, providing him with abundant nourishment.

Just as Lan Zao was planning to continue training after lunch, Shuang Tu continued, “After lunch, you should rest for a while. Don't train at all this afternoon. You need to conserve your energy.”

"I'll introduce you to someone. He'll take you down to an underground cavern to participate in a wild duel."

Chapter 654: Wild Duel (1)

“What is a wild duel?” Lan Zao asked curiously, having only heard of heroic duels.

Shuang Tu explained, "You can think of it as a heroic duel without restrictions. It's more thrilling and bloodier than a heroic duel. Both duelists may employ any means during combat and cannot surrender voluntarily. The duel only ends when one combatant is utterly incapacitated. Whether the loser survives depends entirely on whether the victor chooses to spare them."

"Also, while heroic duels typically involve sentient beings, wild duels are different. Your opponent could very well be a beast like a tiger or wolf, even dragons or demons might appear."

Lan Zao frowned. " This sounds incredibly dangerous."

Shuang Tu nodded solemnly: "This danger can be controlled. You must understand: Hen Fa, you need to engage in such combat. It will help you familiarize yourself with your body and truly hone your battle skill."

“Solitary training in isolation is highly inadvisable.”

"Combat prowess untested in practice is unreliable. You should grasp this principle."

Lan Zao nodded silently.

Shuang Tu continued: “During your upcoming infiltration mission, you'll likely encounter formidable foes, even silver level adversaries like Bing Xiao or Spring Boxer.”

"When that happens, you'll likely have to rely solely on yourself. Therefore, you must ensure you possess a certain level of combat capability."

“I've already registered for you.”

“You're currently iron level, but as a newcomer, your first battle will be against a bronze level beast.”

“The risk is manageable.”

“One more thing: members of the Bayonet Gang will also participate in this wild duel. Rejoining the Bayonet Gang might allow you to approach them and secure their renewed recruitment.”

Lan Zao understood and grew increasingly eager.

Truthfully, he was also curious about his own combat performance.

The two teleported back together. Lan Zao ate, while Shuang Tu went to the secret chamber to handle matters at hand.

"Hen Fa has been properly arranged. Over the next period, he'll participate in several wild duels. His opponents will be pre-selected and arranged by us, enough to hone his combat skills while ensuring his safety. The key is for him to feel genuine threat in battle, pushing him to train harder and use the alchemy gear we've equipped him with more frequently. Take the projectile core, for instance. He'll sustain injuries in combat, but we'll provide follow-up treatment. All these measures will gradually make him dependent on us, both physically and mentally. This is a crucial step in turning him over to our side."

"Master Cai Jing has recruited over thirty potion apprentices, nearly twenty of whom are our operatives. It is now confirmed: the underground space he secretly excavated is precisely where the legacy of the legendary druid lies. This requires close attention."

"Although Ba Qi has received treatment, his condition remains critical. Based on timing, the Heroism Pirates have likely joined forces with Lady Sea Snake and others. The capture of the gold level sea serpent carries minimal risk of failure. We should see the outcome of this operation shortly."

"The temporary base for Bing Xiao and his group has been secured. They are actively arming themselves, having acquired a batch of Ice Spike Bayonets from the black market. Their overall combat effectiveness has improved, though the increase is not significant. All movements remain under our surveillance."

Shuang Tu felt content while handling affairs.

With Ba Qi's assistance, his primary mission had achieved breakthrough progress. Additionally, both Hen Fa and the intelligence gathering on Tranquil Hill were advancing smoothly.

By afternoon, Lan Zao was led underground by a snow elf.

This subterranean chamber spanned at least 500 square meters by rough estimate.

The ceiling, surrounding walls, and floor were all reddish-brown.

“You can familiarize yourself with this space beforehand. This will be your next battlefield,” the guiding snow elf advised.

The underground chamber was nearly empty, save for five or six stone pillars.

These pillars were thick and sturdy, most of which reached up to the ceiling, while a few had been severed.

Lan Zao's gaze lingered briefly on the pillars before sweeping around the room.

He noticed traces of doors lining the perimeter. These doors varied in size, many barred with iron gratings through which dim, shadowy scenes could be glimpsed.

Unlike the arena, there were no spectator seats here.

But magic runes covered every surface, the walls, the floor, and the ceiling.

“It seems that the audience for this wild duel watches through magic projections,” Lan Zao guessed the truth.

Wild duels were filled with bloodshed and violence, and betting rings opened every time they were held. This activity had long been banned by the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and was illegal.

Flower Skirt Island lay in the sea near Ice Sculpture Island, a neutral territory. Islands like this almost invariably hosted wild duel arenas.

Whether driven by the thrill or profit of gambling, a worship of violence, or a craving for bloodshed, these arenas drew countless spectators each year.

Others attended for different motives, like revenge, or to test vicious weapons.

Most spectators hailed from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, including influential figures and dignitaries. Given the event's negative implications, these individuals naturally avoid public exposure.

“May I freely attack and test the strength of the ground here?” Lan Zao inquired.

“Certainly. These surfaces can be repaired,” replied the snow elf.

Lan Zao pressed further: “Will my testing within the arena be leaked?”

“Absolutely not. This is our little private operation,” the snow elf replied, winking at Lan Zao.

“Seems this one is likely Shuang Tu's mole infiltrated into the wild duel,” Lan Zao surmised inwardly.

His suspicion was well-founded.

The clandestine faction represented by Shuang Tu wielded considerable power. Moreover, the master of the wild duel arena on Flower Skirt Island was merely a silver level fighter, penetrating his defenses posed little difficulty.

Lan Zao surveyed the battlefield.

It was undeniably monotonous.

“Stone pillars are the only viable cover.”

Lan Zao personally measured the distance between pillars, calculating the exact time it would take him to dash from one pillar to another.

He proceeded to attack the pillars.

“Very sturdy. Reliable cover.” The test results gave Lan Zao a quiet sense of satisfaction.

He then launched attacks at the floor tiles, ceiling, and surrounding walls.

“The defense is astonishing!” Lan Zao unleashed his full power, employing his strongest skill, Air Cannon, yet couldn't budge it an inch. At most, it caused the hidden magic inscriptions to faintly flicker with a ripple-like glow.

Lan Zao pondered for a moment, then began firing air cannons in rapid succession.

He gradually shifted the impact points closer to himself. This test primarily served Lan Zao's desire to experience the residual shockwave power of the air cannon.

The air cannon could detonate.

In actual combat, the most common scenario wasn't a direct hit on the target, but the explosion's shockwave catching the target off guard.

Moreover, the blast radius varied depending on the battlefield environment.

Before Lan Zao could complete the test, the snow elf pushed open the door, a hint of urgency in his expression: “Time's up.”

Lan Zao felt a pang of regret, yet he was satisfied.

He understood being able to test the battlefield in advance was already cheating.

The snow elf led Lan Zao into a secret chamber to rest.

Here, he cultivated the Air Pressure battle qi, actively restoring his battle qi.

As time passed, he could faintly hear the clamor growing louder outside the chamber door.

The noise became increasingly chaotic, clearly signaling the arrival of more spectators.

By the time Lan Zao had fully restored his battle qi, another half hour had elapsed. The chamber door swung open.

“Follow me.” It was the same Snow Elf again. He led Lan Zao out of the chamber, through a long corridor, and pushed open a large door.

“This is the waiting room. When your name is called to enter the arena, another door will open.”

“Remember, under no circumstances are you to engage in combat with others within the waiting room. Violation will result in immediate execution by the wild duel authorities!” After issuing these instructions, the snow elf parted ways with Lan Zao.

The waiting room was crowded.

A towering, tiger-headed beastman leaned against the entrance gate.

An elderly man with a sullen expression, radiating bronze aura, sat on a bench leaning on a staff that resembled a walking stick.

Two long wooden benches ran along the walls of the waiting room.

An ordinary middle-aged man sat on the same bench as the old man, but they were seated far apart, nearly at opposite ends.

In the middle of the other bench sat a silver level fighter.

Lan Zao recognized him immediately, it was Spring Boxer from the Bayonet Gang.

Spring Boxer couldn't help but show surprise when he saw Lan Zao enter.

He waved proactively, “Never thought I'd run into someone I know.”

Lan Zao knew the Bayonet Gang would send someone, so seeing Spring Boxer didn't surprise him. Lacking any flair for the dramatic, he maintained his usual detached expression, gave Spring Boxer a nod, and walked past him.

Finally, he sat down beside Spring Boxer.

“You advanced to iron level?” Spring Boxer asked, a mix of surprise and curiosity in his voice, “Why come to participate in a wild duel?”

He knew Lan Zao's bloodline had awakened, a pure 100% Fish Bladder bloodline. This meant Lan Zao could steadily advance to the silver level.

In Spring Boxer's view, Lan Zao should focus on cultivating himself and striving to raise his fighter level, rather than rushing headlong into dangerous combat.

Lan Zao replied: "I bought a battle skill and owed some money, so I have to participate in this wild duel."

This was the response Shuang Tu had prepared for Lan Zao.

Spring Boxer nodded.

Lan Zao asked in return, “What about you, Lord Spring Boxer?”

Spring Boxer offered a wry smile. "I'm after specific resources, things hard to come by on Flower Skirt Island. Truthfully, my reasons aren't so different from yours."

“To get us to Tranquil Hill as quickly as possible,” Spring Boxer added silently to himself.

The weight of his responsibility settled on his shoulders, and his conversational mood vanished instantly.

Lan Zao said nothing more.

After all, Hen Fa's persona was one of taciturnity.

The waiting room fell silent once more.

Chapter 655: Wild Duel (2)

A few minutes later, a sudden burst of booming instrumental music filled the air.

Then, the snow elf presiding over the wild duel bounded energetically to the very center of the arena. With a voice brimming with enthusiasm, he proclaimed, “Honored lords and ladies, welcome to witness this wild duel!”

“Enough with the formalities, let us commence the first duel immediately.”

“A bronze thief consumed by greed versus an ordinary man whose wife and children were dishonored and slain.”

“Heh, today's appetizer promises to be quite interesting.”

“An ordinary man versus a transcendent!”

“Let us see, will the wronged commoner find justice, or will transcendent power prevail above all?”

“The next 30 seconds are your betting window!”

The moment the snow elf finished speaking, frantic bell chimes rang out.

After thirty seconds of ringing, the doors facing the arena in the waiting room suddenly slid open to the side.

The ordinary middle-aged man practically lunged out.

Wielding a bloodstained axe, he charged into the arena.

His enemy and opponent, the bronze level thief, limped out from the opposite doors, dragging a leg that bled incessantly.

Clearly, to enhance the wild duel's entertainment value and ‘fairness’, the arena owner had provided a transcendent in a compromised state.

The ordinary middle-aged man roared and charged forward, axe raised.

The bronze thief hopped on one leg, attempting to create distance.

“Damn it, he's faking it!” Spring Boxer suddenly whispered.

The door to the waiting room featured an iron grille. Though the view was narrow, it perfectly framed the middle-aged man's back and the thief who was “escaping.”

Lan Zao's gaze pierced through the iron bars, drawn to the fight.

He couldn't help but feel tense for the middle-aged man.

The middle-aged man lunged at the bronze thief. At the critical moment, the thief flashed a triumphant, sinister grin, channeling his battle qi to make his movements suddenly agile.

The thief deftly dodged the axe swing, then aimed his dagger at the middle-aged man's back and thrust it viciously downward.

Completely unaware of the dagger thrust toward him, the middle-aged man roared in fury. His hand shot out, grabbing the thief's wounded leg and digging his fingers deep into the festering, bleeding wound.

In the next instant, both men roared in agony.

The thief's thrust faltered from the excruciating pain. Though the dagger pierced the man's back, it failed to deliver a fatal blow.

The man's back arched, the dagger lodged deep as he tore at the thief's wound, wrestling him to the ground.

He mounted the thief, raised his iron axe high with his right hand, and brought it down with a savage swing.

In the next instant, the iron axe cleaved the thief's skull clean in two, leaving only the mouth intact.

The thief's horrified expression froze on his face, nearly severed in two.

Yet the middle-aged man's vengeance had only just begun.

He raised the axe again and again, bringing it down relentlessly.

The bronze thief's skull shattered completely beneath the blows, followed by his chest, abdomen, entrails, and thighs.

With each furious swing, the wound on the man's back gushed fresh blood.

Yet the man seemed oblivious, his eyes bloodshot, pouring every ounce of strength into each blow.

Finally, drained of blood and strength, he too collapsed unconscious.

But that didn't stop him from being the ultimate victor.

“Nice work!” Spring Boxer grinned.

Lan Zao glanced at him and suddenly realized: though Spring Boxer was a gang member, a criminal, he wasn't the morally bankrupt type.

The battle between the extraordinary and the ordinary had ended with the ordinary man's victory.

This outcome held no surprises.

Many factors determined the outcome of a fight. Bronze level battle qi could only be amplified internally; external protection was something only iron level fighters could achieve.

In truth, even a fully-fledged bronze fighter could be killed by an ordinary person, let alone this bronze thief, who was already gravely wounded.

Only after the fight ended did Lan Zao notice: the terrain of the dueling arena had changed. It was no longer the red stone pillar terrain he had privately tested before, but a vast, flat plain dotted with sparse grass.

The unconscious middle-aged man was dragged away.

Black mist began to spread, eventually enveloping the entire arena in a pitch-black night.

The night quickly dissipated.

The arena transformed once more, becoming a marshland. Solid stone slabs dotted the deep purple swamp mud.

The snow elf host took the stage once more, standing atop the largest stone slab, his voice booming.

"Esteemed spectators, I trust the appetizer served earlier was to your liking? It is my honor to present the second event in this wild duel."

“A bronze level elderly magician, peddling counterfeit alchemy ingredients for illicit profit.”

"A young noble heir was thoroughly swindled by him. Our victim didn't choose to report it, that would be too boring."

“Instead, he mobilized his family's influence, captured the old bronze magician, and brought him here under guard.”

"Here are his exact words to the old bronze magician: 'Since you can craft pain tentacles so convincing they fool the senses; you must know them intimately.' I'll give you one chance. If you can traverse the swamp concealing the pain tentacles and retrieve the token from the treasure chest, you succeed. I'll spare your life!'"

“Ha! This one is even more entertaining than the last. What do you say, ladies and gentlemen?”

"The 30-second countdown has begun. Time to choose. Will the old magician overcome the pain tentacles and save himself, or will the powerful noble succeed in his revenge, delivering the most brutal lesson to the old magician?"

The moment the host finished speaking, a bell began to toll.

After ringing thirty times, the host shouted, "Now, please witness the bronze magician's journey of redemption!”

The entrance opened again.

Lan Zao saw the old man seated on the bench begin to move.

Taking a deep breath, he leaned on his slender staff, as thin as a walking stick, and stepped slowly and heavily onto the duel platform.

The old magician stood at the edge of the platform as the gate behind him swiftly closed once more.

His face solemn, he did not rashly step into the swamp. Instead, after chanting, he cast a spell, launching a fireball straight ahead.

The fireball struck the marshland and exploded.

Black muddy water splattered everywhere, a putrid stench surged outward, and several suspicious skeletal remains were also blown out.

This impressed Lan Zao, who marveled at how the arena could create such a realistic combat environment.

The old magician, however, lacked Lan Zao's composure. After temporarily confirming that the area ahead was safe, he cautiously moved forward.

The target treasure chest should be hidden opposite him, within a grove of broadleaf trees.

Beyond that, the terrain was open and exposed.

The old magician gradually approached the grove, but his use of fireballs to probe the area decreased sharply.

His personal magic power reserves were finite.

The old magician paused to meditate.

But in the next instant, the swampy ground near him suddenly bulged repeatedly, and one after another, purple-skinned swamp octopusmen emerged.

They howled and charged at the old magician.

The bronze magician shuddered in terror, abandoning all thought of combat as he sprinted forward with all his might.

After only a few steps, he sank into the mire.

Yet the next instant, the magician rose, floating above the swamp.

It had to be said, magicians possessed far more diverse methods than fighters; even at the bronze level, they possessed the ability to fly.

However, the old magician's flight range was clearly insufficient. Soon, he landed on a patch of rocky ground.

The pursuing octopusmen were closing in.

The old magician had no choice but to turn around, pulling a spell scroll from his robe.

A barrage of ice spears shot forth, slaying over a dozen octopusmen.

None of these octopusmen possessed extraordinary abilities, but their sheer numbers were formidable. The loss of a quarter of their numbers only fueled their fury.

The old magician hurled another vial.

The potion exploded, forming a shimmering cloud of crystalline white dust.

The octopusmen charged into the cloud, only to collapse sprawled on the ground moments later. Half their remaining numbers perished.

The remaining octopusmen chose to bypass the white dust cloud.

The old magician gained precious time and set off once more, flying toward the final grove of trees.

Within the grove, he spotted the treasure chest.

But the moment he opened it, countless pain tentacles burst through the ground, lashing at him from all directions.

The protective magic shield he maintained shattered instantly.

Ultimately, he was completely submerged by the frenzied lashing of the pain tentacles.

By the time the octopusmen rushed over and subdued the pain tentacles, the old magician's entire skeleton had been crushed beyond recognition, let alone his flesh and blood.

The manner of death was truly gruesome.

Witnessing this outcome, Lan Zao gained a deeper understanding of the wild duel.

Just as Shuang Tu had described, the wild duel was saturated with violence and bloodshed. If the opponent wasn't human, it was undoubtedly worse. Intelligent beings possessed emotions like mercy, compassion, arrogance, and might even spare a defeated opponent's life. But creatures like these pain tentacles didn't stop at killing the loser, they wouldn't rest until they ground the defeated into dust.

Black mist rose again, enveloping the entire arena.

When it dissipated, the battlefield had transformed into red stone pillars.

Seeing the familiar scene, Lan Zao's heart skipped a beat, knowing it was his turn next.

Sure enough, the snow elf host reappeared: “The third wild duel features a fortunate soul who awakened their bloodline, recently ascending to the iron level.”

“Damn it, why doesn't something this good ever happen to me!”

“Why is this guy volunteering for a wild duel? Is he desperate, or has his power gone to his head, craving more thrills?”

“Let's see who his opponent is!”

“Oh crap!”

“He's screwed. His opponent is an iron level snow eagle!”

“I love this. I can't wait to see his body torn to shreds by that snow eagle's talons.”

“Place your bets now! Our rookie's got nothing but his bare hands against a flying beast.”

“Will you trust this lucky guy, or do you want to see him get torn apart?”

The 30-second countdown clock chimed.

Chapter 656: Wild Duel (3)

The ticking of the countdown clock echoed relentlessly, as if striking directly at Lan Zao's heart.

“Shuang Tu explicitly told me it was a bronze level beast. How could it have become iron level?”

“Bronze level and iron level are entirely different matters. How could such a grave error occur?”

"No, this duel arena isn't under Shuang Tu's control. At most, it's been infiltrated a little. Could a mistake have happened? Of course it's possible..."

Spring Boxer's voice snapped Lan Zao out of his chaotic thoughts.

“Hen Fa, you're not just facing a beast of your own level—it can fly!”

“Did you bring ranged weapons?”

Due to their different life forms, beasts inherently possess far tougher hides than humans, granting them a massive advantage.

Lan Zao was iron level, still one level below silver and two below gold. Yet even among gold level fighters, few mastered aerial battle skills.

Spring Boxer looked at Lan Zao with concern. It was obvious that Lan Zao was at a massive disadvantage against the iron level snow eagle!

Lan Zao shook his head, indicating he had no ranged weapons.

This made Spring Boxer's expression darken further. After a moment of silence, he said, “Perhaps, you should just concede defeat. It's not shameful...”

Surrender?

Just moments ago, that very thought had flashed through Lan Zao's mind.

But upon hearing Spring Boxer's suggestion, he shook his head decisively: “This is the wild duel arena. Early surrender isn't permitted. If I surrender now, I fear the arena owner would grind my bones to dust. Compared to that fate, facing an iron level snow eagle is preferable.”

Spring Boxer's face darkened, yet he couldn't rebuke Lan Zao's logic.

“Thanks.” Lan Zao nodded to him, “I still have a chance to win.”

With that, Lan Zao stepped out of the waiting room at a measured pace, passed through the open doors, and formally entered the wild duel arena.

Spring Boxer's concern had earned him a bit more goodwill from Lan Zao.

His conversation with Spring Boxer had also cleared away Lan Zao's earlier panic, allowing him to settle into calmness.

Lan Zao's figure appeared simultaneously across the magic projections in each spectator room.

In the room where Shuang Tu watched, he studied Lan Zao's expression with keen interest.

This arrangement was unexpected for Lan Zao.

But it was precisely within Shuang Tu's plan.

The truth was that Shuang Tu had deceived Lan Zao, misleading him about the difficulty and easily persuading him to join the wild duel.

This was a test for Lan Zao.

It was also part of his cultivation.

After all, undercover operatives often had to face unexpected incidents.

“Now let me see how you handle this sudden unexpected situation.”

Shuang Tu's initial observation of Lan Zao was, for the most part, satisfactory.

In the observation room where Bing Xiao resided.

“Hen Fa is here too?” Lan Zao's appearance surprised the veteran old leader of the Bayonet Gang.

The human ringleader behind him immediately frowned: “Hen Fa is in trouble.”

“He's not equipped with any crossbows or firearms. Aside from a set of bronze level leather armor and a bronze level scimitar, he has no other gear.”

"Against a land beast, he might stand a chance. But it's a snow eagle!"

Bing Xiao frowned slightly: "His bloodline has awakened. Why abandon proper cultivation to enter such a perilous wild duel? Is there any way to pull him out?"

The human ringleader sighed: “Difficult! Rescuing him will inevitably disrupt this wild duel. Our Bayonet Gang is new here, while the wild duel arena owner has long been the local kingpin. Still, there are precedents for such actions."

"But doing so would mean that the wild duel arena would be destroying its own reputation. The price we'd pay would be enormous. By my estimate, it would cost at least this much."

The human ringleader held up four fingers.

Bing Xiao glanced at him and shook his head slightly, “Then forget it.”

Even if Lan Zao could advance to the silver level in the future, the cost would leave the Bayonet Gang severely in the red if they went through with it.

His choice didn't surprise the human ringleader.

The human ringleader added, "But the loser isn't necessarily Hen Fa. The primary revenue of the wild duel arena doesn't come from ticket sales, but from the betting pools they set up."

“As the major bookmaker, the arena owner rakes in a fortune nearly every time!”

“To fuel gambling, they often design wild duels to feature evenly matched opponents, making the outcome unpredictable.”

"And cases like Hen Fa's now, where one side is clearly at a disadvantage, might be a deliberately set trap. When the crowd heavily bets on the snow eagle to win, if Hen Fa triumphs, it's a massive payday for the bookmakers."

Bing Xiao sighed, “Perhaps.”

They fell silent as the third duel commenced.

As Lan Zao stepped toward the center of the arena, the snow eagle, already poised for attack, let out a piercing shriek and lunged forward.

“So fast!” Even fully focused, Lan Zao was startled by the snow eagle's speed.

He rolled awkwardly to dodge, using his arms to block the eagle's claws.

Fortunately, his bronze level armor prevented any scratches.

The snow eagle, its strike missed, immediately soared skyward.

Lan Zao instantly dove toward a nearby stone pillar.

The snow eagle turned mid-air, charging toward Lan Zao once more.

Lan Zao circled the pillar, using it as cover to evade the snow eagle's attacks.

After all, the snow eagle was a magic beast with limited intelligence, repeatedly missing its strikes.

Instead, Lan Zao seized the moment the snow eagle dove down, swinging his bronze level curved blade to land heavy blows on its body and wings.

Though Lan Zao's blade skills weren't exceptional, they were rock-solid from countless real battles.

The snow eagle sustained minor injuries.

“If only I had an iron level curved blade!” Lan Zao sighed inwardly.

His fighter level had risen, but his equipment hadn't been upgraded yet.

Having suffered repeated setbacks, the snow eagle abandoned its diving attacks. Instead, it employed a racial magic, firing feathers from its own plumage as projectiles.

Lan Zao used the stone pillars as cover, temporarily stabilizing his position.

The snow eagle circled overhead, constantly adjusting its position to fire long-range shots at Lan Zao.

Battle skill - Air Cannon.

Seizing a moment, Lan Zao suddenly unleashed a ranged attack.

Caught off guard, the snow eagle was struck by the blast.

This counterattack immediately drew low gasps from many in the viewing rooms.

“He actually possesses long-range attack capabilities now. That must be the battle skill: Air Cannon.” Bing Xiao felt a flicker of surprise, but quickly shook his head, “Too bad Hen Fa's practice wasn't thorough enough. If he'd fired two shots in succession, the snow eagle would've been severely injured. Hitting just one doesn't amount to much.”

The human ringleader nodded, casually remarking, “It seems his battle qi technique has changed too.”

Struck by the air cannon, the snow eagle grew considerably more cautious, no longer as reckless as before.

It relentlessly fired feathers, while Lan Zao countered through the stone pillars with Air Cannon.

Lan Zao gradually gained the upper hand.

The snow eagle's feathers were blocked by the stone pillars, while the air cannon shells exploded even if they missed their mark, dealing area damage. The snow eagle struggled to evade this damage entirely through flight.

As the damage accumulated, the snow eagle sustained more injuries and its flight speed slowed considerably.

“At this rate, I can win!” Lan Zao was secretly rejoicing when the stone pillars before him began to slowly descend.

“Damn it!” Lan Zao cursed, instantly recalling the previous wild duel.

The bronze magician had attempted to conserve his magic power through meditation, only for a horde of swamp octopusmen to emerge and pursue him relentlessly.

Clearly, tedious, uneventful duels were precisely what the wild duel sought to avoid.

Such matches severely diminished the audience's experience.

Therefore, they directly altered the battlefield to make the combat more intense and spectacular.

All the stone pillars began to sink slowly, stripping Lan Zao of his home-field advantage.

Lan Zao had no choice but to fight desperately. Wielding his curved blade, he charged forward, closing the distance between himself and the snow eagle.

After exchanging several long-range attacks with him, the snow eagle, driven by rage, lunged at Lan Zao once more.

Without cover, Lan Zao could only dodge the snow eagle's claws by rolling.

As it closed in, he relentlessly swung his bronze scimitar or unleashed air cannons.

After a series of perilous exchanges, the snow eagle crashed to the ground, one wing severely bent, its flight capability lost.

Lan Zao was also badly wounded. The snow eagle nearly gouged out his left eye, leaving a gruesome claw mark across his cheek, blood streaming down.

Finally, Lan Zao severed the snow eagle's head with his serrated curved blade, securing ultimate victory.

No cheers erupted.

Only his heavy, exhausted panting filled the air.

Soon, he was led away.

The wild duel arena was once again shrouded in black mist.

“I wonder who Spring Boxer fighter's opponent is?” Lan Zao's question went unanswered.

But his performance satisfied Shuang Tu: “Not bad.”

In another viewing chamber.

The human ringleader remarked, “Hen Fa is truly impressive. We should recruit him—it would strengthen the Bayonet Gang.”

Bing Xiao nodded in agreement, “Wait for a reply. If the situation looks favorable, you can attempt recruitment.”

“A reply?”

Bing Xiao offered no further explanation, “Judging by the timeline, the letter should arrive soon.”

Blood Kin City.

Bayonet Gang's base.

Within a secret chamber, two gold level fighters laughed heartily as they drank fine wine and engaged in confidential discussions.

One was the Axe Gang leader; the other was a gold level fighter named Lin.

Fu Zhao pushed open the door and approached the leader, handing over several sheets of paper, “Father, these are the results of our investigation into Hen Fa.”

The Axe Gang leader took them and scanned them quickly, “Draft a letter. Send all this intelligence to Bing Xiao.”

Lin paused mid-sip, “That Bing Xiao who served under Teng Dong Lang?”

“Precisely,” the Axe Gang leader affirmed, “He escaped Snow Bird Harbor with his men. Crucially, he retrieved Teng Dong Lang's body and brought out the designated coffin bearer.”

A grave expression crossed Lin's face. "Time is running out. They must depart immediately. Otherwise, even if they reach Tranquil Hill, it will be too late."

The Axe Gang leader grinned. "Forget about him! Bing Xiao visited Tranquil Hill before, but his bloodline wasn't pure enough back then, he got rejected. He knows his limits. Besides, with the Lord Guide there, why worry about them not making it back?"

“Our top priority now is eliminating the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps currently in Blood Kin City.”

“With the two of us teaming up, they're as good as dead!”

Chapter 657: The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is Bringing About Their Own Destruction

The gold level fighter Lin was the powerful reinforcement specially invited by the Axe Gang leader for revenge.

The Axe Gang leader expressed confidence in this battle, while Lin remained impassive: “Long Fu managed to kill Teng Dong Lang with the final strike. I suspect there's something fishy about it.”

"I've also reviewed the magic footage from the Cold Wind Valley ambush. His performance showed clear improvement compared to the Snow Bird Harbor arena, but if that's all he's capable of, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps truly is doomed."

Like the Axe Gang leader, Lin was also a veteran of military service, having experienced numerous battles and possessing extensive combat expertise.

His assessment was grounded in solid experience.

As for the magic footage of the Cold Wind Valley ambush, it was naturally provided by the Axe Gang leader.

This was a sound practice of the regular army, documenting battle scenes at all times to facilitate post-battle analysis, learning lessons, and gathering intelligence. Even if one fell in battle, precious intelligence could still reach comrades, aiding future engagements.

“1000%”

The Axe Gang leader couldn't help but recall the battle in Cold Wind Valley, sighing, “That Long Fu was truly extraordinary. He was clearly only silver, yet his actual combat prowess rivaled that of a gold level fighter. What a pity. If Jia Bing had accepted my invitation too, with his ice gargoyles supporting us, our victory rate would have been 1000%!”

Lin looked over in surprise, “You invited that guy too?”

The Axe Gang leader replied, "Unfortunately, he refused after hearing I'd already invited you. He said he had crucial alchemy experiments to conduct and couldn't leave the workshop for more than six hours."

Lin smiled faintly: “That's just his nature.”

“But you, buddy, haven't changed a bit, still obsessively chasing unrealistically high win rates!”

The Axe Gang leader instantly wore a look of displeasure: “How is that a bad thing?”

"War is inherently dangerous; any battle carries risks. Unexpected events can occur in any fight."

“So many factors influence battle outcomes. A single drastic shift can alter the entire result.”

"Pursuing victory odds is taking responsibility for oneself, ensuring success—it's never excessive. Doesn't every outstanding commander strive to maximize their forces' chances of victory with every ounce of ability?"

Lin shook his head slightly, offering a different perspective: "That sounds reasonable, but it's actually incorrect. Resources are always finite, which is why merchants discuss cost-effectiveness. As commanders, the combat resources we can deploy are also limited. Therefore, taking risks is worthwhile. Because often, with only limited combat resources, we have no choice but to take risks. Excessive pursuit of victory rates is usually predicated on already possessing a significant advantage. Like now! But in most real combat scenarios, such circumstances are rare."

Pausing briefly, Lin continued, “Our opponents aren't formidable. The highest level among them are merely two silver level individuals. Though one is somewhat unique, he still falls short of gold level.”

“With two gold level members on our side, we already hold an immense advantage. There's no need to invite a third.”

“We remain undetected and retain the element of surprise.”

"Long Fu might pose trouble for any one of us, but that silver level fire magician, Yao Ma, is mediocre in combat. Whether it's me or you, if we fight at full strength, she won't last three minutes."

“Once she's dead, the two of us can surround and attack Long Fu. Failure is impossible.”

Lin's analysis was logical and well-reasoned, delivered in a calm tone with a composed expression that radiated strong confidence.

The Axe Gang leader shook his head slightly, disagreeing with Lin's military strategy. Yet he nodded in acknowledgment, refraining from challenging Lin's subsequent reasoning—for it aligned perfectly with his own battle projections.

Just then, Fu Zhao burst in urgently.

"Bad news! Father, Lord Lin! The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps suddenly departed Blood Kin City via the teleportation formation!"

Fu Zhao delivered the devastating report.

"What?!" Lin's brow instantly furrowed.

The Axe Gang leader sprang to his feet, fixing his son with a stern glare. "How could this happen? Didn't I order you to station men at the teleportation formation to prevent precisely this?"

Fu Zhao bowed his head: "Yes, that's the situation. I've already planted our people inside. But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is extremely cunning. They secretly recruited people to queue up and, within a short time, bribed a few others with large sums to cut in line collectively. Our people found it difficult to prevent this from happening."

Teleportation formations existed in all major towns of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The cost of teleportation was exorbitant, and the service was extremely busy. Without solid connections, one had to queue to wait for transport.

In Snow Bird Harbor, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps enjoyed excellent relations with the city lord, allowing them to cut in line and teleport directly at any time. But in Blood Kin City, they didn't receive such preferential treatment.

The Axe Gang leader schemed in secret, determined to wipe out Zong Ge and Zi Di's group. He spared no expense, earnestly inviting Lin and Jia Bing to assist him. Though only one accepted, the Axe Gang leader patiently awaited Lin's secret rendezvous.

But just as the operation was about to commence, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps slipped away through the teleportation formation!

The Axe Gang leader frowned deeply. This was precisely the scenario he feared, and yet it had unfolded.

Lin sighed, "Seems there's a capable person among them after all. To secretly hire people to queue up first, then suddenly bribe the remaining few with heavy sums to complete the group teleportation, it looks simple but executing it requires formidable discipline. The slightest mishap could leak the secret and cause failure."

“This is getting troublesome.”

Lin's expression also grew grave.

As the leader of Ice Spear City's gang, his meeting with the Axe Gang leader had been arranged in secret.

The secrecy served two purposes: first, to maintain the element of surprise and prevent enemy detection through information leaks. The other reason was his status as a gold level individual.

At this level, a transcendent's personal strength could rival that of a small army, easily threatening the security of an entire town. Consequently, such individuals were subject to constant surveillance and investigation from all sides.

Any rash action by a gold level individual would trigger heightened vigilance and scrutiny from all sides.

It was like a sharp dagger, normally kept safely sheathed in its place. Suddenly drawn from its sheath, no matter where it was placed, it would draw intense attention.

Thus, Lin had to carefully consider the potential repercussions of every move.

Especially since he also managed a gang faction. As its leader and strongest combatant, his sudden departure would have significant negative repercussions for his organization.

Under these circumstances, concealing his identity and masking his movements was the most effective way to minimize such negative impacts.

This also implied another crucial point - Lin could not openly use the town's teleportation formation.

The reason was straightforward: such large-scale teleportation formations were subject to the strictest oversight. Teleportation fees increased proportionally with the user's power level.

Thus, even if Lin concealed his aura and disguised himself as a low level transcendent, the sheer cost incurred by the formation during teleportation would instantly reveal his true power level.

Subsequent investigations would then easily uncover his identity. After all, he had long since reached the gold level.

The same principle applied to the Axe Gang leader.

It was precisely for this reason that the Axe Gang leader had plotted for so long, waiting for Lin's secret rendezvous - in fact, Lin had come on foot.
