Zong Ge and Zi Di could teleport away, but Lin and the Axe Gang leader couldn't use that method.

This wasn't because they were confined to one location.

Rather, it would cause a massive commotion and attract widespread attention.

If, after their teleportation, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps fell victim to an ambush and perished, then everyone would know they were responsible!

If the Axe Gang leader were willing to risk exposure to eliminate Zong Ge and Zi Di, he would have done so long ago. Why endure the agony of waiting patiently for Lin's arrival?

“So, Long Fu has already discovered us? Was his earlier cooperation agreement with the Axe Gang merely a delaying tactic?” Fu Zhao's expression darkened.

The Axe Gang leader shook his head slowly. "Probably not. Our first collaboration succeeded yesterday. We successfully escorted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' first caravan into Blood Kin City."

“They genuinely intend to cooperate with us.”

“It's just that the battle in Cold Wind Valley made them wary. Unable to verify our true identities, they resorted to this minor trick.”

“If they suspected I was the real culprit, they wouldn't simply leave. They'd use their connections to target us.”

"You must understand, the acting Blood Kin City Lord has deep-seated conflicts with our gang. Even without working with our enemies, Long Fu has deep ties with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Li Jian Clan. He would certainly use them to strike against us."

"But now, they've just teleported away suddenly. They probably just wanted to act swiftly to shake off any potential lurking enemies."

Lin agreed with the Axe Gang leader's analysis.

“In that case,” he said, "we'll just have to wait for another opportunity. We need to know their next destination to set up a new ambush."

The Axe Gang leader gritted his teeth. Though reluctant, he nodded. "We already have men stationed at the teleportation formation. Haven't we uncovered their target location yet?"

Fu Zhao hurriedly replied, "Father, the moment we received this intelligence, I ordered an investigation. It should be here soon..."

He paused abruptly mid-sentence.

An underling had arrived to report fresh intelligence.

Upon receiving the new information, Fu Zhao's face lit up with evident delight: "Good news! After leaving two men behind at the shop, the entire group teleported to Snow Rabbit City."

“Our men succeeded in causing a teleportation error! Long Fu is now outside Snow Rabbit City, a considerable distance from the town!”

The Axe Gang leader froze for a moment, then clapped his hands sharply and roared with laughter: "Jia Bing is right there! All three of us gold level experts will work together, our win rate is 1000%. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has walked straight into their own doom this time!"

Chapter 658: Snow Rabbit City, Rabbit Fur Town

“Who did we plant at the teleportation formation?” The Axe Gang leader laughed heartily before immediately asking.

Fu Zhao reported a name.

The Axe Gang leader nodded promptly and instructed, “Reward him generously.”

“Understood,” Fu Zhao replied, then asked with concern, "Father, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has already teleported away. To pursue them, we must rely on teleportation to close the distance. But if we do that..."

Fu Zhao's worry was well-founded.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' teleportation departure carried little consequence, as their strongest member was merely silver level.

But if the Axe Gang leader and Lin, both gold levels, were to teleport, the repercussions would be exponentially greater.

Given the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' immense prestige within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, any mishap involving them would inevitably trigger a widespread investigation. At that point, the Axe Gang leader and the one known as Lin, who had been acting suspiciously, would be the prime suspects.

If the kingdom launched an investigation, it would be extremely detrimental to the Axe Gang, potentially leading to its annihilation.

The Axe Gang leader smiled faintly. "Rest assured, we won't use the official teleportation formation. In fact, we possess a secret teleportation formation."

“What?” Fu Zhao's mouth fell open, his face etched with utter disbelief.

As the acknowledged successor to the Axe Gang, he routinely managed numerous gang affairs and allocated resources. These experiences had broadened his horizons. Fu Zhao understood very well: constructing a teleportation formation consumed enormous funds. Even the Axe Gang struggled to afford it. Laying a secret formation would require even greater funds than standard construction. Beyond that, it carried immense risk.

Secretly building teleportation formations was prohibited by every government.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom was no exception, relentlessly cracking down on private teleportation formations.

The Axe Gang leader turned his gaze to Lin: “Rest assured, after I die, my son will be the one to carry my coffin!”

This was his deliberate explanation to Lin.

The stakes of a secret teleportation formation were simply too high.

Lin finally nodded slightly: “In that case, sharing some secrets with him is acceptable.”

The Axe Gang leader turned back to Fu Zhao: "Beyond that, I cannot reveal more secrets. For now, you only need to know we possess a secret teleportation formation. Through it, reaching Snow Rabbit City is effortless."

Fu Zhao's mouth fell slightly open. In that instant, he suddenly realized the relationship between his father and Lin was far more than mere former comrades-in-arms. It seemed these powerful veterans, retired from the military, had formed a secret alliance.

Lin frowned slightly, looking at the Axe Gang leader. "I must warn you. If we use that teleportation formation, neither of us will be able to conceal the spatial fluctuations. That means the secret teleportation formation will be exposed. It will most likely be destroyed or seized by the authorities. I know you and Teng Dong Lang are close, but is sacrificing a secret teleportation formation truly worth it just to take revenge on two silver level opponents?"

The Axe Gang leader snorted coldly, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has close ties with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and now collaborates with the Li Jian Clan. They operate as mercenaries on the surface, but rumors suggest arms dealers back them. But do you truly believe they're merely a group of simple arms smugglers?"

Fu Zhao's expression turned puzzled.

Lin's face darkened.

The Axe Gang leader continued, “No, I don't believe they're just arms smugglers!”

"They are daggers, thrust toward our very hearts! They are the vanguard, advancing with great fanfare!"

"They used treacherous schemes to kill Teng Dong Lang directly, annihilated the Bayonet Gang, and yet still gained immense fame. This is a grave provocation against us!"

“We cannot swallow our pride. We must crush them to boost our morale.”

Lin sighed deeply: "Perhaps you are right. "

The Axe Gang leader: "No time to waste, we move now. Map!"

Fu Zhao immediately retrieved the map, unfurling it on the table. He pointed to a spot: “Our men caused a teleportation error. Their current location should be here.”

“Right. They're heading to Snow Rabbit City. This spot along their route is the optimal ambush point.”

The Axe Gang leader nodded. "Good. We'll head to that location ahead of time. But before we teleport again, we must notify Jia Bing. Have him arrive early with his tools. We need him to remove the magic contract binding me before the operation."

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had signed a cooperation agreement with the Axe Gang.

Though the agreement imposed few constraints, dissolving it was still the safest course.

In pursuit of maximum victory rates, the Axe Gang leader planned meticulously.

"Father, I want to join the fight too," Fu Zhao pleaded.

The Axe Gang leader had clearly thought this through beforehand and shook his head immediately, “There's no need. Three against two, and we're gold level experts ambushing two silver level opponents. There's no chance of losing. In my absence, you will lead the Axe Gang."

“Trust me, future battles will come in abundance for you.”

Fu Zhao felt deeply regretful but could only accept the order.

The Axe Gang leader and Lin immediately set off, racing against the clock. Both men had military backgrounds, deeply understanding that opportunities in battle were fleeting. Their execution capabilities were formidable.

Snowflakes drifted silently before their eyes.

In the distance.

A mountain peak stood tall in the northeast.

The terrain sloped gently in the other three directions.

“Where are we?” The people of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps looked around in confusion.

“Weren't we supposed to be teleported to Snow Rabbit City?” Many voiced their confusion.

“It's freezing!” Some mercenaries shivered uncontrollably, unaccustomed to suddenly finding themselves in a snowy landscape.

The deeper into the heart of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom they ventured, the colder the climate became.

Taking the royal capital as the center point, the entire Ice Sculpture Island formed an irregular circle. Snow Rabbit City lay roughly at the one-third mark of its diameter, yet the surrounding temperatures already approached those of the inner two-thirds.

The cause of the bitter cold was plain for all members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to see.

It was the distant mountain range - Splice Mountain.

The mountains held an exceptionally potent concentration of ice and snow elements, generating the frigid climate.

Chaos erupted within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Zong Ge and Zi Di conferred.

Zi Di deliberately spoke loudly: "Calm down. This is likely due to uncalibrated teleportation coordinates. Such errors when using teleportation formations are not uncommon."

Prompted by her words, a snow elf immediately realized with a start: "So this is a teleportation error! I've indeed heard that such mistakes are unavoidable."

Someone objected, “The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's alchemy is exceptionally advanced. Surely they could fix teleportation errors?”

Zi Di reassured them again, "As far as I know, the Sheng Ming Empire faces the same issue. Such teleportation errors have been significantly improved. Even when mistakes occur, they're usually near the original destination. We shouldn't be far from Snow Rabbit City."

With that, Zi Di produced an activity map.

This map had been acquired from the Li Jian Clan at a steep price.

The cost was justified.

It covered virtually the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom in meticulous detail. Naturally, military strongholds, royal enclosures, and such were omitted.

Given its extensive and granular coverage, such a map was impossible to find on the open market within the kingdom.

Only a massive power like the Li Jian Clan could sell it without fear of being reported.

Zi Di quickly located their current position on the map.

Just as she had suspected, despite the teleportation mishap, they were indeed near Snow Rabbit City.

Zong Ge leaned in, glanced at the map, and whispered to Zi Di, “Be careful. I don't think this ‘mishap’ is a coincidence.”

As an outstanding commander, he never relied on luck.

Zi Di's gaze fixed on the map.

Based on their current position, they needed to head southeast to reach Snow Rabbit City by evening. To the northeast lay the Splice Mountain.

Zi Di's brow furrowed slightly. "We must indeed be cautious. We won't go to Snow Rabbit City for now. Head south instead. There's a Rabbit Fur Town nearby, which also has a teleportation formation."

"Though it's a small formation with limited range, we can use it to teleport to Snow Rabbit City first. When we want to leave later, we can simply use the large teleportation formation within Snow Rabbit City."

Zi Di was extremely cautious.

At the slightest hint of trouble, she immediately adjusted their strategy.

If the teleportation malfunction was caused by enemies, then Snow Rabbit City was temporarily off-limits.

Zi Di was deeply concerned they might face another ambush along the way.

Chapter 659: Zong Ge and Zi Di Disagree Again

Different teleportation formations had different teleportation distances.

Generally speaking, in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, villages did not have teleportation formations and towns had a small sized one that could teleport one to a major city.

Said major city had teleportation formations that could teleport one to neighboring major cities, and the large cities even had teleportation formations that went to the royal capital.

When the group of Dragon Lion Mercenaries going to the royal capital encountered a teleportation lapse, it put Zi Di and Zong Ge very on edge.

Zi Di suggested that they retreat and go back to Snow Rabbit Town first.

This was a good suggestion.

Zong Ge immediately agreed.

In light of the Cold Wind Valley Ambush, where the enemy hid their heads and showed their tails, it was very clear they didn’t want to reveal who they were. If they were to attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps inside a town, it would undoubtedly reveal their identities and cause the Ice Sculpture Government to order their arrests, thus, the possibility of doing so was small.

Zong Ge, Zi Di, and the rest set off to Rabbit Fur town.

The reason why Rabbit Fur Town was named so was because it was the biggest producer of rabbit fur in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. The snow rabbit fur Rabbit Hair Town produced was not just white as the snow and smooth, but it also could have transcendent quality – bronze level, iron level, silver level, and even gold level goods were produced.

One must know that snow rabbits were hard to raise, for only wild snow rabbits had quality rabbit fur.

When wild snow rabbits were kept in an enclosure for a long time, even if strong ice elements were provided, the rabbit fur quality would swiftly decline. Without predators to induce the snow rabbit into constantly running, the snow rabbit fur would constantly thicken, and the snow white fur would turn pure white, and even become grizzled, causing its value to decline.

The reason why Rabbit Fur Town could produce rabbit fur at such a large scale was not because they knew how to rear them on a massive scale, for if such a method really existed, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s royal family would have come to claim it personally, it wouldn’t be kept in such a remote village.

The reason why Rabbit Fur Town could produce so much rabbit fur was because of snow rabbit-men

Rabbit Fur Town was close to Splice Mountain.

Splice Mountain had an enormous snow rabbit-man clan living on it.

Every year a large amount of snow rabbit-men would come down from the mountain and sell their fur to Rabbit Fur Town. They wouldn’t go to Snow Rabbit Town. Rabbit Fur Town was sufficient to meet their demands. Furthermore, the town’s militia was far smaller than Snow Rabbit Town’s. This gave the trading snow rabbit-men a greater sense of security.

In fact, there were many cultivators that would take a risk out of desperation and secretly poach snow rabbit-men. Despite the snow rabbit-man clan and Ice Sculpture’s royal family signing a treaty of peaceful coexistence and hunting snow rabbit-men being a crime.

The snow fell more and more.

When the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps group teleported here, there were only sporadic snowflakes wafting through the sky.

By the time they were half way to their destination, the sky’s snowflakes were like goose feathers and were gradually growing larger.

This kind of weather was common near Splice Mountain, and there was weather nastier than this.

The heavy snow fall fluttered through the air, greatly hindering Xu Ma’s scouting.

He was only a bronze level thief, and it was hard for him to resist this bitter cold, and he quickly began to tremble in the frost.

The group’s scouting was not entrusted to him.

From the start of the journey, Zi Di deployed scouting alchemy puppets.

Not just that, she had also disseminated her transformed snake and rat ghosts too.

After a half hour, the snow got heavier and there were even a few hailstones mixed in with the snowflakes.

“Stop!” Zi Di suddenly shouted.

She pursed her brows tightly and glanced at Zong Ge.

Zong Ge’s heart shook and he ran his eyes around, then used a steady tone to order, “Rest and reorganize here for the time being.”

The group happened to have a gradual slope nearby.

The group walked downwind to the gradual slope, then took out fire starters and cooking equipment to rest here for now.

Zi Di and Zong Ge went to a corner.

After checking no one was eavesdropping, Zi Di looked grave, “Three gold levels were discovered in front of us. There are two renowned fighters and one magician, one of the fighters is the one who ambushed us in Cold Win Valley.”

Zong Ge’s face sank, “Three gold levels…they really think highly of us.”

Zi Di was glad, “How fortunate that before we set out, I remodeled my rat and snake ghosts. The three gold levels are hiding in a magic tent, the scouting puppets couldn’t have sensed them, it was a rat ghost would saw through it.”

“This is the advantage of a new school. Now, we have the initiative here.” After a few seconds, Zong Ge’s heavy look was restored to calm.

Zi Di replied, “We didn’t go to Snow Rabbit City, but Rabbit Fur Town, yet this movement had been detected by our enemies, and now they lie in ambush for us.”

“Where can we flee to?”

“We are close to Rabbit Fur Town, but the enemy has learned of that already. Snow Rabbit City is the furthest city, and even if we want to make use of the city guard, the city guard cannot mobilize to such a distant place.”

Zi Di pondered over a magic map, looking at the other cities and towns, paying attention to their distance to these places.

But Zong Ge extended a hand over the map, blocking Zi Di’s drilling gaze, “WE have the initiative now, we shouldn’t want to flee, rather we should ambush them instead.”

“It is too late to flee; three gold levels are far faster than us. The other side also has a magician who can definitely fly. When we choose another route, it will be quickly detected by them and they will realize that we can detect their tracks, that will most certainly cause them to attack first.”

“But if we take the initiative to attack, then it will be an excellent battle opportunity!”

Zi Di’s eyes widened, “You want to attack first?”

The young lady was flabbergasted as she looked at Zong Ge, she was very skeptical if the teleportation had had left Zong Ge’s brain in Blood Kin City.

Zong Ge’s face didn’t waver, and his tone was impatient, “Don’t let fear disturb you, Miss Merchant. Think carefully, we have a massive advantage.”

Zi Di almost found it laughable.

“The enemy is three gold levels, while the strongest among us is me and you, two mere silvers. If we included the silver level ghost Lan Lu, then we’ll barely have three. I don’t know what advantage you’re talking about!”

Zong Ge replied bluntly, “Deceptive Disguise – this is our strongest trump card.”

Zi Di’s eyes narrowed, and her tone became somewhat hurried, “That is indeed our trump card, but don’t forget, we are heading to the Ice Sculpture Royal Capital.”

“We have lost contact with the Lord Commander, and although we have many Pearl Bubbles on us, they are water without a source.”

“We don’t know how long we will stay in the royal capital and how long the Pearl Bubbles will last.”

“We shouldn’t misuse them wantonly.”

“If the Deceptive Disguise spell fades away, and our true identities are revealed, then we will die in the royal capital!”

Especially Zi Di who was a necromancer, if she was exposed, it would surely be a terrifying mortal blow.

It was a nation’s capital; how would it tolerate a necromancer?

Zi Di also said, “Even if we still had the Mermaid Fairy Tale with us, the divine artifact’s production rate declined with each passing day. Only by linking up with imperial higherups as swiftly as possible while we still can will we have a chance of redemption, that is the most important part of our plan.”

Zong Ge shook his head, his attitude resolute, “No, it is most valuable to use the Pearl Bubbles now.”

“Miss Merchant, do you not realize what kind of situation you are in?”

“The enemy is mysterious and very powerful, before it was one gold level ambushing us, now it is three. What kind of power can accomplish this?”

“After the commander beheaded Official Teng Dong, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ name was propagated nationwide, they are still attacking us. To deal with us two silver levels, they didn’t hesitate to mobilize three gold levels. What is the enmity, what is the grudge?”

“Our opponent’s motives are unknown, and if we don’t know who the enemy is or where they come from, that is the worst case!”

“We can attack now, perhaps we can kill one or two and gain crucial information. Most importantly, we can display our true strength and make the enemy realize that if they want to kill us, they will have to pay a disastrous price. If that happens, we will have true motive power in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“Otherwise, even if we can escape this time, the enemy won’t let this matter rest, they will impede us everywhere and manufacture an opportunity to launch a surprise attack on us. In the next attack, they will be more cautious and even eliminate the undead ghost scouts. Our situation will be worse than it is now!”

Zi Di was silent for a moment, then she grumbled out, “We already have a trail that can be investigated.”

Zong Ge coldly snorted, “Is your trail reliable? Since the other side can use puppets to cut off their retreat, why would they leave behind evidence? Furthermore, do we have a suitable candidate that can trace this trail to the truth? Who do you think can do that? Xu Ma?”

Zi Di grit her teeth, “The disparity between the enemy and us is to big…”

Zong Ge directly conceded, “Of course it will be risky, it is impossible to have no risks this battle. With the Deceptive Disguise spell, I am confident I can cut down at least one gold level. We must inflict pain on them and display our strength and threat.”

“Remember that time in Snow Bird Harbor? With the help of Deceptive Disguise, we were completely invisible to location detection equipment, nether that gold level assassin nor Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord sensed anything. Does that not prove the strategic value of Deceptive Disguise sufficiently?”

Zi Di hesitated, she wanted to say something.

Zong Ge interrupted her, “That is the reason why I will fight alone. If you discharge magic, that magic won’t be protected by Deceptive Disguise, that is actually be a weakness.”

“Even if I can’t beat them, I can entangle them for a time. You can take the people away, seize the opportunity for a rapid retreat, and head for Rabbit Fur Town. It will be safer there.”

Zong Ge’s tone was much milder than it was in Cold Wind Valley.

Because he had to win over Zi Di’s support.

He was not a magician, he still needed Zi Di to use the Deceptive Disguise formation.

Chapter 660: As Long as Victory is Possible!

Zi Di pondered quickly.

After a few brief minutes, her head was full of sweat.

Zi Di wanted to avoid battle as much as possible, but Zong Ge’s words made sense; if they didn’t take up this challenge now, next time, they would be worse off and in a more passive state.

Zong Ge was also taking responsibility; he wanted to fight alone and give Zi Di and the others a better chance of escaping.

“I…agree!” After Zi Di made her decision, her entire body seemed to be relieved from a burden.

At once, Zi Di and Zong Ge walked to the other side of the sloped mount, and after deploying temporary arrangements, they took out formation slabs from storage equipment.

Zi Di was skilled at installing these slabs and quickly activated the Deceptive Disguise formation.

She had privately trained doing this, to prevent accidents from occurring.

Zong Ge walked into the middle of the formation, then Zi Di took out a Pearl Bubble, fusing it slowly into the activating Deceptive Disguise formation.

The magic formation sparkled, and erupted with a fierce magic aura, but Zi Di and Zong Ge weren’t worried about it being exposed.

Because this formation had also been imbued with Deceptive Disguise, and gold level investigation magic would be hard pressed to see through it.

Zong Ge personally determined the camouflage settings this time.

The effect was very simple, and also very direct, it was total invisibility!

Zong Ge first used it on his equipment. The Gold Flower Hacksaw used 2 Pearl Bubbles, and the rest of his equipment, along with himself used 9 more.

Even though Zi Di knew the truth, when she looked at Zong Ge, she only saw indistinct shadows.

“I also want to take some Pearl Bubbles along. I want 20! When crushed during combat, perhaps it will have a wonderous effect.” Zong Ge requested upon leaving the formation.

Zi Di shook her head, “To be safe, take 30.”

She only had 60 Pearl Bubbles on hand, and for this battle, she invested over 40 of them.

When Zong Ge took the Pearl Bubbles, his eyes betrayed his astonishment.

At this moment, he had a whole new level of respect for Zi Di.

Zi Di reluctantly said, “I am a great merchant, and at this crucial juncture, a great merchant is willing to part with her investment.”

“Follow my ghosts, they can lead you to the enemy’s tent.”

The squalls screamed and great snow wafted through the air.

Zong Ge, a one man army, departed. He followed a snake ghost through the wind and snow, heading towards the enemy’s location.

His entire being and equipment was blessed by Deceptive Disguise, to those unaware, he looked transparent. In this kind of blizzard filling the sky, there were nearly no flaws in his camouflage.

Battle was imminent, and Zong Ge grew calmer, he even recalled some memories.

The wind and snow as far as the eye could see in his eyes gradually turned into a sandstorm.

When Zong Ge was still an iron level, he had been following a swordsmanship military instructor that was leading a group of cavalry in a desert battle.

Desert bandits united together to elect a sand bandit king, they had already destroyed a good few towns and plundered and massacred five oases.

The nobles had also united and established an allied army.

The decisive battle was here, and both armies were visible to each other in the distance, however, a sudden sandstorm swept in.

This was abnormal weather stirred up by the Sand God the desert bandits believed in/

The noble collation hurried to use large scale magic battle formations but found it hard to change the situation. The noble collation was gravely perplexed and its tight battle formations were thrown into disorder.

The desert bandits seized the opportunity to launch their attack, and the superior noble collation sank into a bitter struggle, many nobles saw things were far from good and were already beginning to retreat.

The cavalry group Zong Ge was with had lost contact with the main army and had been trudging through the sandstorm for a good while.

Visibility in the sandstorm was very low, and Zong Ge’s eyes could only see a hundred meters around him.

“It’s like that now.” Zong Ge’s eyes cleared as the yellow sand changed back to the snow of reality,

He stopped walking and looked into the distance.

In the snow and wind everywhere, he saw the faint outline of a dark green magic tent.

It was like that sandstorm, when he saw the desert bandits’ great tents.

“Found the enemy!”

“It’s the desert bandit king’s camp!!”

The cavalry hastily reported.

The swordsmanship military instruct burst into laughter, “It seems the sandstorm is also disturbing the desert bandit king, I heard that his conviction isn’t pious, our opportunity has come!”

The still iron level Zong Ge looked worried, “But instructor, only a third of us remain.”

Most of the cavalry had been lost in the sandstorm or perhaps became deserters.

“With our current troops, can we charge into the enemy’s main camp? If we get stuck here, and enemy gets reinforcements, we will all die tragically.” Zong Ge expressed his concerns.

The swordsmanship instructor replied, “The enemy is obviously not on their guard, when we raid them, they will be caught off guard and will likely sink into chaos. Most wonderful of all, they won’t know how many people we have. The sandstorm filling the entire sky is also a hindrance to our enemies!”

Zong Ge grinded his teeth, “But can we win…”

The swordsmanship instructor suddenly interrupted him, “Don’t hesitate Zong Ge, this is war!”

“Look for every chance of victory, even if it puts yon into danger.”

“We have the initiative, so long as there is a chance of victory, we should bet it all.”

“Troops, here my order, prepare to charge!”

The bugle horns of that charge from times past seemed to echo in Zong Ge’s ears.

He softly took out the Gold Flower Hacksaw and strode towards the magic tent.

Inside the tent, the magician Jia Bing was in the middle of casting.

His casting target was Axe Gang Leader.

“Endure it, it might be a little painful.” Jia Bing reminded him.

He was in the process of removing a magic contract that bound the Axe Gang Leader.

The Axe Gang Leader laughed heartily, “Do not hesitate.”

A moment later, a magic radiance filled the entire tent with a tempestuous transcendent aura.

“I will stand guard.” Lin maintained his coordination from the army, so he alone lifted the tent flap and walked into the blizzard.

He walked a circle around the tent; the detection battle skill didn’t notice any tracks.

He walked to the side of the tent, his back to the wind, then untied his underpants, fished out his appendage, and began to urinate.

“Hm?” While urinating, he slightly wrinkled his eyebrows, subconsciously turning around.

There was only the wind and the snow.

He kept turning around as liquid poured down, then he suddenly felt a trace of pace in his abdomen.

The pain wasn’t big, and Lin muttered, “Have I found too many women recently?”

“I am a gold level, how can I suddenly feel a little empty?”

A moment later, he sank into darkness.

The hacksaw severed his upper body, casting it into the snow.

Whoosh!

The tent flap lifted up.

The Axe Gang Leader and Jian Bing turned around at the same time, only seeing the wind and snow blowing past the tent flap and into the tent itself.

Jai Bing had a flash of unease but soon thought of the continued snow outside the tent, he was a gold level and had detection type battle skills, once the strongest in the army.

Jia Bing relinquished his worry and continued to focus his spirit on casting.

“The now is getting heavier, are those people still out there?” The Axe Gang Leader was also a bit uneasy.

Jai Bing put some energy into a magic map on the table, “Don’t worry, those people are still headed towards Rabbit Fur Town, according to their speed, they will get there by evening.”

A moment later, Jia Bing’s head flew into the air, his nape spraying blood all over the Axe Gang Leader’s face.

From the Axe Gang Leader’s perspective, the wind and snow was strongly fluttering across his face, “The snow is a little odd…”

He muttered, then his expression slightly changed, “Hey! Don’t tell me you finished? I still feel the magic contract on me.”

He was completely unaware of Jia Bing’s tragic end.

“Lin, Jia Bing, what is our countersign?!” The Axe Gang Leader shouted, his brows greatly wrinkling.

Zong Ge walked slowly to his side, then upon hearing what was said, quickly crushed a Pearl Bubble in his palm.

The Axe Gang Leader’s brows slowly smoothed out, “That’s the right countersign, but what’s going on? I feel…”

He unconsciously used a defensive battle skill.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw was raised up, then stabbed straight down.

A moment later, the Axe Gang Leader’s chest was impaled through the chest. His heart was fragmented into flecks in an instant.

Berserk battle qi corroded him from the inside, rapidly bursting all of the Axe Gang Leader’s internal organs.

The Axe Gang Leader’s eyes widened, and before he arrived at death’s door, he saw through the illusion and grabbed hold of the hem of Zong Ge’s clothes.

Blood splattered, causing a Blood Enmity Axe to burst forth in a flash of light.

The strange light squarely hit Zong Ge.

This was the Axe Gang Leader’s final struggle.

A second later, his blood dyed hand went limp.

It was silent inside the magic tent as the odor of blood spread out, only Zong Ge was left standing there, expressionless.

A dozen minutes later.

Zi Di obtained the news and decided to break away from the main group to greet the returning Zong Ge.

In the sky covering wind and snow, Zong Ge was carrying three gold level corpses towards Zi Di.

Shock flashed through Zi Di’s face, then soon after it was replaced by more complex emotions.

Zi DI deeply gazed at the half beastman, “So, you were already a gold level. When did it happen?”

Zong Ge calmly glanced at her, “At Snow Birg Harbor, after the battle with Li Jian Clan’s Yue Qian.”

On Mystifying Monster Island, Zong Ge’s battle qi had a golden luster, he was only a step away from the gold level.

On Mystifying Monster Island, his arm had been maimed, causing him to not have a speedy promotion.

After his injury had been healed, and with the help of the battle with Yue Qian, he finally broke through into the gold level. However, he didn’t leak this information out and even Tripleblade was kept in the dark about it.

Zi Di took inn a soft breath, then showed her understanding, “No wonder you decided to spar with Yue Qian.”

“Also at Cold Wind Valley, when you and that gold level fighter did battle, you didn’t get suppressed at all.”

“When faced with three gold levels, you chose to attack, because you were completely confident in yourself.”

“You…” When Zi Di said that she hesitated.

Her mood was very complicated.

On the one hand, she was happy the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps finally had a gold level. On the other hand, she was also worried that after Zong Ge was promoted to the gold level, it would gravely batter the dragonman youngster’s position of leadership.

Zong Ge explained, “Promoting to the gold level is not something worth flaunting to me. What is more crucial is to deceive the enemy, and to do that, I needed to start with deceiving our people. The facts prove that keeping this important information is very favorable for us!”

After saying this, his body glinted with a blood light, and soon after, the corner of his mouth leaked with a trace of blood.

Chapter 661: Splice Mountain, Graft Town

A sharp pain shot through him, causing Zong Ge's face to twist unnaturally for a moment.

“What happened?” Zi Di asked with concern.

After all, Zong Ge was one of their own. His promotion to gold level was a tremendous boon for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Especially now, when the enemy was powerful and mysterious, and they hadn't yet fully understood their capabilities, Zong Ge had become the absolute mainstay of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Naturally, Zi Di hoped he was in good condition.

Zong Ge drew the Blood Vengeance Axe from his back: “It's this weapon! I killed the Axe Gang leader but fell victim to his final trick.”

“Periodically, a crimson blood aura swirls around me, striking vital points inside and outside my body.”

“The exact timing is unpredictable. I must remain constantly vigilant, channeling my battle qi for protection to survive.”

Zi Di's brows instantly furrowed: "The Axe Gang leader? He's the one who ambushed us? Who are the other two? Let me examine you first. Perhaps I can purge this blood aura."

Zong Ge nodded.

He'd already tried, but could only defend passively, unable to locate the source.

Magic offered more diverse methods than battle qi; perhaps Zi Di could provide assistance.

After a thorough examination, Zi Di confirmed Zong Ge was indeed a genuine gold level fighter but found no other clues.

Having meticulously analyzed the battle sequence Zong Ge described, Zi Di concluded: “This is likely the power of the Blood Vengeance Axe, merely activated by the Axe Gang leader.”

“Based on our current intelligence, the Blood Vengeance Axe is no ordinary gold level weapon.”

“To eliminate this threat, we must focus our efforts on the axe itself. That holds the key to resolving this.”

Zong Ge nodded. "I've taken down three gold level opponents, but we can't afford to be complacent. Let's depart immediately!"

“Wait. Before we go, you must replenish your Deceptive Disguise spell first,” Zi Di reminded him.

Zong Ge looked at himself and nodded. "During intense combat, the Deceptive Disguise spell drains energy extremely rapidly. In fact, I crushed two Pearl Bubbles during the fight."

Following this, Zong Ge explained to Zi Di how the Deceptive Disguise spell and Pearl Bubbles performed in actual combat.

"Before the fight, I used the Deceptive Disguise spell to make myself ‘completely invisible.’ Because the camouflage was so deep, after slaying the first gold level fighter, the spell's effect diminished by more than half."

“I had no choice but to shatter the first Pearl Bubble.”

“The Pearl Bubble's power acted on the battlefield, allowing me to lift the tent flap without alerting the enemy.”

"Under the scrutiny and reconnaissance of a gold level fighter, Deceptive Disguise spell's effectiveness plummeted. Especially after I struck, the moment the enemy suffered heavy damage, I distinctly felt the spell's power diminish drastically."

"Inside the tent, I crushed the second pearl bubble. Even so, the Axe Gang leader launched a death throes counterattack."

“After that battle, these are the only Pearl Bubbles I have left.”

At this point, Zong Ge produced eight Pearl Bubbles.

Zi Di listened intently while retrieving the formation diagram panel and installing the Deceptive Disguise Formation.

Seeing the eight pearl bubbles, she sighed, “The higher the level of deception, the greater the spell's consumption, this is something we'd already tested before.”

“I just didn't expect it to consume this much still.”

Before Zong Ge departed, Zi Di gave him thirty pearl bubbles. Upon his return, only eight remained. Including the Deceptive Disguise spell Zong Ge had to recast before and after the battle, his total pearl bubble consumption was almost thirty.

When Zi Di set out from Snow Bird Harbor, she had carried only about sixty pearl bubbles herself. Meaning Zong Ge's battle alone had used up half her reserves.

Zi Di retrieved only five: “The remaining three are for your use, in case of unforeseen circumstances.”

"Logically speaking, losing three gold level beings at once would be a massive blow to any faction within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Even if they wanted to target us, they'd surely hesitate to act."

Zong Ge nodded in agreement with Zi Di's assessment. For the Ice Sculpture Kingdom wasn't the Sheng Ming Empire; its strongest beings were merely at the saint level. Below saint level were the gold level individuals.

Gold level individuals formed the backbone of any force.

Take the Axe Gang, for instance: with their leader deceased, their remaining strongest combatants would instantly drop to silver level, inevitably triggering massive turmoil. Handled poorly, it could very well lead to the gang's dissolution.

Zong Ge carefully tucked the pearl bubbles into his robe: "Our use of the Pearl Bubble is too crude. If we had a high-ranking cleric from the Charming Blue Church, I believe the Pearl Bubble's consumption would plummet significantly, and the Deceptive Disguise spell's effect would be far superior."

He turned his gaze to the three corpses lying on the ground. "The Axe Gang leader was the head of the gang, but he was once a soldier of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. His identity and background bear a striking resemblance to Teng Dong Lang. I strongly suspect a close connection between them. This might explain why we were ambushed!"

The clue was too obvious. As a commander skilled in battlefield analysis, Zong Ge immediately pieced together part of the truth.

“As for the other two, I also suspect they share the same background as Teng Dong Lang and the Axe Gang leader,” Zong Ge stated.

Zi Di smiled faintly: “They're both gold level, no small figures. A little digging will reveal their backgrounds and identities.”

“I have a feeling this time, our gains will be substantial!”

The triple reconnaissance of Xu Ma, the alchemy puppets, and the modified ghosts kept the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps ahead in intelligence gathering.

After reapplying a Deceptive Disguise spell, Zong Ge reunited with Zi Di and his subordinates.

None of them were privy to the fact that during Zong Ge's brief absence, he had achieved the formidable feat of slaying three gold level opponents.

The journey ahead proceeded smoothly.

By evening, the group entered Rabbit Fur Town.

Their first task was to request teleportation.

The teleportation formation in Rabbit Fur Town remained heavily used. Even with Zi Di spending a fortune in bribes, they were still booked until the following afternoon.

After consulting with Zong Ge, Zi Di decided to stay overnight in Rabbit Fur Town and use the teleportation formation to reach Snow Rabbit City.

After a hearty dinner, they obtained a crucial piece of intelligence from information purchased from subordinates.

“Magician Jia Bing?”

“A powerful magician retired from the military, an Alchemy Great Master. His specialty is crafting variants of the alchemy puppet, a gargoyle – an ice gargoyle.”

“So, his storage belt should contain ice gargoyles?”

“Fantastic!” Zi Di's eyes lit up, already envisioning acquiring ice-carving techniques from Jia Bing's legacy.

This was an alchemy technique unique to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!

"Additionally, Magician Jia Bing maintains an alchemy laboratory in Grafting Town. He occasionally trades with outsiders, mainly selling alchemy tools.

Noticing this, Zong Ge spoke: "It seems we need to make a trip to Grafting Town."

A gold level magician, who was also an Alchemy Great Master, made Zi Di and Zong Ge decide to make a detour to his alchemy laboratory.

Zi Di immediately checked the intel on Grafting Town.

Situated at the foot of Splice Mountain, Grafting Town was the closest settlement to the mountain. Due to environmental factors and poor transportation, its size was less than a third of Rabbit Fur Town.

The origin of Grafting Town was tied to the legendary druid, Fan Sheng Za Zhong.

This great druid, a wood elf and direct descendant of the Fan Sheng Clan, excelled in grafting techniques that enabled different plants to coexist and thrive.

During his travels, he had journeyed to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and conducted numerous cultivation experiments on Splice Mountain. The drastic transformation of Splice Mountain's landscape drew widespread attention, threatening the rule and interests of certain factions.

The snow elves' transcendents united to expel Fan Sheng Za Zhong. The great druid descended to battle the snow elves. His masterful grafting abilities displayed in combat shattered their morale, forcing their retreat.

The battlefield became home to several colossal, grafted hybrid magic plants, formed from the residual power of the great druid.

Years later, some snow elves successfully survived the harsh cold of Splice Mountain beneath these plants, gradually forming Grafting Town.

"According to intelligence, the five magic plants in Grafting Town still retain potent grafting capabilities. Severely damaged plants can be directly grafted onto their trunks, drawing life-sustaining nutrients to slowly recover and sprout anew."

“Interesting.”

Zi Di began anticipating the journey to Grafting Town.

Night deepened.

As the ship neared port, the captain breathed a sigh of relief, navigating at night carried far greater risks than by day. It wasn't the danger of getting lost; that probability was low. The captain's greatest fear was pirate attacks, especially since the emergence of the Pirate Throne had unleashed rampant piracy worldwide. Even once-safe routes now harbor hidden dangers.

The Gold level Magician Da Bei and silver level Magician Zhong Bei disembarked, formally setting foot on Flower Skirt Island.

They suppressed their auras and activated magic artifacts, disguising themselves as two iron level fighters.

The identity of iron level transcendents drew less attention while still commanding enough respect to deter many from acting rashly. This saved Da Bei and Zhong Bei a lot of trouble.

Especially since their mission was to seek Master Cai Jing's assistance in crafting a replica of the dragon rider potion, minimizing unnecessary complications was paramount.

It was precisely for this reason that they concealed their true identities.

Da Bei didn't want to expose himself to arouse suspicion from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, potentially forcing him into conflict with these arms smugglers.

What Da Bei hoped for was to handle the matter discreetly, eliminating the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps while remaining entirely uninvolved.

"Let's head to the tavern first and inquire about Master Cai Jing's residence. It's too late for a visit, so we'll need to find an inn for the night." Da Bei whispered.

Zhong Bei nodded repeatedly.

The largest tavern on Flower Skirt Island was located right in the harbor.

Da Bei and Zhong Bei entered the tavern, still drawing no attention.

Though they were unfamiliar faces, the harbor was a place of constant movement, teeming with strangers.

The first and second floors were filled almost entirely with ordinary folk, while the transcendents occupied the third and fourth floors.

Bearing the status of iron level fighters, the two ascended to the tavern's third floor.

There, they found a crowd gathered around a massive fish tank, shouting and cheering incessantly.

Zhong Bei scanned the scene and spotted a blue-skinned, curly-haired, triangular-headed fishman and a snow elf fighter engaged in a high-stakes wager over a miniature fish-themed tactical board game.

By the time Zhong Bei and Da Bei found an empty table and sat down, the match had become a one-sided affair.

The fishman held a clear advantage, drawing roars of encouragement from the crowd: "Hen Fa! Hen Fa! Hen Fa!"

Chapter 662: Big and Small Fish, Magic Battle Chess

"Hen Fa! Hen Fa! Hen Fa!" A group of people surrounded a massive fish tank and shouted.

The massive fish tank was filled with blue seawater. Within the water, lifelike miniature models swam about.

These tiny models were mostly various marine creatures, like fish, shrimp, shellfish...

Lan Zao stood at one side of the massive fish tank. His fingers splayed wide, with his palms pressed firmly against the tank's base where a pair of palm-shaped indentations served as pre-set control points.

He focused intently, manipulating his magic model pieces within the fish tank.

The massive fish tank functioned as a silver level magic chessboard, continuously absorbing the battle qi and spirit power of both players.

Lan Zao commanded a tiny electric shark.

A real shark would dwarf his entire body, but the piece he controlled was less than the length of his index finger.

The electric shark radiated an iron level aura. It was chasing a school of iron-plated batfish.

The batfish couldn't match the electric shark's speed, but they scattered in a panic, causing the shark to lunge at empty air.

The shark shot out an electric fish bone. At such close range, practically point-blank, even the most skillfully controlled batfish couldn't dodge. One was struck, its body dissipating like smoke.

The entire school numbered only five iron-plated batfish. Having lost one-fifth of their strength, the remaining batfish immediately scattered in panic.

This outcome drew cheers from the onlookers.

Clearly, Lan Zao held the upper hand.

His opponent was a snow elf fighter, his face now dark with gloom.

“You bastard just got lucky once, when your bloodline mutated into an electric shark, and now you're getting cocky!” The snow elf fighter cursed before focusing all his attention on controlling his pieces.

Under his command, the remaining four iron-plated batfish abruptly split into two groups, each fleeing to opposite sides.

Lan Zao controlled only one electric shark. Without hesitation, he chose the left path and continued the pursuit.

The snow elf fighter maneuvered the two fleeing iron-plated batfish, darting about to buy time. Meanwhile, he directed the other pair of iron-plated batfish to swim deep, heading toward the bottom of the massive fish tank.

The massive fish tank contained more than just seawater. At its base lay miniature coral reefs, fine sand, and pebbles, simulating an underwater landscape.

Dotted throughout the sea sand were numerous rubber sea cucumbers.

These sea cucumbers were among the fishmen's favorite foods. The higher the level, the larger, darker, and thicker the sea cucumbers became.

Deep within the coral clusters, numerous spitting cloth clams were also concealed. This transcendent shellfish species had been cultivated to prominence by a merchant alliance of the Sheng Ming Empire. The cloth woven from them now dominated the global textile market.

Two iron-plated batfish carried the entire hopes of the snow elf fighter.

Most spectators' eyes were fixed on these two batfish. Everyone knew: the two batfish being hunted by the electric sharks were merely sacrificial pawns for the snow elf fighter. If the snow elf fighter wanted to turn the tide, his true hope lay with the iron-plated batfish along this underwater route.

“What does he want to eat?”

“It's too late now to consume large quantities of rubber sea cucumbers, Hen Fa's electric shark is too powerful.”

"If he could ingest the spitting cloth clams, there might still be a sliver of hope for a comeback. But relying solely on two iron-plated batfish, there's absolutely no way they could breach the spitting cloth clam's hard shell in such a short time."

“At this stage of the Magic Battle Chess match, both combatants' battle qi should only have one final round of amplification left.”

“Speaking of which, what bloodline options are associated with the iron-plated batfish and spitting cloth clam?”

Spectators murmured among themselves, some silently calculating in their minds.

The snow elf fighter guided his two iron-plated batfish through the coral groves, ignoring the spitting cloth clams within and abandoning numerous rubber sea cucumbers. His target lay beyond the coral, a patch of deep green seaweed.

This choice baffled many spectators.

Some gamblers who'd bet on his victory began cursing under their breath.

Both Magician Da Bei and Magician Zhong Bei found themselves drawn into the duel.

Magician Zhong Bei possessed far greater knowledge than most transcendents present. Upon witnessing this, he instantly grasped the snow elf fighter's intent.

“Judging by the spiral shape of this deep green seaweed, it must be ascending dragon grass.”

“According to the latest bloodline charts, ascending dragon grass is linked to only two batfish bloodlines.”

“One is the green hair turtle, the other the bramble turtle.”

Unlike ordinary sea turtles, the inner shell of the green hair turtle was covered in “moss” -which was actually its downy fur. This alchemy material had widespread application.

The bramble turtle's physique has always been massive, with adult specimens resembling giant elephants on land. Herds of bramble turtles surge through the ocean depths with astonishing momentum. Their backs bristle with sharp thorns, serving both offensive and defensive purposes.

“Clearly, the direction he seeks for his bloodline mutation is the bramble turtle.”

“If he could mutate into a bramble turtle, he could turn the tables.”

"The electric shark is a sea beast with an advantage in electric attributes. When facing the wood-attributed bramble turtle, the effectiveness of electric attacks is significantly reduced."

This Magic Battle Chess game was called Big and Small Fish. Its rules dictated that two or more players each controlled their own pieces, continuously devouring other magic models on the board. Through constant consumption, bloodline mutations were triggered, causing the life forms of the pieces to undergo sudden transformations. Consequently, they acquired different functions or attribute changes.

Ultimately, through accumulated functional differences or elemental advantages, pieces clashed until only one remained, declaring the victor.

Thus, a skilled Big and Small Fish player had to possess not only abundant energy but also exceptional mental control techniques. More crucially, they needed profound bloodline knowledge! Players had to know precisely when to devour which creature models to transform them into specific life-form magic pieces. This allows them to counter opponents or resolve their own predicaments.

Lan Zao was utterly new to the Magic Battle Chess, Big and Small Fish.

He commanded the electric shark to pursue relentlessly, but the target remained elusive under his opponent's deft control. It took Lan Zao a considerable time to finally kill those two iron-plated batfish.

The electric sharks devoured these two batfish, merely extending their own lifespan without triggering bloodline mutation.

This was one of his opponent's deliberate choices in selecting iron-plated batfish—even in defeat, the resources gained by the enemy remained minimal.

After clearing one path, Lan Zao immediately drove his electric sharks forward, pursuing another pair of iron-plated batfish.

Those two iron-shelled batfish had already devoured vast quantities of ascending dragon grass.

The ascending dragon grass' bloodline combined with the batfish's own bloodline, causing both to undergo bloodline mutations prematurely as the electric shark closed in.

The two magic pieces instantly dissolved into wisps of smoke. The smoke dispersed outward before immediately coalescing again, forming new pieces in altered life forms.

But to the snow elf fighter's utter disappointment, the two newly born magic pieces were both green hair turtles! There was not a trace of the bramble turtle he had pinned his hopes on to turn the tide.

The snow elf fighter had originally reserved a portion of his battle qi, intending to invest it into the mutated bramble turtle piece to raise its level.

Players could expend their own battle qi, magic power, or divine power to increase their number of pieces or elevate the transcendent level of existing pieces.

But upon seeing his pieces ultimately transform into two green hair turtles, the snow elf fighter lost all desire to expend even a shred of his battle qi.

Defeat was certain!

Sure enough, under Lan Zao's control, the electric shark effortlessly tore the two green hair turtles to shreds and swallowed them whole.

Lan Zao had won.

The crowd who had bet on him erupted in a thunderous cheer, pushing the tavern's already feverish atmosphere to new heights.

Many repeatedly shouted the name “Hen Fa,” celebrating Lan Zao for winning them money.

After participating in this wild duel, Lan Zao's reputation began to spread among the transcendents.

The underground arena on Flower Skirt Island served as the primary entertainment venue for many transcendents on the island.

Though it was Lan Zao's first time participating in a wild duel, the battle skill he displayed in the arena, Air Cannon, left a deep impression on the spectators.

This time at the tavern, he “accidentally” got into a verbal altercation with the snow elf fighter. As the dispute escalated, both sides engaged in this Magic Battle Chess.

The tavern promptly and efficiently set up corresponding betting odds.

Due to Lan Zao's modest reputation, some gamblers placed their chips on him.

Of course, the primary reason was that Lan Zao was a novice in Magic Battle Chess, commanding much higher odds than his opponent.

Lan Zao's victory netted some gamblers substantial profits, prompting loud cheers. In an instant, Lan Zao became the tavern's brightest star.

His opponent, the snow elf fighter, stepped before him: "You got lucky this time. I didn't lose to you, I lost to luck!"

With that, the snow elf fighter stripped off his iron level leather armor and tossed it to Lan Zao.

This had been their agreed-upon wager before the match began.

Lan Zao felt no surprise at his victory.

From the moment he entered the tavern to the ensuing dispute and the Magic Battle Chess, it was all part of Shuang Tu's prearranged script.

Shuang Tu had instructed Lan Zao: “You've now advanced to iron level, but your equipment requires an upgrade.”

“Since you lack substantial funds, acquiring suitable gear will necessitate some groundwork.”

“Following my plan, you'll not only hone your disguise skills but also secure equipment replacement without arousing suspicion from Bing Xiao.”

Lan Zao needed to grow.

His bloodline had awakened, possessing 100% concentration of the Fish Bladder bloodline. Yet this merely signaled his future potential.

He still needed to reach a certain stage of growth, developing tangible combat capability. Only then, upon rejoining Bing Xiao, could he secure a higher standing—facilitating his mission to infiltrate Tranquil Hill. With insufficient strength, Bing Xiao would be reluctant to entrust him with critical tasks.

Chapter 663: Legend Level Inheritance

The primary purpose of this Magic Battle Chess was to legitimately secure iron level armor for Lan Zao.

Lan Zao felt no joy in victory.

Despite winning, his expression remained impassive.

The Hen Fa he portrayed was a man perpetually silent and taciturn.

However, this Magic Battle Chess sparked Lan Zao's interest in the Big and Small Fish when he encountered for the first time.

Even after winning, Lan Zao commanded his electric shark to begin devouring the ascending dragon grass.

After consuming large quantities of ascending dragon grass, the electric shark's bloodline underwent a transformation.

It dispersed into a cloud of mist.

Within a second, the mist coalesced anew, and the electric shark transformed into a dragon whisker electric eel.

Its entire body was covered in fine, smooth, azure-blue scales. Its most striking feature was the two long whiskers growing from the corners of each mouth.

This spectacle immediately drew gasps from the spectators.

Upon witnessing this, the snow elf fighter cursed again: “You have dog shit luck today!”

With that, he pushed through the crowd with a dark expression and left the bustling tavern.

The snow elf fighter was indeed a master strategist. He had actively played along with Lan Zao's show without revealing any flaws.

He had deceived every gambler in the hall, even the two magicians known as Zhong Bei and Da Bei.

Zhong Bei murmured in awe, "Combining the electric shark bloodline with ascending dragon grass produces countless mutations. Statistically, the probability of transforming into a dragon whisker electric eel is only 3%. This fishman's luck is truly extraordinary. Despite clearly being outmatched by his opponent, he emerged victorious."

"He's not a fishman, just a human fighter with the Fish Bladder bloodline. His Fish Bladder bloodline purity must be exceptionally high to manifest such a form.” As Magician Da Bei spoke, his expression grew complex, and he mused, ”So you see, luck is part of strength too."

Magician Zhong Bei's expression also darkened.

Back then, Magician Da Bei had gone to great lengths, paying a hefty price, to place his son Zhong Bei beside Magician Bu Quan. The plan was twofold: to secretly protect her and to monitor and document her daily activities.

Under normal circumstances, once Bu Quan returned to the Alchemy Guild, Magician Zhong Bei would be promoted and rewarded for this service.

But Magician Zhong Bei's luck was truly terrible. Bu Quan joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, stunning everyone. The plans of Da Bei and Zhong Bei were completely dashed.

To salvage the situation, Magician Da Bei had no choice but to take his son away from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to Flower Skirt Island, intending to seek help from Master Cai Jing.

After obtaining iron level leather armor, Lan Zao also left the tavern.

Most would have stayed at the tavern, boasting and drinking with the gamblers and drunkards around them, reveling in their victory.

But Lan Zao, disguised as the aloof Hen Fa, had a solitary nature, the type who acted fiercely but spoke little. Thus, his departure wasn't abrupt; it perfectly aligned with Hen Fa's character and habits.

This was no longer part of Shuang Tu's specific instructions. It was Lan Zao's own insight and choice after days of playing the role of Hen Fa.

Lan Zao's departure didn't arouse suspicion from Da Bei or Zhong Bei.

Neither of them was particularly familiar with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Their primary focus was on Magician Bu Quan, followed by key figures like Long Fu, Shi Qi, and Yao Ma. During his time in the mercenary corps, Lan Zao was an unremarkable bronze level sailor.

Now, though he'd risen to an iron level fighter, his appearance had undergone a drastic transformation due to his awakened bloodline. Even if he stood before unsuspecting survivors, recognition would be unlikely.

The father-son duo of Da Bei and Zhong Bei focused their attention more on gathering intelligence.

Simply sitting down in this bustling tavern, sipping a mug of beer, allowed them to glean valuable intelligence from the chatter of patrons.

Moreover, they could purchase valuable information directly from the bartender.

The father and son gained extensive insight into Flower Skirt Island's current state.

They uncovered the island's current power structure.

It was loose.

Several silver level individuals, each wielding their own influence, had carved out their respective spheres of influence across Flower Skirt Island. Should major events occur, these smaller factions would unite, deliberate, and then act in concert.

In ordinary times, they competed while cooperating and cooperated while competing.

The undisputed strongest on Flower Skirt Island was, of course, the gold level Master Cai Jing.

Several days ago, a clash between two gold level cultivators erupted on Flower Skirt Island. This confrontation remains a hot topic among tavern patrons to this day.

Both Ba Qi and Cai Jing voluntarily halted their exchange during the probing phase. It could be said the outcome was inconclusive.

Following this battle, Master Cai Jing turned his attention to the entire island, launching a large-scale recruitment drive for potion-making apprentices, a stark departure from his previous reclusive lifestyle.

Amidst this shift, another rumor began quietly spreading across Flower Skirt Island.

"A legend level great druid left their legacy hidden within Flower Skirt Island. Master Cai Jing deliberately chose to live in seclusion here to seek out and unearth this legacy."

"Had Ba Qi not suddenly arrived, clashed with him, and shattered the surface during their battle to reveal the subterranean caverns, this secret might have remained hidden until Master Cai Jing fully claimed the legacy."

The magician father and son exchanged glances upon hearing this whispered rumor, falling silent for a moment.

"Is this information true? Which legendary great druid is it exactly?” Asked Magician Zhong Bei, his expression doubtful.

“It should be Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong.” Murmured Magician Da Bei.

“This great druid once traveled through the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and lived in seclusion on Splice Mountain for a time. Back then, Splice Mountain was still called Ice Step Mountain. He changed its name. The Grafting Town at its base emerged as a settlement because of him."

"During his travels on Ice Sculpture Island, Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong seemed to gain much insight. His cultivation experiments indeed yielded significant results."

"Records indicate that after leaving Ice Sculpture Island, he lived here on Flower Skirt Island for a time. He transformed this barren island into a place teeming with life. This was likely the outcome of his cultivation experiments here.

As for whether he left behind any legacy... I always thought it was just a rumor. After all, similar tales circulate in places like Splice Mountain and elsewhere. It's only natural, a legend level figure like him, every move he made sparked imagination, giving birth to countless stories and legends."

“But now it seems the legacy here on Flower Skirt Island might actually be real.”

Magician Zhong Bei couldn't help but show a hint of curiosity: “Father, in your opinion, what exactly might this legendary legacy entail?”

“I have no idea about that.” Magician Da Bei shook his head, his brow furrowed.

Magician Zhong Bei grew troubled too: “Father, is this really the right time for us to come?”

Magician Da Bei nodded silently.

In his opinion, if he were Master Cai Jing, and had just unearthed a legend level druid legacy. Amidst swirling rumors, two magicians arrived. They'd disguised their identities and infiltrated Flower Skirt Island.

What would Master Cai Jing think?

“Could they be here to compete with me for this legacy?”

That would be a perfectly natural assumption.

After a long silence, Magician Da Bei finally spoke: “First thing tomorrow morning, we will formally visit Master Cai Jing, reveal our identities, and express the sincerity of our journey.”

Magician Zhong Bei agreed: "Exactly. Since we've come this far, we shouldn't abandon our efforts halfway. We must at least try. That way, even if we fail, we won't have regrets."

After spending the night at the inn, the two arrived at the southern part of Flower Skirt Island early the next morning.

The father and son voluntarily shed their disguises, revealing their true auras, and paid a visit to Master Cai Jing.

As they had anticipated, Master Cai Jing's attitude was far from welcoming, brimming with wariness and suspicion.

Even though Magician Da Bei and he shared a history and considerable friendship.

Magician Da Bei sincerely expressed his curiosity about the great druid's legacy, though he had neither interest nor inclination to pursue it. His primary purpose in coming was solely to request Master Cai Jing's assistance in replicating the dragon rider potion, for which he was willing to pay a sufficient price.

Master Cai Jing refused outright.

His primary focus now lay entirely on excavating the legacy.

Ever since his battle with Ba Qi exposed the subterranean caverns, he had, after deep deliberation, altered his excavation strategy. He began mass-recruiting potion apprentices, accelerating the digging process to seize precious time.

The secret rumors circulating on the island had long caught his attention.

This added pressure to his shoulders yet also solidified his resolve to excavate as swiftly as possible.

Under such circumstances, he would certainly not allow himself to be distracted. Who knew what unforeseen complications might arise if time dragged on?

Now, Master Cai Jing was single-minded in his pursuit, to secure this inheritance into his own hands as quickly as possible.

Better safe than sorry!

After failing to negotiate, Magician Da Bei reluctantly bid farewell to Master Cai Jing.

Yet as they returned to the dock to board their ship, a potions apprentice intercepted them, bearing a new proposal from Master Cai Jing.

The father and son thus journeyed back to the subterranean alchemy chamber in southern Flower Skirt Island to meet with Master Cai Jing.

Master Cai Jing addressed Magician Da Bei: “We can collaborate to jointly excavate the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong.”

"Is it truly the legacy of that legendary great druid? But why?" A look of astonished delight spread across Magician Da Bei's face.

Master Cai Jing explained the reason: “Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong set several trials. Only the final challenge remains. Once we overcome it, his legacy will be ours."

Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong excelled in the transplantation and grafting of magic plants. Through cross-grafting different magic plants, he could even create temporary, unique, extraordinary flora.

“Only by cultivating a hybrid magic plant that meets the challenge's requirements can one meet Master Za Zhong's standards and solve the final puzzle.”

Chapter 664: Electric Ball Projectile

Master Cai Jing said, "I have some experience cultivating magic plants, but I'm nowhere near Master Za Zhong's level. I could meet his standards, but it would take at least two years. And time is precisely what I lack right now."

“My plan is to recruit numerous potion apprentices. By mass-cultivating diverse magic plants and saturating them with my potions to force accelerated growth, we can maximize survival rates. With sufficient quantity, we may well obtain hybrid magic plants meeting the requirements.”

“With both of you assisting now, this plan's timeline will advance significantly.”

"Of course, if Magician Da Bei has better ideas, feel free to propose them. My original plan isn't set in stone."

“After all, over the years, we've worked together quite harmoniously.”

Da Bei and Zhong Bei exchanged a quick glance, both seeing delight in each other's eyes.

They had originally intended to ask Master Cai Jing to replicate the magic potion. But now, a legend level inheritance lay before them, their previous plan could be completely discarded!

With this legacy, Zhong Bei could establish his own advantage within the Alchemy Guild entirely on his own; there was no need to bother with Bu Quan anymore.

Da Bei immediately expressed his gratitude to Master Cai Jing.

This wasn't mere lip service; he pledged to compensate Master Cai Jing with corresponding magician resources based on the legacy's value.

Zhong Bei followed suit: "Who would have thought this trip to Flower Skirt Island would bring such a tremendous surprise? We will give it our all!"

Master Cai Jing nodded coldly.

It had taken him an hour of deliberation to make this difficult decision.

The exposure of the underground caverns caused by Ba Qi's attack made him realize he had to act swiftly. The spreading rumors had completely shattered any lingering hope he might have had.

At this moment, the arrival of Da Bei and Zhong Bei triggered extreme vigilance in Master Cai Jing.

In the worst-case scenario, these two had come solely for the inheritance of the legendary master.

“And this won't be the end—only the beginning.” After careful consideration, Master Cai Jing felt the situation would only worsen.

As time passed, the rumors spread farther and wider, inevitably drawing more people to the island.

Rather than facing numerous powerful adversaries alone when that time came, it would be wiser to sacrifice some of his own interests now, recruiting allies to defend his position.

In this light, Magician Da Bei emerged as a suitable collaborator.

Not only had they worked together before and understood each other well, but there was another crucial factor, Magician Da Bei was not skilled in combat.

Lan Zao honed his battle skill in the sea.

He continuously expelled air projectiles.

Each shot struck his chosen targets with pinpoint accuracy.

Through relentless training, his practice targets gradually shifted from stationary reefs and coral to moving schools of fish, turtles, crabs, squid, and more.

Following the training materials provided by Shuang Tu, after diligent practice, Lan Zao's accuracy rose to 100%.

"After cultivating the Air Pressure battle qi technique, I can compress air effortlessly. When these compressed air projectiles are fired, it feels as natural as using my own arms and legs."

“This is the power of bloodline!”

Lan Zao marveled.

The benefits of bloodline manifested in every aspect.

His pure Fish Bladder bloodline allowed Lan Zao to effortlessly master the Air Cannon battle skill. He progressed at an astonishing pace through subsequent training exercises.

“But achieving this level alone is insufficient.”

Following the methods outlined in the training materials, Lan Zao activated a compact alchemy device he carried.

The spherical device swam forward, stirring chaotic currents in the surrounding seawater.

The air projectiles Lan Zao fired lost their accuracy amid the turbulent currents.

He adapted through trial and error, quickly finding his rhythm.

As time passed, his hit rate climbed again, rapidly rising from the low ten percentages to over fifty percent.

This was the result of his bloodline talent - Fluid Perception.

Using Fluid Perception, he keenly sensed every movement of the currents. This allowed him to evade their interference and ensure the projectiles struck their targets cleanly.

After about half an hour of practice, Lan Zao's hit rate climbed to around 80%.

Another hour passed, and Lan Zao's hit rate dropped to just over 30%.

This was because he began experimenting with using ocean currents to increase the projectile's flight speed.

He already possessed the ability to harness ocean currents to boost his swimming speed, and could even utilize air currents to increase his flight speed and distance.

Now, applying this ability to accelerate air projectiles represented a further refinement of his skill.

He continued practicing until the sun dipped below the horizon, the sky ablaze with brilliant sunset hues. By then, Lan Zao's accuracy had rebounded to around 70%.

Shuang Tu arrived, witnessed the training results, and applauded on the spot in admiration.

“Your opponent for the next wild duel has been confirmed.”

“You will fight underwater. Your opponent will be a shark.”

Lan Zao inquired, “What specific type of shark? What is its transcendent level?"

Shuang Tu shook his head slightly, “I haven't been able to gather specific details yet. In truth, you must understand that as the organizers of the wild duel, they need to create certain illusions: either a balanced matchup or one where one side holds a clear advantage. This allows them to steer the number of bettors and maximize their profits in the wagers."

"Like earlier, our investigation had an error because the arena made last minute modifications to the duel. They replaced the original bronze level beast with an iron level snow eagle." Shuang Tu explained the discrepancy in their earlier intelligence.

"You can breathe underwater during combat. This type of battle is rarer than land combat. The wild duel arena likely arranged this underwater combat to enhance the spectacle."

“Their selection criteria for aquatic beasts referenced the combat prowess you demonstrated in your previous wild duel.”

“Therefore, our strategy is straightforward, maximize your combat strength beyond their benchmark. This will increase your win rate in the next wild duel.”

Lan Zao couldn't help but look worried, “Compared to the last duel, I've only added a piece of iron level leather armor. And this was done publicly, many in the tavern know this information. This also means that the wild duel arena is aware of my improvement in this area. They might raise the standards for selecting aquatic beasts.”

Shuang Tu smiled faintly, producing an alchemy artifact and handing it to Lan Zao: “That's why I also brought you these.”

Though seeing the actual items for the first time, Lan Zao instantly grasped their purpose.

“These are the alchemy projectile cores designed for my Air Cannon?”

Shuang Tu confirmed: "Correct. The training materials I gave you earlier contained extensive details. Refer to those materials for specific usage details."

Lan Zao nodded and accepted the alchemy projectile cores.

They were significantly smaller than standard bullets, roughly one-quarter the size.

Holding them in his hand, Lan Zao noticed their surprising weight.

Picking one up between his fingers for closer inspection, he observed a faint magic glow shimmering across its surface. It was faint and required close scrutiny to perceive.

Shuang Tu had provided him with three types of alchemy projectile cores.

The first was a deep blue electric ball projectile.

It could transform the Air Cannon attack into an electric ball, granting it a lightning attack effect.

The second was a grayish-white range-enhancing core.

Using it would increase the Air Cannon's range by at least double.

The third was an orange-yellow pressure-boosting core.

This core increased the Air Cannon's pressure, halving its volume while maintaining the blast radius but significantly boosting its explosive power.

Lan Zao understood: his upcoming training would revolve around these alchemy projectile cores.

Mastering their usage and raising proficiency to combat-ready levels was essential.

Shuang Tu offered sound advice: "Merely increasing proficiency isn't truly excellent. You must think critically. Consider which alchemy projectile core would be most effective in specific combat scenarios."

“Practicing this foresight repeatedly will grant you quicker reflexes and more precise responses when facing actual combat.”

"In real battle, both sides fight against the clock. Opportunities are fleeting—the slightest misstep can completely alter the outcome."

After relaying these instructions, Shuang Tu hurriedly departed.

He needed to address an unexpected situation promptly.

This unexpected turn of events was the sudden appearance of Da Bei and Zhong Bei who paid a visit to Master Cai Jing. After some deliberation, the latter actually began collaborating with Da Bei and Zhong Bei!

Shuang Tu's primary mission at the moment was to acquire the inheritance of the great druid.

Progress on this task had been going quite smoothly.

Master Cai Jing was actively recruiting potion apprentices, allowing Shuang Tu to easily infiltrate numerous agents into his ranks. Upon receiving frontline intelligence, Shuang Tu immediately grasped Master Cai Jing's true progress.

With just one final hurdle remaining, Master Cai Jing could unearth the legendary master's legacy.

His shift in strategy for excavating the legacy clearly signaled a desperate attempt to buy time and secure the inheritance swiftly.

Shuang Tu remained unflustered, having prepared long ago.

He even gave things a nudge. The rumors circulating on Flower Skirt Island about the great druid's legacy were deliberately spread by him.

Shuang Tu's goal was to intensify the pressure on Master Cai Jing, compelling him to complete the final trial as swiftly as possible.

The greater the pressure on Master Cai Jing, the more he would pursue higher efficiency and shorter timelines. In this process, he would inevitably invest more energy and resources, potentially making mistakes in the rush.

By the time Shuang Tu made his move, Master Cai Jing's strength would likely be significantly depleted.

However, the unexpected appearance of Da Bei and Zhong Bei, coupled with Master Cai Jing's proactive offer of cooperation, resulted in the alliance of two gold level magicians. Shuang Tu quickly realized his rumor scheme had backfired.

If he wanted to claim the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong, he would have to face the combined forces of two gold level and one silver level magicians.

Such power far exceeded his prior preparations.

“It seems I must contact Ba Qi and ask for his aid once more.”

“But I lost contact with him shortly after he departed.”

“To this day, I cannot reach him. What on earth happened to him?”

Ba Qi was praying.

"My Lord, the Charming Blue Goddess, You are so great. When You are angry, the seas tremble. When You are merciful, You grant us illusory care. I look up to Your mighty power, certain of Your salvation. Grant me the status of a worthy believer, so that Your loyal believers may escape their current peril."
