The goddess did not answer.

Ba Qi's expression remained composed.

He was thoroughly familiar with the rituals and protocols of prayer, certain neither had been violated.

After being captured by Zhou Zhang, he swiftly grasped his true predicament and pledged conversion on the spot.

But conversion proved far from simple.

Especially since the Charming Blue believer lay in slumber, rarely responding to her believers.

Ba Qi had naturally prepared a contingency plan.

But escape was no simple feat.

He found himself surrounded by a vast undersea army, tightly encircled and under strict surveillance. Most critically, he had no means to counter the Opportunistic Deception divine spell of the octopusman priestess Zhou Zhang!

“Shuang Tu knows I went to capture the sea serpent. Having lost contact for so long, he must be suspicious.”

“Hope he's smart enough to send reinforcements soon.”

“For now, I can stall a little longer.”

Movement at the altar interrupted his thoughts.

Ba Qi stepped out of the makeshift coral shelter beneath the sea and saw another blood sacrifice beginning.

“Wait, this one's different!” Ba Qi's pupils contracted slightly.

Chapter 665: Path of Blasphemy

The White Hair Trench.

Beyond the Ocean Mother Nest.

A blood sacrifice had been prepared.

Zhang Yu Wan drove a vast herd of sea beasts onto the golden altar. Bound and suppressed by the power of the magic formation, the beasts could not move.

Jiu Hua Bi stood at the foot of the golden altar, bellowing as he called out names one by one.

The believers whose names were called stepped forward in turn, circling the golden altar before kneeling down to begin silent prayers.

Ba Qi keenly observed: these believers had been meticulously selected, almost exclusively naga in race.

This differed from previous prayers.

In earlier rituals, believers gathered around the golden altar without regard to race or order, scattered casually. They prayed in various languages. Some withdrew silently after finishing, their vacated spaces swiftly filled by others.

They did not vie for position; to them, proximity to the altar held no bearing on the depth of their faith.

This unusual ritual drew not only Ba Qi but also Jian Gui, Lady Sea Snake, Cang Xu, and others.

By the time Jiu Hua Bi ceased calling names, over a hundred knelt at the altar's base.

The remaining believers maintained a distance from the golden altar, creating a gap of at least two hundred meters between them.

The blood sacrifice formally commenced.

The presiding officiant of the blood sacrifice was none other than one of the archbishops of the Charming Blue Church, the octopusman Zhou Zhang.

As the ceremony's host, she reaped the greatest benefits. However, being a saint level being with an inherently massive form, her enhancement was not particularly noticeable.

Yet there was no doubt: Zhou Zhang held the entire army in check. She was the core of the entire ritual, the most crucial figure, without question!

The gold level altar and the grand magic formation slowly activated, revealing magic lines and divine script that radiated surging energy and brilliant light.

Upon the golden altar, countless sea beasts were instantly dissolved.

Midway through the blood sacrifice, the accumulated radiance of the golden altar reached its peak, erupting with intense spatial fluctuations.

A strange light enveloped the area within a hundred meters of the altar, and the nagas kneeling on the ground instantly vanished from their spots.

"It's teleportation! Where were they sent?" Ba Qi's eyes widened; he had never witnessed such a phenomenon before.

Jian Gui and the others also witnessed this scene, their faces filled with astonishment and doubt.

The gathered believers, witnessing this, knelt en masse and began fervent prayers. Others turned their gaze toward the captives like Ba Qi and Jian Gui, their faces twisted into cold grins.

They tolerated these heretics precisely because they knew their fate.

If Ba Qi, Jian Gui, and the others converted, they would become one of their own, accepted into the faith. If they refused, they would become sacrifices, offering everything to the gods they worshipped.

The Ocean Mother Nest instantly erupted into chaos.

The saint level sea beast, Snow Pear Octopus, unleashed a bloodbath, feasting voraciously on countless sea monsters.

The naga believers had long since retreated to corners, praying fervently in terror.

The alchemy puppets dispatched by the fishman youngster were already fishing in troubled waters. Taking advantage of the naga and saint level sea beast's inattention, the Rubber Swallowers smoothly captured several naga believers.

Subsequently, these Rubber Swallowers quietly returned to the Deep Sea Monster Fish without alerting the saint level sea beast.

Alchemy Master Zhong Yong stood ready. Upon receiving the captives, he activated the horned cages, imprisoning them all.

These horned cages were Zhong Yong's latest creation, primarily crafted from the spiral horns of narwhals.

As the naga captives were thrown inside, countless spiraling spikes erupted from the cage, piercing deep into their bodies.

The excruciating pain jolted the prisoners awake.

But their attempts to struggle were futile.

The force unleashed by the spikes suppressed their supernatural abilities.

The test results were outstanding, prompting the fishman youngster to praise Zhong Yong repeatedly.

Zhong Yong was also pleased: “Your satisfaction is the greatest reward.”

He was indeed satisfied.

These past days, holed up aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, his workload had been immense, yet he reveled in it, finding endless satisfaction.

For he had encountered countless alchemy blueprints, each ingeniously conceived and uniquely brilliant, leaving him in awe. Furthermore, vast quantities of alchemy materials awaited his refinement.

Wasn't this precisely the happy life he pursued?

The youngster began interrogating these naga believers.

With only himself remaining, he used his Bloodcore to absorb all the naga believers, transforming them into his own bloodline accumulation.

Through interrogation, the fishman youngster learned of Zhou Zhang's intentions.

“She truly does intend to spread her teachings.”

“That's why this teleportation didn't bring these prisoners aboard, they were sent directly into the Ocean Mother Nest.”

“For now, it's about letting Ba Qi, Jian Gui, and the others witness it firsthand, making them feel fear and pressure, forcing them to convert their faith to the Charming Blue Goddess in their despair.”

“Well then, Zhong Yong also needs more time to alchemize more powerful artifacts to capture Ba Qi, Jian Gui, and the others.”

The horned cages Zhong Yong had rushed to produce were mostly iron level, with only one being silver level.

Given his current abilities, he was still unable to forge a gold level one.

When the naga believers teleported back and appeared around the golden altar, the waiting undersea army erupted in boisterous cheers.

Seeing this scene, Ba Qi was stunned once more.

“They've teleported back again!”

“Wait, not all of them returned unscathed. There are some casualties.”

"Huh?"

Ba Qi nearly thought he was seeing things. To his astonishment, he discovered that the auras of the returned believers had universally strengthened. He remembered clearly that before teleportation, some believers had been merely low level transcendents, but now their life levels had directly ascended by one level.

Even those transcendent believers who hadn't gained a level upgrade possessed significantly denser life auras than before.

Upon the altar, Zhou Zhang surveyed the naga believers before turning his gaze to Ba Qi and the others.

The grim, bewildered expressions on Ba Qi's group caused a faint smile to curve Zhou Zhang's lips.

The gold level figures like Zhang Yu Wan and Jiu Hua Bi also watched the prisoners' reactions with keen interest.

The teleported believers merged into the crowd.

Cheers surged anew as the crowd surged forward. The remaining believers rushed to greet them, chanting the divine name and praising their Lord. Hands reached out to touch the naga believers' hands, shoulders, legs, feet, and other body parts. As if touching these believers' bodies could also bestow divine glory.

The naga believers, having endured rigorous trials of faith, now bore solemn expressions, radiating honor and nobility. The trembling, desperate prayers they had offered in the Ocean Mother Nest had been utterly forgotten.

For them, though teleportation to the Mother Nest carried mortal peril, the rewards far outweighed the risks.

Their power had surged dramatically, their renown soared to new heights, and their status ascended by more than one level.

As Ba Qi, Lady Sea Snake, Jian Gui, Cang Xu, and others watched the jubilant crowd, the shock fading from their faces gave way to a heavy, somber atmosphere.

Even the fishman youngster aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, witnessing this scene through the magic projection, couldn't help but utter a heartfelt exclamation: "So many intelligent species gathered together, celebrating the same event. At this moment, racial barriers seemed nonexistent. So this is faith!"

Jian Gui silently retreated to his makeshift shelter, his mind churning with turmoil: “This place likely conceals another Ocean Mother Nest!”

Unlike Ba Qi, Jian Gui, a gold level magician, had spent his days in captivity observing.

Each time a ritual commenced, the magic formation would manifest.

Jian Gui attended each one, meticulously studying the magic runes, divine inscriptions, and the precise layout of the formation.

“Our founder once tasked us with gathering all intelligence concerning Charming Blue Goddess.”

“Though the Charming Blue Goddess slumbers, she can reshape the Mother Nest. By using it as a relay point, she ensures her own safety while deepening devotion and accumulating divine power!”

“Lord Jian Gui.” At that moment, Qi Ling's voice came from outside the shack.

Jian Gui summoned Qi Ling inside.

Qi Ling then relayed all the intelligence gathered during this period to Jian Gui.

Jian Gui confirmed his suspicions, and a wave of profound despair surged from his heart.

“Gather our people. I have words for them.” Jian Gui instructed Qi Ling, who promptly obeyed.

Though they were prisoners, the intensity of their surveillance had drastically diminished over time. By now, Jian Gui, Cang Xu, and the others could move freely within a small area.

Even gathering together wouldn't be stopped. It was like the treatment of prisoners in a jail.

On one hand, the saint level Zhou Zhang possessed formidable power. On the other hand, Jian Gui and others wore magic-suppressing shackles, severely limiting their ability to wield extraordinary abilities.

Soon, the necromancers, including Qing Xin, gathered in Jian Gui's makeshift hut.

Qi Ling sighed, “Who would've thought we'd reunite under these circumstances.”

Xiao Wang Zi snorted coldly, “And it's all your fault!”

Jian Gui replied, "You all witnessed today's ritual. This isn't a simple blood sacrifice. There should be a Mother Nest hidden here..."

Jian Gui voiced his conjecture.

Xiao Wang Zi froze.

Over the past few days, his sense of complacency had grown increasingly severe. He'd even considered putting on a show, feigning deep devotion to buy time until powerful reinforcements from the necromancer faction found them.

Jian Gui and his companions harbored no such illusions.

They hadn't sent out a distress signal. Even if they had, it was doubtful anyone would receive it. The battle on the Underworld River had been pure luck for Jian Gui!

Ku Feng realized: "I see. No wonder Zhou Zhang wanted us to convert. Whether the conversion succeeded or not, she stood to gain either way. The Ocean Mother Nest has been reshaped by the goddess, it's nearly impossible to deceive them with superficial faith."

Qi Ling's face darkened with worry, “Then our only hope now rests on the success of the transformation.”

Though she had been a fishman in life, she had never worshipped the Charming Blue Goddess.

In truth, had she been a devout believer, her soul wouldn't have lingered after death, she would have been immediately guided by the deity.

Jian Gui shook his head, “Faith isn't something you can just talk your way into.”

“You saw it yourselves, those naga believers were handpicked. Their devotion is undoubtedly at least devout level.”

“We need to convert successfully in such a short time, and become at least devout believers, to survive this deadly crisis.”

“The real trouble is, even if we convert out of fear of death, our faith won't run deep.”

His words plunged the necromancers into silence.

Cang Xu asked, “Then what should we do?”

“There's only one way,” Jian Gui's voice echoed in their minds, “We must use the means of a blasphemous ritual to disguise ourselves.”

“Blasphemous ritual?”

“I recall this being part of the advanced soul aspect inheritance.”

Xiao Wang Zi and Qi Ling responded one after the other.

They both possessed mastery of this covert communication method. Cang Xu, however, was encountering it for the first time, able only to listen, not speak.

The next moment, Jian Gui replied, “Given the urgency, a slight breach of principle is permissible.”

“To boost our survival odds, I intend to impart some aspects of the blasphemous ritual to you!”

The words barely left his mouth before Cang Xu received a vast trove of magic knowledge.

This knowledge materialized directly within his mind, as if conjured from thin air.

“The legacy of the blasphemous ritual!”

“The way to fabricate false memories!”

Chapter 666: Seabed Shadow

The instant he received the legacy of the blasphemous ritual, Cang Xu felt an overwhelming surge of gratitude toward Jian Gui.

As a descendant of Founder You Shen, Jian Gui demonstrated the true responsibility of the undead orthodoxy at a critical moment. He had risked violating principles to secretly disseminate the legacy of the advanced soul aspect, solely to increase the survival odds of the necromancers.

Beyond gratitude, he felt profound astonishment.

Cang Xu never imagined he would acquire the blasphemous ritual's legacy under such circumstances!

According to his prior assumptions: the method for crafting soul crystals resided within the intermediate soul aspect inheritance, while the blasphemous priest's legacy belonged to the advanced soul aspect inheritance.

Given the principles Jian Gui had previously demonstrated, Cang Xu would first need to demonstrate sufficient talent to qualify for the intermediate soul aspect inheritance. Then, he would have to complete certain tasks to prove his loyalty. Only then could he be accepted by the orthodox necromancers. Of course, this process would inevitably involve extensive covert investigations.

Cang Xu hadn't even met the first requirement yet.

Even if he had, this would undoubtedly be a long-term, large-scale endeavor. It would consume immense manpower, resources, and time from Cang Xu.

Beyond that lay even greater risks.

For Cang Xu could never join the undead orthodoxy under his true identity. The secrets and interests of the Mystifying Monster Island far outweighed any intermediate or advanced soul aspect inheritance.

They were far more critical. Critical enough to threaten Cang Xu's very life.

Under these circumstances, Cang Xu unexpectedly obtained a blasphemous priest's legacy, even if only a fragment of it, which was enough to fill him with astonishment.

After imparting the inherited knowledge to everyone, Jian Gui dissolved the gathering.

Cang Xu returned to his own dwelling.

It was a makeshift shack, similar to Jian Gui's abode, hastily constructed from coral.

Cang Xu sighed deeply, "Fate is truly unpredictable. I failed to secure the intermediate soul aspect inheritance yet unexpectedly gained a portion of the advanced knowledge ahead of schedule. But..."

His expression darkened slightly.

The earlier gratitude and excitement faded significantly.

The pressure of survival now loomed heavily.

Today, Cang Xu witnessed a unique blood sacrifice. At the necromancers' small gathering, he had also confirmed intelligence, learning part of the truth concerning the Ocean Mother Nest.

Cang Xu had no confidence in his own conversion.

“For now, my greatest hope for survival lies in fabricating memories, becoming a blasphemous priest to deceive and manipulate Charming Blue Goddess, Zhou Zhang, and the others.”

“But the crucial question remains - what is Jian Gui's true intention?”

Having served as a noble steward for many years, Cang Xu had witnessed countless schemes and plots. When contemplating matters, he instinctively interpreted others' motives with the utmost suspicion.

The previous battle at the Underworld River had also left an indelible impression on him.

In that conflict, Jian Gui had manipulated Cang Xu, generously lending him the supplementary volume of the spellbook. Then, using that duplicate, he forged his own life essence. Unbeknownst to Cang Xu, he disguised Cang Xu as himself, causing the gold level assassin Wei Ying to mistake Cang Xu for Jian Gui. Wei Ying struck to kill Cang Xu. Had the dragonman youngster not provided Cang Xu with a decoy puppet beforehand, Cang Xu would have died.

Not only Cang Xu, but in subsequent battles, Xiao Wang Zi was also used by Jian Gui as bait, luring Wei Ying out once more.

This time, however, Jian Gui acted far more discreetly, leaving Xiao Wang Zi oblivious afterward.

Yet Cang Xu etched this experience and its lessons deep into his heart.

Cang Xu pondered, his doubts growing.

"When I previously sought the intermediate inheritance from Jian Gui, he upheld his principles, revealing a fierce determination to defend them. Why would he suddenly abandon those principles, even proactively imparting advanced inheritance to outsiders?"

“When facing existential threats, Jian Gui will resort to any means to preserve himself, treating those around him as pawns and sacrificial offerings.”

“Under these circumstances, his voluntary imparting of the blasphemous ritual's secrets is highly suspicious!”

Jian Gui's inconsistent behavior defied logic.

Every believer harbored profound loathing for the blasphemous priests. This was evident the day Zhou Zhang publicly interrogated Jian Gui at the golden altar.

Such hatred ran deep in their very bones.

For Jian Gui to now willingly impart knowledge of the blasphemous ritual, if Zhou Zhang and the others discovered this, his fate would be grim. He might even be executed on the spot!

“Would he risk such consequences merely to increase others' chances of survival?” Cang Xu shook his head silently.

That's clearly impossible.

Cang Xu began to seriously doubt the reliability of this particular segment of the inherited knowledge in his mind.

Carrying this doubt, he sat cross-legged on the sand, eyes slightly closed, and began to silently delve into the matter.

Not long after, he slowly opened his eyes. Though his doubts remained unresolved, he couldn't hide his delight.

It was the joy of discovering true knowledge.

“So, the core of the blasphemous priest still revolves around soul transformation.”

The reason blasphemous priests could conceal themselves from the gods lay in a breakthrough in a crucial technique: memory alteration.

Necromancers forged their own memories to craft the image of devout believers.

They convinced even themselves, thereby deceiving the gods as well. Mistaking necromancers for their most pious or fervent believers, the gods often bestowed blessings without restraint.

The essence of memory alteration was, in truth, a technique for reshaping souls.

“The foundational legacy of the necromancer soul aspect includes the Soul Summoning Spell, capable of transforming recently departed souls present at the scene into ghosts.”

"Within the elementary soul aspect inheritance lies the technique to reshape these spirits, transforming them into myriad forms. Beyond this, there are also the Burden Alleviation Spell and Karma Removal Spell. These two spells form the core of the elementary inheritance, targeting the necromancer's own soul."

"The intermediate soul aspect inheritance contains methods for crafting soul crystals. This unique artificial resource is clearly a major breakthrough achieved through deep research into souls. Soul crystals are pure, memory-less essences of souls. Reaching this stage indicates that the ancient necromancers already possessed methods to completely erase memories."

"In the advanced inheritance, the necromancers' ability to modify their own souls becomes even more profound. Magicians can perform highly intricate surgical procedures on their own souls. They can directly fabricate memories and imbue them into themselves. This constitutes the foundational technology for blaspheming the gods."

"So then, the means by which deities discern their believers lies within the soul. Deities should be able to peer into a believer's memories, using them as proof. Necromancers, the magicians, forge memories, essentially tampering with the soul itself to alter this proof, thereby deceiving the gods. No wonder... Necromancers have become the scourge of all, targeted by nearly every faction!"

The Soul Summoning Spell of the foundational lineage, the elementary inheritance's ghost modification spells, the Burden Alleviation Spell and Karma Removal Spell, the soul-crystal technology within the intermediate inheritance, and the memory-forgery techniques of the advanced inheritance...

At this point, Cang Xu had fully grasped the core framework of the soul aspect inheritance!

“This must be the sequence and developmental trajectory of Founder You Shen's soul research.”

“What earth-shattering ingenuity this represents.”

Cang Xu glimpsed the essence through a narrow window, profoundly sensing the Founder You Shen's extraordinary genius in that moment. It made him feel like an ant gazing up at a giant.

Cang Xu tentatively used his spirit to secretly modify his own soul.

"Just as Jian Gui said before, it can indeed be modified covertly. In this regard, the undead still hold an advantage as a newly emerging faction."

Cang Xu's brow furrowed slightly, for he simultaneously sensed constraints and restraints.

Evidently, while the technique for fabricating memories wasn't entirely blocked, it remained severely impacted by the external restriction.

“However, directly altering one's own soul... even if forged memories are acquired, there remains a significant drawback.”

The inheritance provided by Jian Gui explained this drawback quite clearly.

That drawback was memory confusion.

If the fabricated memories were too realistic, they could easily cause the necromancer to develop a distorted self-perception. This could lead the necromancer to lose recognition of their true self, or even come to fully believe the false memories, rejecting their authentic identity.

To address this flaw, Founder You Shen developed the Memory Switching Spell.

The Memory Switching Spell was also one of the most crucial components within the advanced inheritance knowledge obtained by Cang Xu.

When employing the Memory Switching Spell, the necromancer could either block or activate the fabricated memories.

Upon activating the fabricated memories, the necromancer would genuinely believe themselves to be the fabricated persona, thereby ensuring the purity of their faith. This increased the success rate of deceiving the gods.

Upon deactivating the fabricated memories, the necromancer immediately recalls his true identity, guaranteeing accurate self-awareness.

To Cang Xu's regret, the inheritance provided by Jian Gui did not include the memory crystal making device.

Cang Xu reasoned: ‘Memory alteration can be performed directly on oneself or applied to other targets.’" The memory crystal making device is the tool for this. Using it makes memory alteration more convenient, precise, and efficient."

"It's like when humans first ate with their hands. After inventing cutlery, handling foods like steak became far more convenient and efficient."

“So, back to that crucial question!”

“What exactly is the hidden danger within this inheritance? What trick is Jian Gui employing?"

Cang Xu was certain Jian Gui had his own agenda.

The situation was dire.

Zhou Zhang's purpose was obvious, and Cang Xu lacked confidence in his ability to convert successfully.

Though he had obtained the advanced inheritance, Cang Xu dared not blindly alter his own memories. Until he uncovered Jian Gui's hidden tricks, the survival odds he boosted through cultivation weren't his own—they belonged to Jian Gui.

“Another moment of life and death...”

Cang Xu sighed.

His experiences on Mystifying Monster Island had taught him to endure such immense survival pressure while maintaining inner calm.

But this calm couldn't alter reality, couldn't break through this deadlock.

“What other options do I have to cling to in hope of rescue?”

“Wait for the Twelve Saints like Doctor Sui Shi to come to my aid?”

“Ba Qi and Lady Sea Snake are members of the Fire Beard Pirates. Perhaps they have reinforcements?”

“No, none of these are reliable...”

Cang Xu pondered long and hard, arriving at only one possibility.

The secret of Mystifying Monster Island!

It involved the divine artifact, especially the Mermaid Fairy Tale ,the very core artifact of the Charming Blue Goddess. If this secret were revealed, Zhou Zhang would surely be tempted, granting Cang Xu a temporary reprieve.

“Besides that, there's... the Bloodcore!”

Once the secret of the Bloodcore was exposed, the entire world would be tempted. Cang Xu was utterly certain of this.

“I... I could use these secrets to bargain for my life...”

The moment this thought surfaced, Cang Xu immediately stifled it.

Magic flames bloomed in the seawater, mingling with the golden altar's light. Through the crude shack, it illuminated Cang Xu's back.

His silhouette swayed left and right on the sandy seabed, dancing with the flickering magic flames.

Amidst the swaying, the thought Cang Xu had just suppressed surfaced once more.

“Use these secrets to buy myself a chance at survival?”

He crushed the notion again.

Yet swiftly, the same idea stirred to life, like his own shadow cast upon the sand before him.

Crush, emerge, crush, emerge...

After this repeated cycle, the thought grew stronger than before, suddenly filling his mind until it consumed his thoughts entirely!

At the same moment, the image of the dragonman youngster appeared in his mind.

In an instant, the previous thought vanished without a trace.

But the next instant, the thought sprouted again and began to rapidly grow.

It was becoming impossible to suppress.

Chapter 667: Commander, You Saved Me Again

Cang Xu's heart was in turmoil.

“I do not fear death, I do not fear...” At this moment, he desperately tried to convince himself of this truth.

He told himself: “On Mystifying Monster Island, in that cave, I was even willing to sacrifice myself, to offer myself as food...”

A scene from the past flashed before his eyes.

Cang Xu addressed the youngster: "Eat this food, and you'll regain your strength. Not only will your chances of survival increase, but you'll also protect your fiancée. Perhaps it was because you ate an extra piece of meat that she managed to survive the breakout attempt tomorrow morning."

The youngster, Zhen Jin, erupted in fury: “Are you forcing me?!”

Back then, Cang Xu had smiled. "Yes, I am forcing you. But please forgive me, Lord Zhen Jin. I am about to die. A dead man fears no worldly authority. I wish to fulfill my dying wish. For this, my life is but a bargaining chip."

Now, Cang Xu wanted to laugh too, but he couldn't.

Because his own words of persuasion had failed.

At this moment, he suddenly realized, he feared death!

Back on Mystifying Monster Island, he had truly been cornered, with no choice but to entrust his final wish. But now it was different. Deep down, Cang Xu knew he still had hope. This wasn't a complete dead end!

Why not take a chance? If he sold the secret, he was almost certain he could survive!

But without a doubt, this was—betrayal!

Guilt surged like flames within him, burning fiercely, bringing agonizing torment.

“Am I betraying the commander?”

“Betraying someone like him?”

In Cang Xu's mind, the sincere and resolute declaration of the young Zhen Jin echoed—."

"I once vowed to do everything in my power to save everyone, to abandon no one.. This is my promise, and my duty.” Zhen Jin had said this to Zi Di and Cang Xu.

“Lord, your noble character makes even the brightest stars in the night sky pale in comparison!” Cang Xu exclaimed from the bottom of his heart, “You are the very epitome of a templar knight.”

The memory surfaced: the speech before the final battle at the underground factory, the alchemy hub.

“Even my own appearance looks like someone else, this should have been caused by War Merchant’s modulations and transformations.”

“In fact, as you can see, I am a beastified person, at most I could only be considered a comparatively special beastified person.

What a stirring speech!

At that moment, Cang Xu admired the youngster's courage immensely.

“We were betrayed by a close relative.” The youngster looked at Cang Xu.

The old scholar narrowed his eyes, shaken by these words!

“We seem to have lost our direction in life, but as a matter of fact, our hearts understand how we want to move forward.”

“You were always more suited to knighthood and nobility than Zhen Jin. I've always believed that.” Cang Xu spoke softly then, wiping his lenses to mask his surging emotions.

Victory was theirs. They had eliminated Jia Sha and seized the divine artifacts.

Compared to the templar knight Zhen Jin, the beastified youngster earned Cang Xu's deeper admiration and approval.

Cang Xu recalled how, after escaping the Mystifying Monster Island, he had averted total annihilation through necromancy magic, only to face growing alienation and suspicion from his companions. It was the dragonman youngster who publicly declared his loyalty, tirelessly mending the rifts between Cang Xu and the others.

And in the battle at the Underworld River, it was the dragonman youngster who entrusted Cang Xu with a substitute puppet, saving his life!

“It was the substitute puppet that revived me.”

“No, it was the commander who saved my life!”

Amidst the lingering dread, a wave of warmth surged through Cang Xu once more.

Now, as Cang Xu revisited this memory, the original intensity of that emotion had faded considerably. Yet it still stirred his heart.

“The commander saved me time and again. Without him, I never would have escaped Mystifying Monster Island or returned from the Underworld River.”

“He never wronged me. And now I intend to betray him?”

“To expose secrets that would bring him enemies of the saint level?”

“Am I to... repay kindness with betrayal?”

The flames of guilt erupted from his heart, spreading rapidly as if consuming his entire being.

Though a necromancer who no longer needed to breathe, Cang Xu felt the illusion of suffocation.

He recalled the moment he learned the secret of the Bloodcore.

It had been utterly staggering.

Cang Xu was stunned not only by the Bloodcore's ingenuity, but also by the dragonman youngster's magnanimity and vision!

“Without his sharing, I would never have known the Bloodcore's secrets.”

“And now I must use this very secret to bargain for my own survival?”

Cang Xu's head drooped lower and lower, his back growing increasingly hunched. It was as if an invisible mountain weighed upon his shoulders.

Overwhelming guilt transformed into a burden life could not bear.

His hands braced against the sand and stones of the seabed, fingers splayed wide, clawing viciously into the mud.

His eyes had clearly lost their focus, unable to see the scene before him, lost only in his own world.

“But I don't want to die...”

“I truly don't want to die!”

“Dying here now, I refuse to accept it!”

“It's too unfair...”

"If I hadn't known about the Bloodcore, it would have been one thing, but now I have new hope. Why must I lose my life here?"

"I still had hope! I had a future! A bright future!"

“Truthfully, I don't need to reveal the Bloodcore. Just exposing the secret of the Mermaid Fairy Tale would make Zhou Zhang spare me for now.”

“Perhaps I could buy time, then later join forces with the commander and the others to defeat Zhou Zhang?”

“Even if... even if Zhou Zhang wins, perhaps the Bloodcore won't be exposed. I could secretly obtain it and gain a new life!”

Cang Xu's expression twisted into a grimace, his gaze unfocused. His mouth gaped open as he gasped for breath unconsciously.

“Wait... there's still the magic contract.”

To bind themselves, the survivors had once purchased magic contracts, pledging mutual secrecy.

“If exposed, the contract's power would kill me!”

“But there's still room to maneuver.”

"That contract was low level. Zhou Zhang is saint level! And she's the archbishop of Charming Blue Goddess, the deity most skilled in deception and disguise. Deceiving the magic contract should be no problem..."

“Wait, what am I even thinking!?”

“Do I really want to do this?”

“Become a shameful traitor and betray the commander?”

Cang Xu's thoughts wrestled fiercely within him.

Amidst his struggle and hesitation, it felt as though he heard the dragonman youngster's speech once more.

It was during the battle of Snake Rat Island, when they lay defeated beneath the Domain of the Light of Contrition, their fighting spirit shattered.

“We are guilty!”

But it is precisely because of our sins that we repent and weep sorrowful tears. We can then understand more the importance of pursuing justice, no?

“We should be guilty!”

“Who hasn’t done wrong? Who can guarantee that throughout their life, they never made erred?”

“Survival, survival!”

“Only by continuing to live can we have the chance to admit our mistakes, to redeem ourselves, to feel remorse, and to set back onto the path of righteousness.”

The dragonman youngster's words had rallied his comrades and allies, marking the pivotal turning point of that battle.

“Yes, yes.”

“I am guilty, deeply guilty!”

“But I must live on, I must survive...”

“For survival, I must bear this guilt!”

“Thank you, Commander. You saved me many times, but now I must betray you.”

“Forgive me, forgive me this once.”

“As you said, I do this for survival!”

“I will repent. I swear I will.”

Cang Xu made his final decision within his heart.

He slumped completely, his cross-legged posture resembling a kneeling stance.

Strangely, once the decision was made, his chaotic, agitated state began to settle.

“Since the decision is made, it's time to act.” Cang Xu told himself.

His gaze grew resolute, his vision sharpening.

Then.

He saw the mark etched into the seabed gravel.

The emblem of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

In that instant, time seemed to freeze for Cang Xu. He froze in place, motionless, like a stone statue.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' mark, or more accurately, a secret signal.

It was designed by Tripleblade.

He had once been a mercenary leader, and its members often used secret signals in various situations. For instance, scouts would leave them along reconnaissance routes as directional markers. Or they'd mark city street corners to indicate target buildings for missions.

During his time on Two Eyes Island, Xu Ma had left such codes, successfully aiding other mercenaries in accurately assessing the situation.

Cang Xu had no idea how long he stood there before his hand moved instinctively, wiping away the code etched into the seabed sand.

The code itself was formed by small, slightly raised grains of sand, distinct from the surrounding sandy terrain.

With a light sweep, Cang Xu destroyed the grains forming the mark.

Yet the next moment, the grains coalesced once more, recreating the original signal.

Cang Xu's lips twitched, his expression growing intensely complex and peculiar.

Then the markings shifted again, the sand grains forming a brief message - I'm nearby.

Without reason, Cang Xu knew this “I” referred to the commander!

His Adam's apple bobbed as overwhelming emotion threatened to breach his defenses, making him want to shout - even cry out.

"Commander…"

“You've saved me once more.”

Cang Xu sighed deeply within his heart.

Inside the cockpit of the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the fishman youngster watched the magic projection silently, his expression unreadable.

The substance forming the pebbles was, of course, not actual sand, but precision eel drillers.

These delicate, miniature alchemy automatons could bore through the walls of the Ocean Mother Nest, creating countless minute cavities.

Through these intricate networks of tiny cavities, the dual-eared detectors could relay sonar feedback, revealing the world beyond the Mother Nest.

After testing the conditions of faith, the fishman youngster's plan to teleport his disguised self out had failed. After deep contemplation, he resolved to take a risk and conduct a test.

The test's purpose? To determine whether his Deceptive Disguise spell would be exposed by Zhou Zhang!

Zhou Zhang was a saint level magician and divine spell wielder, as well as one of the archbishops of the Charming Blue Goddess. The survivors had never faced an opponent of this caliber. She was highly likely to see through the Deceptive Disguise spell, being an expert in such matters. Moreover, the Deceptive Disguise Formation was merely a design by the War Merchant, failing to fully harness the potential of pearl bubble's alchemy technology.

The probe carried significant risk. If exposed, Zhou Zhang could trace the deception back to them and come knocking.

Yet the fishman youngster understood this reconnaissance was essential!

For once the Mother Nest broke, the Deep Sea Monster Fish, still cloaked by the Deceptive Disguise spell, would still face Zhou Zhang. Rather than react passively then, it was better to gather intelligence beforehand, allowing the fishman youngster to prepare for escape.

Naturally, the fishman youngster wouldn't risk himself. After brief consideration, he decided the precision eel drillers were the optimal choice.

Thus, several drillers eventually bored their way out of the Ocean Mother Nest into the outside world.

The Mother Nest did not shatter from these small holes; in fact, they were rapidly sealing themselves. The Mother Nest possessed self-repair capabilities.

The drillers were cloaked in the Deceptive Disguise spell, designed by the fishman youngster to blend as seabed gravel.

They slipped beneath the feet of countless believers, even deliberately circling several times right under Zhou Zhang's nose.

No issues arose.

The test results showed that the Deceptive Disguise spell, utilizing god level materials, surpassed Zhou Zhang, a saint.

The fishman youngster then dispatched these drillers to locate Cang Xu's residence.

Soon after, he communicated with Cang Xu in advance by manipulating the drillers to form written characters.

This step, of course, carried a greater risk of exposure.

But the fishman youngster had scouted beforehand and found no overly sophisticated surveillance methods here.

On one hand, Zhou Zhang likely relied on her immense power and legions of believers, assuming these prisoners, heavily restrained, couldn't cause any trouble.

On the other hand, the presence of the golden altar, covered by an extremely vast hybrid formation of magic and divine script, played a role. Deploying any magic or divine detection methods here could interfere with the grand formation. Put another way, each time the blood sacrifice was held, the immense power unleashed by the formation would likely neutralize most reconnaissance measures.

Chapter 668: Jian Gui’s Self Rescue Plan

The next day, Jian Gui once again summoned the necromancers.

While engaging in superficial conversation, he secretly inquired about the progress of fabricating their memories.

Xiao Wang Zi muttered under his breath, “This is way too difficult! I worked all night and didn't even reach 5%."

Qi Ling echoed, “Given this level of difficulty, it truly lives up to being an advanced necromancer bloodline inheritance.”

If both magicians struggled, Ku Feng's progress was even lower, a mere 2%.

Yet someone lagged behind him: Cang Xu.

Cang Xu suspected Jian Gui had tampered with the inheritance content, so he didn't rush his practice but merely pondered it.

Since connecting with the fishman youngster, Cang Xu's mindset had undergone significant changes.

Jian Gui reassured everyone: “Forging memories is like performing surgery on your own soul. It requires extreme precision to avoid self-harm, or else it easily leads to confusion in self-awareness.”

"Even for me, having practiced this inheritance for years, creating a completely fabricated memory alone takes at least a week. Of course, this doesn't mean seven days and nights without sleep, but rather with normal rest periods."

"Then what should we do? My greatest fear is that another blood sacrifice will begin before we successfully fabricate our memories," Xiao Wang Zi expressed his deep concern.

Qi Ling analyzed, “While blood sacrifices occur frequently, the special ones that transmit believers inside aren't like that. They happen at intervals.”

Xiao Wang Zi perked up at this and hurriedly asked how long the interval was.

Qi Ling answered him.

“At most two or three days. That's nowhere near enough!” Xiao Wang Zi clutched his large skull, looking utterly helpless.

Jian Gui chuckled softly, “It's far too early to despair.”

"Forging memories on our own would be painstakingly slow. But don't worry, I have specialized tools. This alchemy apparatus not only speeds up memory fabrication but also creates memory crystals. With memory crystals, we can directly overwrite our original memories instead of tampering with our souls."

"Among the techniques I previously taught you is the Memory Switching Spell. Combined with this spell, we can relatively freely toggle between the ‘disguised’ and ‘true self’ states. This prevents prolonged disguise from turning us into someone else entirely, losing our true selves."

Ku Feng frowned, quietly objecting: “But how can we use the alchemy device in our current state?”

Qi Ling added: "The reason we can gather freely now is likely because Zhou Zhang and the others are deliberately letting us be. They want us to discuss endlessly without results, plunging us all into despair to increase the likelihood of conversion."

For this challenge, Jian Gui had a solution prepared.

He didn't beat around the bush, stating directly, “In our current predicament, it's impossible to openly use this alchemy device.”

“But once we reach the Ocean Mother Nest, things will be different.”

"At that point, isolated from the outside world, I can break free from the restraints binding me, gaining some freedom of movement. I will swiftly remove the restraints binding you. With that, we can operate the memory crystal making device."

Cang Xu heard these words and doubt immediately took root in his heart.

He himself lacked the ability for such covert communication, but fortunately, the next moment, Xiao Wang Zi voiced the concern weighing on his mind: “Doesn't the Ocean Mother Nest house a saint level sea beast? If we do this, we'll likely be devoured by the saint level sea beast!"

Jian Gui looked at him: “A low-intelligence saint level sea beast is still better than facing the saint level Zhou Zhang.”

“In truth, the enemy we should fear most is Charming Blue Goddess!”

“Because the Ocean Mother Nest was remodeled by her.”

"However, this deity fell into slumber long ago. Even if we utter her divine name, it won't trigger any response."

"This Ocean Mother Nest was personally transformed by her. That saint level sea beast serves as her guardian beast, aiding her in guarding the Ocean Mother Nest while assisting in the completion of the blood sacrifice."

"If she is conscious, any slightest change within the Ocean Mother Nest will be instantly perceived."

"Facing any other deity, we will be doomed. But with the slumbering Charming Blue Goddess, we still hold a sliver of hope!"

Cang Xu remained silent, yet a realization dawned upon him as he listened.

His thoughts drifted back to Weeping Pine Island, where he had first encountered Jian Gui and the others.

On Weeping Pine Island, he attended his first gathering of the undead. He clearly remembered the terms of the deal proposed by Jian Gui.

One task was to gather intelligence on the various aspects of the Charming Blue Goddess, while the other was to find new ways to contact the War Merchant.

The survivors possessed the capability to accomplish both missions. This left a profound impression on Cang Xu.

"Jian Gui wants to gather intelligence on the Charming Blue Goddess. This clearly isn't his own initiative. Behind him lies the orthodox undead faction, and likely even the will of You Shen. Those orthodox necromancers are almost all blasphemous priests. These blasphemous priests have probably already set their sights on the Charming Blue Goddess. They likely intend to strike against her."

"Charming Blue Goddess has slumbered for years due to severe wounds. To the blasphemous priests, she is a perfect target."

"As a member of the undead orthodoxy, Jian Gui must have access to some hidden intelligence. Therefore, he now possesses profound knowledge about the Ocean Mother Nest and this blood sacrifice."

Jian Gui began to reveal his true plan: "Charming Blue Goddess is now in a state of deep slumber. The Ocean Mother Nest, personally modified by her, is guarded by the saint beast. Zhou Zhang and her believers are conducting this grand blood sacrifice here precisely because of the Mother Nest's unique properties. The offerings from the blood sacrifice are absorbed and processed by the Mother Nest with near-perfect efficiency. The vast majority of this nourishment is channeled directly to the Charming Blue Goddess's true body."

“Most of the harvest is absorbed by her, while a small portion is retained to empower the saint beast, Zhou Zhang, and other devout believers.”

“Even this minuscule fraction of the harvest represents an unimaginable boost for most transcendents.”

"If we act alone, forging memories is nearly impossible. You've all experienced its difficulty firsthand."

"Therefore, to save ourselves this time, we must cooperate fully! Once we teleport into the Ocean Mother Nest, we'll have a brief window for action."

Ku Feng realized with a start: “You mean when the saint level sea beast hunts those sacrificial sea beasts?”

“Correct.” Jian Gui nodded slightly.

This intelligence had been deduced through his own reasoning and later corroborated by information gathered by Qi Ling. Jian Gui could even roughly estimate the exact duration of this window.

He continued, “Based on my calculations, we're still slightly short on time to complete a full fabricated memory.”

“Therefore, we must delay the saint level sea beast's advance.”

“Fortunately, by that time, we will have partially restored our combat strength. We can use our summoned creatures to wage a war of attrition, buying crucial time for you.”

“At the final moment, even I, as a gold level being, can barely hold my own against the saint level sea beast.”

"To conserve time as much as possible, we must consult and collaborate to fabricate a false memory. Each of us completes a segment, and by layering our contributions, we can dramatically accelerate the overall progress. I will personally oversee the final consolidation and refinement."

Xiao Wang Zi widened his eyes: "So we'll all share one fabricated memory? Is that truly feasible?"

“Rest assured,” Jian Gui affirmed. "As an orthodox undead, I've long studied the methods our masters used to defy the gods. Even a normal deity couldn't possibly inspect every believer's entire memory. Of course, during a major festival, multiple believers simultaneously possessing identical memories remains a significant flaw."

"But Charming Blue Goddess is slumbering. A sleeping deity cannot perceive this flaw. As I mentioned before, her slumber is our greatest opportunity."

"Therefore, as long as a single false memory covers all of us, we can pass the test of faith."

At this point, Jian Gui's tone grew wistful: “Among all deities, the Charming Blue Goddess excels most in deception and disguise. Now it seems fate is truly wondrous and ironic. The Charming Blue Goddess, master of deception and disguise, will be deceived and outwitted by us!"

These words ignited the necromancers' fighting spirit.

Ku Feng chuckled, his voice brimming with anticipation: "Deceiving a deity skilled in deception and disguise? How thrilling!"

Jian Gui sighed, scanning everyone present: "The situation now should be quite clear to all. In short, unless we receive reinforcements or some extraordinary, unforeseen event occurs, this is our only chance to survive. I urge everyone to seize it with all their might!"

The necromancers obeyed with solemn resolve.

Cang Xu was no exception.

His expression was unwavering, yet his thoughts churned beneath the surface.

Though this gathering yielded no new inheritance content, it proved immensely valuable nonetheless.

For he had finally grasped why Jian Gui had defied his principles, voluntarily imparting fragments of the advanced undead inheritance.

Cang Xu had previously worried that Jian Gui might tamper with or manipulate this inherited knowledge. Now, however, that possibility seemed unlikely.

For he needed the necromancers to collaborate seamlessly in crafting a complete fabricated memory. If he were to tamper with it, Jian Gui would not only betray others but also himself.

Yet regarding Jian Gui's other words, Cang Xu remained deeply suspicious.

“When time is critical, Jian Gui would likely sacrifice others without hesitation!”

“He'll undoubtedly use the first fabricated memory he creates on himself!”

"Can the same fabricated memory truly aid others? Can this be a deliberate lie spun by Jian Gui? Does he intend to use this deception to make everyone serve him, ultimately benefiting only himself?"

"Sharing a single fabricated memory among so many carries risks. If I were Jian Gui, the safest and most secure path to survival would be abandoning and sacrificing others to preserve only myself!"

After the battle at the Underworld River, Cang Xu understood Jian Gui's true nature all too well.

This Jian Gui would resort to any means to ensure his own survival.

“But the others remain completely unaware.”

It had to be said, Jian Gui's methods were highly covert. His usual demeanor was impeccable, greatly benefiting Xiao Wang Zi, Qi Ling, and Ku Feng.

These three, gathered around Jian Gui, still harbored not a shred of suspicion toward him.

“What a bunch of naive fools! Hard to believe they're all necromancers,” Cang Xu sighed.

Under these circumstances, he couldn't unite the others to guard against Jian Gui.

“Had it not been for the battle on the Underworld River, I wouldn't have understood Jian Gui's true nature so thoroughly.”

“Perhaps there's another reason. That is, Jian Gui and I share many similarities. We're both cold-hearted, capable of calmly weighing pros and cons!" Cang Xu couldn't help but recall his recent struggle, the fierce internal conflict that ultimately led him to decide to sell the deepest secret to secure his own survival.

“If the commander hadn't appeared in time...”

A wave of lingering fear and bitterness washed over Cang Xu once more.

Yet his subsequent conversation with the fishman youngster would radically shift his perspective.

Cang Xu realized this peril concealed an immense opportunity!

Thus, after Jian Gui dismissed the others, Cang Xu returned to its shack.

“Lord Jian Gui, I've been thinking. If we all master the operation of the memory crystal making device, it would greatly benefit our current plan!” Cang Xu declared.

Jian Gui looked at Cang Xu with surprise and suspicion.

The method Cang Xu was using to communicate with him now was none other than the secret method they had used before!

Chapter 669: Cang Xu vs Jian Gui

The gaze hidden beneath Jian Gui's hood flickered faintly.

He felt a twinge of unease and doubt.

This covert method of communication wasn't simple.

It could only be established because Jian Gui shared a secret link with others.

Jian Gui knew Cang Xu could use this covert method, because back on the ghost ship in the Underworld River, Cang Xu had already been tampered with!

“The question is, has Cang Xu truly discovered this?”

With this doubt, Jian Gui probed, "Xu Jia, it seems your bloodline mutation is heading in a positive direction. It likely granted you enhanced necromancy aptitude, enabling you to master this covert communication method."

Cang Xu smiled: "I don't know why, but perhaps it's the initial fruit of my bloodline mutation. Anyway, after listening to your communications for so long, I naturally picked it up. It's truly remarkable!"

Jian Gui chuckled: “Had you communicated this way during the gathering just now; it would have brought others quite the surprise.” (Since you've mastered this method of communication, why didn't you interact before? Hiding your abilities like this—your motives aren't pure!)

Cang Xu gave a wry smile: "They've asked every question there is. There's nothing left for me to inquire about. My Lord, I sought you out privately because something occurred to me.”

“Oh? What is it?”

“As you said, the Ocean Mother Nest will be our final chance. There, we'll face a saint level sea beast. At that point, we silver levels won't stand a chance against it. The Ocean Mother Nest is a closed environment; we can't create distance to stall the saint level sea beast.” Therefore, at the critical moment, we must still rely on your power. But you cannot simultaneously fight and operate the memory crystal making device. At that time, only we can perform the operation!"

At this point, Cang Xu abruptly fell silent.

Yet his meaning was made abundantly clear - he desired this specific knowledge.

Jian Gui sank into silence, seemingly unsettled by Cang Xu's sudden offensive.

Cang Xu continued, "Lord Jian Gui, what do you think of my proposal? Only by harnessing the strength of all of us can we truly cooperate, build mutual trust, and ultimately escape together." (I now suspect you intend to use us as cannon fodder! Only by allowing us to operate the alchemy apparatus can you demonstrate sincerity in cooperation. Your refusal would only confirm my current suspicion.)

After a long silence, Jian Gui laughed heartily: “Xu Jia, just as I sensed when we first met, you truly are a clever one!”

"Indeed, your suggestion is excellent! I admit my planning was somewhat lacking. Thank you for pointing out my oversight."

"I shall impart this knowledge to you immediately. This will greatly increase our chances of success. This time, if we cooperate wholeheartedly, we will surely weather this storm together!" (You actually dare to threaten me? Impressive! But I've agreed. We truly need close coordination and mutual trust to work together effectively; only then do we have a chance of escape. If you want to live, you must align yourself with me and genuinely cooperate. That's your only chance!)

Their conversation was truly fascinating.

On the surface, they exchanged pleasantries, revealing their fears and offering suggestions on how to convert. This was the first layer of deception, deliberately spoken for Zhou Zhang and the others to hear.

In reality, they communicated covertly through secret means.

But the hidden meaning behind this exchange was their true core message!

Next, Jian Gui taught the method to use the memory crystal making device to Cang Xu.

"You're the first among them to receive this inheritance knowledge. Once you see it, you'll find the operation simple and remarkably convenient. But without the crucial alchemy apparatus, this knowledge is utterly useless."

"The most important thing for us now is to work together seamlessly to forge a complete memory. Do you understand?" Jian Gui asked.

Having received his reward, Cang Xu appeared exceedingly compliant: “I understand, Lord. I'll return immediately and devote myself to altering my faith!”

He declared this publicly.

Jian Gui naturally understood Cang Xu's hidden meaning. He nodded: “Go. Go.”

Cang Xu left his shack, and Jian Gui's gaze darkened like water.

He gritted his teeth inwardly: "Xu Jia, well done. You actually dare to blackmail me? Hah, you've got some nerve. Do you realize your life hangs on my whim? No, you don't. If you did, how could you dare treat me this way?"

Jian Gui sneered coldly before refocusing his mind to forge memories.

His escape depended on this task; he dared not be careless or negligent.

Cang Xu returned to his shack, his expression darkening rapidly.

He clenched his fists and gritted his teeth, “If that's the case, then something must have been done to me. I've already been controlled by Jian Gui.”

“It probably isn't just me. Qi Ling, Xiao Wang Zi, Ku Feng, and the others must be the same!”

Having served as a noble steward for many years, coupled with his innate disposition, Cang Xu had become quite adept at the art of scheming.

Ever since recognizing the true nature of Jian Gui, he harbored deep suspicions toward this gold level necromancer.

This time, Jian Gui had proactively imparted advanced soul aspect inheritance to other necromancers. Even if it was only a portion, and even after Jian Gui explained his reasons, Cang Xu remained profoundly uneasy.

He feared Jian Gui had tampered with the inheritance content.

Another doubt gnawed at him - did Jian Gui not fear that one or more necromancers might betray him upon learning his plans?

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng might be trustworthy. But Cang Xu, an outsider who had joined only recently - had he truly earned Jian Gui's complete trust? Simply because he participated in the Underworld River battle?

Then there was Qi Ling.

Though Qi Ling herself worshiped other sea gods, and not Charming Blue Goddess, her heart remained with her fishman clan from her mortal days. Even after becoming a necromancer, she continued serving the Da Ji Clan.

What if Qi Ling betrayed Jian Gui to Zhou Zhang for her clan's sake?

It was entirely possible.

A man like Jian Gui couldn't fail to consider such a possibility.

Had Jian Gui been someone like Xiao Wang Zi, Cang Xu wouldn't have harbored such doubts.

But the crux was that he was Jian Gui, a man who, at the brink of life and death, could sacrifice others as pawns to secure his own survival.

Thus, a conjecture naturally formed in Cang Xu's mind.

“We've likely all been tampered with, and this method ensures Jian Gui's own safety.”

Recalling the backgrounds of the other necromancers, Cang Xu immediately realized: If Jian Gui wanted to tamper with them, it would be incredibly simple. Opportunities abounded.

Qi Ling had been summoned by Jian Gui himself. Qi Ling had also served as Jian Gui's alchemy assistant for years. For Jian Gui to tamper with them would have been all too easy.

As for the undead ronin Ku Feng, Jian Gui had originally intended to summon him personally. But ultimately, it was Doctor Sui Shi who stepped in and forged Ku Feng. Doctor Sui Shi was one of the Twelve Saints, sharing the same orthodox undead lineage as Jian Gui. Considering the scenario where Doctor Sui Shi rushed to support during the battle of the Underworld River, their close relationship was evident. Given this connection, it's entirely plausible that Doctor Sui Shi would willingly cooperate with Jian Gui to tamper with Ku Feng.

As for Xiao Wang Zi, this necromancer wasn't personally converted by Jian Gui. But Xiao Wang Zi's mind was simple; all these years, he never saw through Jian Gui's true nature. Having interacted for so long, how could Jian Gui not find an opportunity?

Finally, there's Cang Xu himself. The Underworld River battle exposed his greatest vulnerability. On the ghost ship, Cang Xu was saved by Jian Gui. Cang Xu remained unconscious for an extended period. In truth, his current body was actively provided by Jian Gui. These are all loopholes!

“For Jian Gui, only by tampering with us can he truly trust us.”

“Thus, the extent of this safeguard can be inferred.”

Jian Gui possessed the confidence to proactively impart advanced knowledge in the inheritance. Clearly, he harbored no fear of these necromancers informing on him or leaking the inheritance content.

Should any necromancer among them commit treachery, whether informing or divulging the inheritance, Jian Gui’s covertly planted measures would activate instantly to suppress it.

"Therefore, such measures should be capable of instantly eliminating any one of us at a critical moment. "

“Then, does it possess surveillance capabilities?”

“Most likely not,” Cang Xu pondered, his mind racing with speculation.

If it had surveillance, Jian Gui would have detected his earlier communication with the fishman youngster.

At this life-or-death juncture, Jian Gui would never pass up any opportunity to exploit external forces. He would definitely seek out Cang Xu for negotiations.

After all, he was practically cornered now, with no choice but to rely on the methods of the blasphemous priest to fight for a chance to escape.

“In fact, if there's still surveillance capability, then the control mechanisms deployed by Jian Gui are simply too vast.”

It was like setting up a magic formation: the more functions it had, the stronger its power, the larger the area it covered, the more potent its aura, the greater the resources it consumed, and the more conspicuous it became.

“We're all necromancers. If such methods become too extensive, the likelihood of detection increases significantly.”

"As seen in the advanced inheritance, all necromancer inheritances share a common origin. While elementary inheritances lack the expansive vistas of higher ones, their content undoubtedly forms the foundational bedrock."

"If my understanding is correct, Jian Gui's methods should be rooted within our very souls. The constraints imposed upon us mirror how Xiao Wang Zi commands his skeletal legions, just as I manipulate those death heads, or how Qi Ling and the ghost ship command their ghosts."

“Manipulating souls is what necromancers excel at. Especially Jian Gui, who specializes in the soul aspect.”

“In a way, we could be seen as Jian Gui's undead summons.”

"We're bound by divine and magic spells, yet we can communicate secretly. This unbroken, undisturbed link is likely the strong bond between master and summoned creature."

“Therefore, that earlier exchange, Jian Gui's first words were actually a test directed at me.”

“He wanted to see if I had already sensed the tampering within myself.”

As Cang Xu pondered further, the truth gradually unfolded before his eyes.

It must be said that Cang Xu possessed a meticulous mind; in discerning plots and schemes, he was undoubtedly the foremost among the survivors.

"If our connection to Jian Gui is indeed like this, then in his eyes, we are his possessions, and he is our master in a potential sense."

“This explains why he has been lavishly supporting Qi Ling, Ku Feng, and Xiao Wang Zi all along.”

Cang Xu recalled the first undead gathering on Weeping Pine Island.

Even then, he had noticed: in such undead assemblies, Jian Gui was the pivotal, central figure. He provided resources others couldn't offer like soul crystals, memory crystals. And Jian Gui's demands for trade were remarkably modest. Though holding a monopolistic advantage, his prices remained fair in every transaction.

It was as if Jian Gui was playing with the others!

Cang Xu had previously believed: as a member of the orthodox undead lineage, Jian Gui sought to nurture and support other necromancers, embodying the magnanimity and vision befitting his status.

This perception had been shared by Xiao Wang Zi and the others.

But upon deeper reflection, a contradiction emerged.

Namely, undead resources!

For necromancers, undead resources were finite. Though Jian Gui belonged to the orthodox lineage of the undead, was his resource pool truly vast enough to justify such investments?

Necromancers like Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng, even if they were silver level, did their talents truly warrant such substantial investment?

As Cang Xu recalled this, the echo of Jian Gui's booming laughter filled his ears, brimming with hearty cheerfulness.

Yet now, he sensed a chill emanating from that very laughter.

"From Jian Gui's perspective, these three necromancers are his summoned servants, his property, not outsiders. Therefore, investing resources to cultivate three capable silver level subordinates is entirely worthwhile."

“This is Jian Gui's true face.”

The warmth he displayed was false.

His methods were covert.

He concealed his true intentions exceptionally well.

Even Cang Xu couldn't help but admire: Jian Gui truly had a knack for this!

“In that case, my very life is manipulated by Jian Gui. Life or death rests solely on his whim.”

Cang Xu's expression was utterly composed.

He would eliminate any threat to his life without hesitation.

But clearly, Jian Gui was a gold level. Even now, with everyone else captured, Cang Xu couldn't handle him alone.

"To confront Jian Gui requires utmost caution, then more caution. The slightest slip, and he'll activate his methods, ending me in an instant."

“Yet precisely because he possesses such power, I unexpectedly gained his trust ahead of schedule.”

"That's why he imparted the advanced inheritance to me and accepted my earlier negotiation demands. He secretly implanted control measures within me, making him believe my life and death are entirely within his grasp."

“If I escape this time, he'll likely confront me about it.”

“Phew... Thank goodness the commander is nearby!”

Cang Xu thought of the fishman youngster, exhaling a breath of relief, feeling profoundly fortunate once more.

This was the power of wisdom!

Due to Cang Xu's limited magician abilities, he couldn't detect the passive manipulation affecting him nor determine the nature of this control method.

Yet through his ingenuity, he pushed beyond the limits of his magician skills, compensating for his lack of transcendent powers.

“Wait a moment, perhaps I can seize this opportunity to extract more undead inheritance from Jian Gui!” The thought stirred Cang Xu's heart.

After further deliberation, Cang Xu concluded there was no better opportunity!

Under normal circumstances, he'd need to prove his bloodline, endure countless trials, and demonstrate unwavering loyalty before even becoming eligible for inheritance of the undead orthodoxy.

It was a lengthy process fraught with danger.

The orthodoxy would undoubtedly conduct thorough, covert background checks.

But in this peculiar predicament, Cang Xu could leverage Zhou Zhang and the pressure of life-or-death stakes to force Jian Gui to reveal more inheritances.

“Esteemed Jian Gui, surely you don't wish my failure to ultimately doom your escape, right?”

“Killing me is simple, but it will inevitably alert Zhou Zhang.”

“Since you've already imparted some advanced inheritance, surely you won't mind sharing the intermediate undead knowledge as well?”

Chapter 670: Zhou Zhang’s Plan

No sooner had Cang Xu obtained the second part of the advanced soul aspect inheritance than he began scheming for the intermediate soul aspect inheritance.

This wasn’t due to greed, but rather because such an opportunity was exceedingly rare!

Missing this chance meant there might never be another such golden opportunity in the future. Even if he exhausted every effort and resource, he might still fail to secure it.

The undead inheritance was currently divided into three branches: the soul aspect, corpse aspect, and bone aspect.

Cang Xu held little interest in the corpse aspect or bone aspect materials. However, he knew that the intermediate soul aspect inheritance contained the method for crafting soul crystals and a spell capable of testing one's aptitude upon becoming undead.

He wanted both of these things!

First was the soul crystal, it was truly vital.

This unique resource, forged only by necromancers, was nearly indispensable for their cultivation. Especially for necromancers transformed into ghostly forms, its benefits were paramount.

Though Cang Xu could currently procure them from Jian Gui through existing connections, the inherent risk was obvious: relying on trade was unreliable. Being able to craft them himself would be ideal.

The spell for assessing undead aptitude was also vital.

In Cang Xu's plan, this spell would be paired with the Bloodline Purification Formation. First, use the spell to determine the exact bloodline within the undead. Then, seek out the corresponding undead bloodline and purify it through the Bloodline Purification Formation. Following this entire process would enhance the aptitude of any undead life form.

Of course, Cang Xu could only speculate about the exact effects of this soul-testing spell.

It might merely assess a soul's potential without directly identifying its bloodline.

If that were the case, Cang Xu would need to extort more undead information from Jian Gui..

Jian Gui's group was known as the orthodox undead faction, possessing astonishing foundations. With a founder like You Shen, they were undoubtedly the world's foremost authority on this.

Cang Xu had gained immense assistance from the War Merchant's database, so he naturally coveted the orthodox undead faction's archives.

“But extorting them now wouldn't be appropriate.”

The timing was poor.

Cang Xu had just successfully extorted information from Jian Gui, obtaining the method for using the memory crystal making device. To return immediately after leaving and demand more extortion? That would be too much!

Jian Gui would surely interpret it as provocation, perhaps even suspecting Cang Xu had lost his mind.

Cang Xu had already deduced that Jian Gui had tampered with him, and this covert control method was exceptionally potent. Perhaps a single thought from Jian Gui could annihilate Cang Xu outright.

Thus, Cang Xu dared not overstep too far, after all, his life was still temporarily in someone else's hands.

Of course, based on Cang Xu's understanding of Jian Gui, he knew the probability of him dying under such control was currently low.

For Jian Gui wasn't the type to kill in a fit of rage.

Moreover, Cang Xu's unexplained death would inevitably draw suspicion and investigation from figures like Zhou Zhang. This would be highly detrimental to Jian Gui, especially given his plans for covert maneuvers.

Still, with his very life at stake, Cang Xu dared not gamble recklessly.

“Besides, I have far more important matters to attend to.”

Cang Xu returned to his makeshift shelter.

Soon after, he received another communication from the fishman youngster.

Sitting cross-legged on the sand, he felt grains coalesce between his thighs into characters: “The device has been made.”

“I shall now describe its form and function.”

Accompanying the description, an alchemy device shaped like a coin gradually materialized within Cang Xu's vision.

After establishing contact, the fishman youngster cut the communication abruptly without elaborating.

He immediately ordered Zhong Yong to devote all efforts to crafting a communication alchemy device.

Using the precision eel driller for communication proved inefficient and carried exposure risks.

The coin-shaped alchemy communication device was compact, easily slipping through the narrow holes drilled by the precision eel driller.

The coin device was also imbued with Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, set to render it completely invisible.

Even if an unsuspecting person touched it, their sense of touch would be deceived. They would believe they had merely touched empty air.

However, as Cang Xu uncovered more truths, he could not only see the coin communication device but also feel it.

The coin device possessed autonomous movement capabilities, actively floating up and hovering near Cang Xu's lips.

Cang Xu opened his mouth slightly, allowing the coin device to float inside.

He held the coin device in his mouth and pressed it down with his tongue.

The coin device began to heat up. Within seconds, like a branding iron, it fused directly beneath his tongue, becoming a badge.

The intense agony it caused was endured by Cang Xu with sheer willpower.

Throughout the process, his expression remained utterly calm, his gaze utterly still.

Cang Xu focused all his attention, silently reciting the contents of the undead inheritance in his mind.

These contents were all recorded by the coin.

Soon, the coin was loaded with methods on how to become a blasphemous priest, spells like Memory Switching Spell, and the “correct operating procedures for building the memory crystal making device” that Cang Xu had just extorted.

When Cang Xu ceased feeding content into the coin, it lay dormant for thirty seconds before heating up again. From a badge-like imprint, it solidified into a physical form once more.

Seizing the moment while Cang Xu sighed, it floated out and landed among the sand and stones.

The precision eel driller immediately retrieved it, clutching the coin device as it swiftly burrowed through the shack's cracks, vanishing from Cang Xu's sight in moments.

Soon after, the coin landed in the hands of the fishman youngster.

Rather than inspecting it himself, the youngster placed the coin directly into a specific reading device.

Almost instantly, he saw the specific text regarding the inherited knowledge displayed on the device's screen.

“Memory Switching Spell, Memory Concealment Spell, Subconscious Reminder Spell...” The fishman youngster glanced at the blasphemous priest's methods. Clearly, this wasn't just the Memory Switching Spell alone, but a combination of many spells working together.

However, the fishman youngster was solely a cultivator of battle qi, having not pursued spellcasting.

Although his bloodline within his Bloodcore possessed considerable magic aptitude, the youngster had never found the time or energy to dedicate to it. Before this, he had indeed considered dual cultivation.

“Starting magic training from scratch now would be far too slow,” the fishman youngster sighed, “Once Cang Xu arrives, he could use these spells to become a blasphemous priest.”

By coordinating with Cang Xu, the fishman youngster might find a way to escape this place.

"Huh? Perhaps I could try it on myself right now?” The fishman youngster discarded his earlier thoughts upon reading the latter part.

“According to these records, even without the abilities of a spellcaster, I could alter my own memories using the memory crystal.”

At the same moment, Jian Gui murmured a similar exclamation.

“Too bad I have the memory crystal making device but can't use it.”

The memory crystal alchemy device was an incredibly powerful tool, not only precise but also efficient and convenient. For Jian Gui to manually alter his own memories was extremely troublesome. Bound by dual restraints, he couldn't achieve the necessary precision.

“At this rate, there's no way I could complete a full fabricated memory on my own.”

Jian Gui grew impatient.

He began to berate himself: Why hadn't he practiced memory forgery more diligently in the past?

He analyzed the root cause: it was primarily because he had acquired a memory crystal making device. With this device, even non-magicians could easily operate it to meet standards. The time and materials consumed were at least ten times less than when he did it himself.

With such a powerful tool, who would bother to painstakingly hone memory fabrication abilities?

"If I possessed a Soul Weaver, Gray Snake Turtle Bone, Death Herald Bird, or Falling Star bloodline, I might have trained diligently, after all, there'd be hope to surpass the limits of the memory crystal making device. But I don't..."

Jian Gui's bloodline restricted his potential in this area.

No amount of diligent practice could ever match the memory crystal making device. So why bother?

Investing precious time, energy, manpower, and resources elsewhere clearly yielded far greater returns.

Who could have imagined he'd end up in this desperate predicament?

Jian Gui refused to die here.

Though he had already died once, becoming a necromancer, an undead life was still a life!

“My progress is minuscule. Others must be faring even worse.”

As time slipped away under death's relentless pressure, Jian Gui's sense of urgency surged.

He concluded sending a distress signal was impossible. His greatest, and only path to survival lay in the blasphemous priest's method.

To ensure the progress of memory fabrication, Jian Gui had to strictly monitor everyone else's advancement.

The next day, Jian Gui gathered everyone once more.

They pretended to discuss prayer content while secretly exchanging information and reporting their respective progress. Jian Gui continuously imparted his insights and experience to others.

To ensure his own survival, he held nothing back in sharing his expertise.

The reason he hadn't shared this knowledge earlier was that he understood the importance of gradual progression.

After gaining some practical experience, learning from others' wisdom would be more effective for absorption and comprehension, thereby accelerating their growth.

"It seems you've all studied our Lord's teachings quite deeply. Very good." Zhou Zhang suddenly materialized outside the shack.

She had used divine magic to disguise herself.

The necromancers were engrossed in their discussion, none noticing her arrival.

Zhou Zhang's sudden appearance startled everyone.

They hurriedly rose to their feet, bowing and greeting her.

Zhou Zhang observed them, a smile on her face. "Do you know? Many have urged me to sever ties with you. Each of your gatherings stirs the nerves of countless souls. I have suppressed these concerns because I believe exchange strengthens faith and deepens devotion. This test has not disappointed me."

At these words, the undead breathed a collective sigh of relief.

They had feared their secret gatherings had been discovered. Now it seemed their worries had been unfounded.

But in the next moment, Zhou Zhang continued, "Though the next blood sacrifice transmission is still some time away, I believe it best to inform you now."

At this point, Zhou Zhang flashed a teasing smile: "You must select one undead companion to participate in the next blood sacrifice transmission. Remember, only one."

In an instant, every undead present, including Jian Gui, turned deathly pale!

Chapter 671: Who will be First?

"When people find themselves in dire straits, banding together for warmth is the usual choice," Zhou Zhang left the shack with a smile.

On the surface, she allowed the captives to meet freely, but secretly, she kept a close watch.

Zhou Zhang was a saint level expert. Her innate strength already surpassed that of Ba Qi, Jian Gui, and the others. The pirates and necromancers fighting each other provided her with an opportunity. She easily took them all down.

Thus, the prisoners split into two factions.

The necromancers led by Jian Gui, and the human pirates led by Ba Qi.

Zhou Zhang arranged it this way not because she had uncovered Jian Gui's plans, but because this was her original intention.
