"Back on Weeping Pine Island, I approached you willingly, seeking the intermediate soul aspect inheritance. Little did I know it would lead to the Underworld River battle, where I barely escaped with my life. Yet in the end, I must still lose this life!"

"Perhaps this is simply my fate. It's destined to end here."

These words caused Jian Gui's gaze to flicker repeatedly.

Cang Xu's gloomy confession sent a chill down his spine, fearing Cang Xu might act irrationally.

Like voluntarily informing Zhou Zhang, exposing the plot Jian Gui and others were plotting, in exchange for his own survival.

Of course, Jian Gui had planted safeguards within Cang Xu and wasn't worried about his secrets being exposed.

But killing Cang Xu rashly would inevitably draw a thorough investigation from Zhou Zhang and the others.

Jian Gui's escape plan was already fraught with difficulty. Facing a rigorous investigation on top of that would have unimaginable consequences.

He still needed Cang Xu now!

“Don't give up hope. You might succeed in converting after all,” Jian Gui offered consolation.

Cang Xu shook his head slightly, his expression utterly hopeless.

Jian Gui pondered for a moment, then took a deep breath and said, "We undead are beings of negative energy. We possess advantages distinct from ordinary lifeforms, even if your conversion fails this time, death isn't necessarily guaranteed."

"If you don't trust me, surely you must trust Doctor Sui Shi. He is one of the Twelve Saints, a figure of saint level standing."

Cang Xu's expression immediately shifted, incredulity evident as he asked, “Are you suggesting, Lord Jian Gui, that I might still survive this time?”

As he spoke, a new spark of hope ignited in Cang Xu's eyes.

This was, of course, Jian Gui's deception!

Cang Xu understood this, and Jian Gui knew it too.

Jian Gui continued, "Since Doctor Sui Shi bestowed the Flying Head Spell upon you, he would never let you slip from his sight. He must have placed some arrangement upon you."

“I don't know the specifics myself, so unless absolutely necessary, you shouldn't give up so easily!”

“By the way, didn't you just mention wanting the intermediate soul aspect inheritance?”

“Once this crisis passes, I shall impart a portion of it to you.”

Jian Gui knew such vague lies wouldn't fool Cang Xu, so he had to offer some tangible benefit to bolster his persuasion.

Cang Xu froze slightly, “But wasn't the intermediate inheritance conditional?”

Jian Gui smiled. "We're sharing life and death this time, that fulfills part of the condition. Therefore, imparting a portion to you wouldn't be considered a violation."

Cang Xu sneered inwardly but outwardly wore an expression of surprise and delight.

Had Jian Gui not brought it up first, Cang Xu would have requested it in a more tactful manner.

Now that Jian Gui had spoken, Cang Xu felt not satisfied but instead pressed further.

He sighed deeply, “Lord Jian Gui, despite whatever methods Doctor Sui Shi has laid upon me, my fate this time seems grim indeed.”

“Among the intermediate soul aspect inheritances, what I most wish to see is the section concerning soul crystals.”

“I beg you, could I view it now?”

“Even a single glance would suffice!”

Seeing Cang Xu's sincerity, Jian Gui recalled their first meeting on Weeping Pine Island.

He uttered the same words of admiration as on Weeping Pine Island: “Cang Xu, you are a true magician, possessed of an intense thirst for knowledge.”

“I shall grant your request at once.”

“The section concerning soul crystals...”

“I'm sorry, I cannot give you more than this. It is a matter of principle.”

“Thank you, Lord Jian Gui!” Cang Xu made no attempt to conceal his excitement, immediately bowing in gratitude.

Returning to his quarters, Cang Xu studied the newly acquired soul aspect inheritance, marveling at the mysteries of fate.

Though he now found himself a captive, his life under grave threat, opportunity truly lurked within such peril.

This time, he had seized that chance firmly, achieving most of his objectives.

Had he followed the conventional path, the time and effort required would have been far more immense.

Chapter 679: The Inheritance Door Opens

Beneath the snow-capped Splice Mountain, Grafting Town gradually faded into the dusk of the setting sun.

In the heart of Grafting Town, within the residence of a gold level magician Jia Bing, Zi Di and Zong Ge were making their final attempt.

Zi Di's spell succeeded, scattering beams of magic light that clung to the square metal door.

The door hummed and vibrated under the searing glow.

Zi Di gritted her teeth, straining every ounce of concentration to sustain the spell.

Zong Ge took a deep breath and lunged forward. His biceps bulged as he swung the Gold Flower Hacksaw in his clenched hands, creating a blur of light in the air.

Clang!

The saw struck the metal door with savage force. The powerful recoil sent it bouncing slightly upward before Zong Ge slammed it back down.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw clung to the metal, its teeth spinning violently as they gnawed into the surface.

Sparks flew in a whirlwind, dancing through the air before crashing back down onto the door.

Smack, smack, smack.

The crisp clatter of sparks against metal mingled with the frenzied grinding of the saw against the door, assaulting Zi Di and Zong Ge's eardrums.

Time passed, bit by bit..

Not long after, a flash of crimson blood light suddenly flickered across Zong Ge's body.

The half beastman snorted coldly, forced to take a step back and withdraw the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

Zi Di could only slowly cease the spell she had been sustaining.

As the spell's radiance and the swirling sparks gradually faded, the exposed metal door remained completely intact, not even a scratch visible.

Zi Di sighed, "Ah, it still won't work! This is our strongest attack."

For that assault, she had expended a silver level spell scroll to support Zong Ge.

Zong Ge led the charge, wielding the Gold Flower Hacksaw. This weapon excelled at slicing through hard materials.

But during the attack, the blood aura erupting from Zong Ge disrupted his battle qi infusion, nearly injuring him.

Zi Di looked at Zong Ge: "The technique the Axe Gang leader planted on you before dying must be removed immediately. Have you noticed it's triggering more frequently?"

Had this technique not activated, Zong Ge could have endured far longer.

Yet it offered little aid in breaching the metal door.

Zong Ge stared at the metal door, his voice calm, “Even without the blood feud hindering me, we would still fail this time. The metal door's defenses are beyond comprehension, truly befitting something originating from a legend level figure.”

The two had now pieced together the information regarding the metal door.

They knew it was left behind by Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong, concealing his legacy!

This answer came from the documents and notes Zi Di had obtained from Jia Bing.

After Jia Bing unexpectedly discovered the legacy, he rented this building and converted it into an alchemy laboratory.

He had tried countless methods here to crack the metal door, but none succeeded.

This was precisely why he had deliberately placed the alchemy lab in the town center. Because the metal door was right here!

Typically, magicians establish alchemy labs in secluded areas rich in natural resources.

Simply touching the metal door granted anyone a fragment of information left by Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong.

The message detailed the test criteria.

Only when grafted magic plants met these standards could the metal door be opened.

Jia Bing first attempted this legitimate path.

But he failed.

Though Jia Bing was a magician, a gold level one at that, his expertise lay in alchemy puppets. He was quite inept at cultivating magic plants.

While not entirely unskilled, he clearly couldn't meet the legendary figure's standards.

Jia Bing didn't give up, constantly trying new methods. His latest plan involved using acid to corrode the metal door. That's why, when Zi Di first arrived, she saw the unfinished potion Jia Bing had left behind, specifically designed for acid corrosion.

“Our last attempt failed. Let's go,” Zong Ge said.

Zi Di nodded without hesitation.

This had been their agreed-upon plan all along.

Securing Jia Bing's inheritance had already been a significant achievement. Breaching the metal door held no hope.

Zi Di and Zong Ge could only rationally call it quits.

Before departing, however, they activated the formation diagram panel, expending pearl bubbles to imbue the metal door with a Deceptive Disguise spell.

From Jia Bing's diary, Zi Di learned that he had never shared information about the metal door with anyone else.

His specific plan was to act alone first. If he could take it down covertly, that would be ideal. Only if it proved impossible would he seek assistance from others.

Thus, Jia Bing's faction remained unaware of the metal door's situation.

Had they known, Zi Di and Zong Ge would never have resorted to the Deceptive Disguise spell.

Jia Bing's situation bore a striking resemblance to that of Master Cai Jing.

Both had discovered the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong and sought to secretly excavate it.

Jia Bing was the unluckier of the two.

He was killed before making any progress whatsoever. His entire inheritance was subsequently seized by others.

By comparison, Master Cai Jing had better fortune.

Though disrupted by Ba Qi and exposing the legacy's secrets, he swiftly adjusted his strategy. Inviting Da Bei and Zhong Bei to assist accelerated his progress exponentially!

Now, it was their turn to attempt it.

A row of potted plants was arranged before the metal door.

A scene reminiscent of before unfolded once more.

Curved beams of condensed light shot forth from the centers of five fingers. The beams pierced the potted plants, relentlessly draining the nutrients from the grafted magic plants.

These plants withered at a visible pace, some collapsing outright.

Cai Jing, Da Bei, and Zhong Bei held their breath, growing increasingly tense. For this was the final moment, only the last yellow beam remained.

The beam pierced the magic plant, drawing energy that flowed back along the beam into the five fingers insignia.

“No!” Zhong Bei cried out suddenly.

The last magic plant showed signs of instability, seeming on the verge of collapse.

Da Bei immediately pulled a vial of potion from his robe and splashed it directly onto the magic plant in the pot.

It was the bloodline stabilizer!

With the potion's aid, the plant on the brink of collapse steadied itself once more.

But the respite was short-lived. Within seconds, signs of collapse reappeared.

Da Bei emptied another vial of bloodline stabilizer, forcing the plant to hold on.

Within about ten minutes, Da Bei had used over twenty vials of the potion. The exorbitant cost sent a chill through his heart.

Despite such a heavy price, the magic plant finally stabilized.

The yellow light seemed to be sated, slowly retracting and making the badge with its five-fingered design shine even more brilliantly.

Multicolored radiance emanated from the badge, covering the surface of the metal door before rapidly seeping into it.

The glow was not intense and soon faded away.

The next moment, Cai Jing, Da Bei, and his son watched as the metal door slowly vanished, revealing a small, safe-like interior space within.

“We did it!”

“Is this the legend level inheritance?”

Cai Jing and the others, too preoccupied to celebrate, all turned their intense, probing gazes toward it.

Chapter 680: Weeping Beauty + Sun Crown Flower

The metal door of the inheritance chamber finally swung open.

Master Cai Jing, Da Bei, and Zhong Bei finally beheld the scene within the chamber.

The vast space held only a single potted plant.

Nothing else.

The legacy left behind by the famed Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong was unexpectedly meager.

“Only this one item?” Magician Zhong Bei voiced his disappointment.

His father, Magician Da Bei, and Master Cai Jing also wore unsatisfied expressions.

Especially Master Cai Jing.

He had invested immense time and effort here, yet the reward seemed meager.

Compared to him, Magician Da Bei remained more composed: “This potted plant appears to be a Weeping Beauty, but it doesn't quite match the descriptions in the records.”

A single blossom stood planted in the pot.

The flower remained in bud form, not yet fully bloomed. The bud was plump and firm, hanging slightly downward. Its petals shimmered ice blue, dotted with faint fluorescence.

Judging by the flower alone, it was a Weeping Beauty.

It was a water attribute magic plant.

However, examining its roots revealed significant differences from the typical Weeping Beauty.

The roots of a Weeping Beauty were deep green and slightly curved downward. Their sinuous S-shaped curve resembled the graceful form of a slender beauty. Paired with the drooping bud and two large leaves, it evoked the image of a flower-like maiden weeping with her face hidden.

Hence the name “Weeping Beauty.”

Yet in this potted specimen within the inheritance door, the Weeping Beauty's rhizome was now entirely concealed beneath a vast expanse of orange-yellow petals.

These petals were densely arranged, layered upon each other like a woven orange skirt. Its hem trailed to the ground, creating a truly magnificent sight.

For a moment, all three magicians' gazes fixated on this extraordinary floral skirt.

Magician Zhong Bei mused, "I think I understand why Flower Skirt Island bears its name now. This island's moniker embodies the very essence of Fan Sheng Za Zhong, doesn't it?"

These words stirred something within Master Cai Jing.

He narrowed his eyes, scrutinizing closely. "The petals resembling an orange-yellow skirt appear to originate from the Sun Crown Flower."

The Sun Crown Flower was also a magic plant, belonging to both the light and fire attributes.

Magician Da Bei nodded slightly, acknowledging Master Cai Jing's observation. He continued his analysis: “This must be a magic plant specially grafted by Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong.”

“It has two main components: the Weeping Beauty and the Sun Crown Flower.”

"The Weeping Beauty is a water attribute plant capable of absorbing and secreting vast amounts of moisture. This plant is extremely delicate, often succumbing to root and leaf rot due to excessive moisture secretion."

"The Sun Crown Flower, conversely, thrives on sunlight, acting as a solar collector among plants. Yet it lacks intelligence; its petals can be scorched and damaged from storing too much sunlight, eventually withering and dying."

“Both are notoriously difficult magic plants to cultivate.”

“Yet Fan Sheng Za Zhong seems to have combined them perfectly.”

"The Sun Crown Flower's skirt of petals absorbs and stores immense sunlight. This light and heat dispels the heavy moisture inherent in the Weeping Beauty. Simultaneously, the Weeping Beauty's moisture nourishes the Sun Crown petals, cooling them and preventing scorching."

"Fan Sheng Za Zhong excelled at cultivating magic plants through grafting. Now, it seems his inheritance holds profound meaning, this magic planter perfectly aligns with his style."

Magician Da Bei's analysis earned agreement from the other two.

Zhong Bei spoke up: "Both the Weeping Beauty and the Sun Crown Flower are highly valuable magic plants. Now that they're stably grafted together, large-scale cultivation becomes feasible. Once mass production begins, the profits will be enormous!"

Master Cai Jing added: “Druids differ from us magicians. They don't impart knowledge directly but set trials, emphasizing practice and encouraging self-discovery. This magic planter is likely a milestone achievement from Fan Sheng Za Zhong. But how much knowledge one gains from it depends entirely on personal insight and ability."

Master Cai Jing analyzed it from his personal approach.

As the three conversed, the magicians' wisdom shone through.

With each comment, they dissected the scene before them piece by piece.

After confirming no danger or ambush lay ahead, the task turned to retrieving the magic planter.

Magician Da Bei stepped aside, deferring this opportunity to Master Cai Jing.

Throughout this excavation of inheritance, Master Cai Jing had always taken the lead. On this point, Magician Da Bei understood his place, demonstrating keen tact.

Master Cai Jing felt a quiet satisfaction at his own maneuver.

However, as he approached the secret door of inheritance, he suddenly picked up a hidden message.

“So Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong's legacy isn't confined to this place alone!” Cai Jing's eyes flickered, but he maintained his outward calm as he reached out and retrieved the magic planter.

He held the hidden message to himself for now, instead scrutinizing the magic planter in his hands.

He immediately noticed the extraordinary nature of this flowerpot.

Approximately three magic spells and five divine spells were imbued within it. Enchantment techniques had been employed, effectively stabilizing most of the magic and divine spells within.

This was hardly surprising. Fan Sheng Za Zhong was a great druid, skilled in both divine and magic spells.

The soil within the pot was Abundant Earth.

This was a renowned product of the Fertile Land Church.

True to its name, Abundant Earth contained immense earthen blessings, capable of nurturing nearly any crop to bountiful harvest.

Finally, Master Cai Jing began to deeply examine the grafted magic flower.

He couldn't help but exclaim in admiration, “This is nothing short of a work of art.”

Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong had ingeniously grafted two distinct bloodlines together, achieving not only balance but mutual benefit.

The master had carefully selected two magic plants for grafting. Though multiple divine and magic spells were used to enhance the flowerpot, the grafted plant itself possessed almost no extraordinary power!

This meant that even separated from the pot, the grafted magic plant possessed strong inherent vitality.

In stark contrast, the large-scale grafted magic plants previously cultivated by Master Cai Jing and others required cryogenic preservation techniques just to survive for a short time.

The gap between the two approaches was truly enormous!

Moreover, the masterfully concise design by Fan Sheng Za Zhong made the previous works of Master Cai Jing and others seem like childish scribbles by comparison.

After examining it for a while, Master Cai Jing handed the magic planter to Magician Da Bei for inspection.

Witnessing this, Magician Zhong Bei nearly cried out in excitement.

Earlier, he was still slightly worried.

After all, this inheritance excavation was led by Master Cai Jing. Zhong Bei and his father had only been invited to join midway.

Though the father-son duo contributed significantly, it would have been perfectly reasonable for Master Cai Jing to withhold the final inheritance gain from them, offering compensation instead.

But reality was different, Master Cai Jing handed the magic planter directly to Magician Da Bei for inspection.

The significance of this gesture was extraordinary, leaving Master Zhong Bei deeply moved.

Magician Da Bei hurriedly expressed his gratitude, took the planter, and held it close to examine it carefully.

After a moment, he spoke: “This confirms our previous conjecture.”

“Just as you, Master Cai Jing, had suggested.”

"Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong left us only one outcome. It's deeply connected to the grafting techniques we examined during our assessment. But this single result clearly surpasses the sum of all our previous grafted creations."

"How much knowledge can we ultimately gain from this result? That's impossible to say. It depends on each of our insights and abilities."

"Personally, I prefer the legacy left by magicians. Their inheritance consists of knowledge, far more straightforward than this."

This difference revealed the essence of magicians versus druids.

Mages pursued knowledge, leaving behind legacies that were knowledge itself, ensuring absolute precision. One was one, two was two, ambiguity was taboo.

Druids, however, often practiced both divine and magic spells. Compared to magicians, they placed greater emphasis on intuition and perception. They encouraged people to feel the present moment, to seek the meaning of life itself. They wanted the inheritor to achieve personal growth through their own practices. As for how much one could advance? That was nature's choice, life's evolution, fate's destiny. There was no need to force it.

Zhong Bei gazed at the magic planter in his father's hands, his voice brimming with excitement: "In truth, even the worst outcome of this insight grants us a never-ending goldmine. Simply mass-cultivating these grafted magic plants would yield staggering profits."

Master Cai Jing added: "Therefore, moving forward, we can collaborate. Let's cultivate these grafted magic plants together!"

Da Bei and Zhong Bei, father and son, both looked stunned.

Cai Jing looked at them and continued, "You know me well. Though honored as a master, I'm really just an outcast from the mainstream alchemy world, an exiled wandering magician. But you're different. You have connections back home and possess considerable strength yourselves. You could easily establish large-scale cultivation zones right in the heart of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom."

"You also contributed significantly to this legacy. Without the ice-preservation technology you provided, we never could have achieved success so swiftly."

Upon hearing this, Magician Zhong Bei nearly danced with excitement. He never imagined Master Cai Jing would be so generous, this meant complete sharing!

He'd assumed Master Cai Jing would only lend the magic plants for a short time. Yet here he was offering full access. This was an absolute windfall for the father-son duo.

Da Bei felt a pang of doubt, but outwardly played it coy: "Master Cai Jing, please don't misunderstand us. We would never touch what we shouldn't, nor harbor the slightest covetous thought. Your self-assessment is far too modest. With your abilities, you could easily leave the Ice Sculpture Kingdom behind and find suitable cultivation bases on countless islands. Wherever you go, whatever faction you join, you would be welcomed as an honored guest."

Chapter 681: Benefits Attract Enemies and Friends

Upon hearing his father's words, Magician Zhong Bei nearly popped his eyes out in panic.

What was wrong with his father? Was he actually pushing away a never-ending gold mine?

Cai Jing fixed a deep gaze on Da Bei, pointed at him, then burst into laughter, “I knew I hadn't misjudged you. Working with you has been truly delightful.”

“But you needn't doubt my motives. My reason for seeking your partnership is simple.”

“You'll all understand the moment you approach it.”

Master Cai Jing gestured toward the secret door of inheritance behind him.

Magician Da Bei remained motionless, instead signaling his son with a glance.

Such tacit understanding between father and son was, of course, present.

Magician Zhong Bei slowly began walking, approaching the secret door of inheritance.

He suddenly halted, his expression shifting slightly. Turning back, he urgently reported to his father.

“I've received a hidden message. It should be accessible after opening the door and approaching it.”

“Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong wishes us later generations, upon taking the magic potted plant, to place a brand-new one within the secret door of inheritance. This will establish a new lineage, allowing it to continue flowing down through the ages.”

Upon hearing this, Magician Da Bei's eyes flickered slightly, “That is indeed the druid way of doing things.”

This lineage was like a small cottage in the forest.

It welcomed and assisted all travelers who entered to stay. Simultaneously, it hoped each guest would contribute what they could to the cottage. For instance, chopping a pile of firewood before departing. This way, future travelers arriving on rainy days could immediately light a fire for warmth.

This encouraged mutual support and reciprocal benefit.

“Good news, Father!” Magician Zhong Bei beamed, “This message also tells us that if we place a brand-new magic potted plant inside, and this plant gains recognition from the secret door of inheritance, we'll obtain the exact location of another inheritance site belonging to Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong!”

Upon hearing this, Magician Da Bei raised his eyebrows and looked at Master Cai Jing again.

Everything clicked into place.

In that moment, he grasped the unspoken intent behind Master Cai Jing's words, and his guard rapidly dissolved.

Magician Zhong Bei returned, his expression charged with excitement.

He declared to Master Cai Jing, "This holds tremendous potential! We'll continue using the ice-preservation technique, mass-cultivating magic plants for grafting. Eventually, we'll produce a specimen that meets the criteria. Once we insert it, we'll uncover information about the second inheritance site."

But on this matter, Master Cai Jing held a different perspective: "Our previous use of the ice-preservation technique relied on sheer volume. It was essentially passing off inferior work as genuine, muddling through. Based on my estimation, cultivating grafted magic plants that meet the standards of Fan Sheng Za Zhong is far more difficult than opening a secret door. Using our previous method again offers little chance of success."

Magician Zhong Bei paused slightly, then wore a thoughtful expression.

Master Cai Jing's analysis made perfect sense.

Undiminished in his enthusiasm, Magician Zhong Bei continued, “If that's the case, we'll simply have to commit to long-term research and development. I believe that with the combined strength of our two parties, as long as we cooperate wholeheartedly, we will certainly succeed in cultivating qualifying magic plants in the future.

In Magician Zhong Bei's view, this was an investment well worth making! This was a legacy left by a legend level figure.

They had already succeeded once. Though they had only harvested a single grafted magic plant, that single achievement alone was priceless, making them rich beyond measure.

But upon hearing Magician Zhong Bei's words, Master Cai Jing smiled faintly and shook his head.

The Medium Magician looked puzzled, unable to grasp Master Cai Jing' meaning.

His father, Magician Da Bei, gently reminded him, “Fool, why should we let ourselves be led by the nose by a single piece of inheritance information?”

“Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong arranged this with a deep understanding of human nature, but he is, after all, a druid, perhaps a bit too innocent.”

"This is indeed a clue, but the cost is high. We needn't follow his setup; we can find other clues ourselves and deduce the locations of other inheritances."

“In this regard, Master Cai Jing, you must have profound insights!”

Magician Zhong Bei couldn't help but fix his burning gaze upon Master Cai Jing.

Master Cai Jing replied calmly, “To put it plainly, it's actually quite simple.”

"Records show that Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong traveled the entire world. He is a figure of legend level; wherever he went, storms inevitably followed, leaving behind profound historical accounts nearly everywhere."

"This greatly facilitates our search for clues. Often, we need only follow the trail."

Magician Zhong Bei frowned slightly, “Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong did indeed travel the world. To my knowledge, he even ventured into Dragon Island and all manner of peculiar elemental realms. But to search for his legacy, the scope of ‘the entire world’ is rather too vast.”

Master Cai Jing continued, “During the early stages of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong's travels, the likelihood of him leaving a legacy is quite low.”

"Typically, he would gain insights during his journeys. Once these insights accumulated to a certain level, he would settle in one place for brief research. Only after achieving results would he leave behind a legacy."

"Take the legacy we acquired, for instance. After journeying through the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong settled on this deserted island for a time. He named it Flower Skirt Island. Here, he achieved his breakthrough and left behind this legacy, arranging it as we find it. That was his modus operandi."

Magician Zhong Bei perked up instantly, blurting out, “I know!”

“Splice Mountain!”

"Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong once stayed at Splice Mountain. The Grafting Town at its base now also traces back to him."

Master Cai Jing smiled and nodded, adding, "Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong also ventured deep into the heart of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, entering Tranquil Hill. He emerged only after over ten days. The likelihood he left a legacy there is also very high."

Magician Zhong Bei looked hesitant. "Tranquil Hill... that's just a rumor, right? True, Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong did receive an invitation from the royal family. But whether he actually entered Tranquil Hill isn't clearly recorded."

“Searching for the master's legacy within Tranquil Hill would be incredibly difficult.”

"The royal family won't allow us to go. It's one of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's most sacred forbidden zones!"

“Moreover, we can't be certain that Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong left any legacy there.”

Belonging to the Alchemy Guild, Magician Zhong Bei held deep reverence for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Master Cai Jing, however, held a different view: “We've already unlocked one of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong's legacies. Judging by our gains so far, we stand to benefit immensely.”

"The substantial profits we earn can help us build greater influence. Given time, we could fully negotiate with the royal family."

“As long as the power is equal and the benefits are sufficient, there's nothing that can't be negotiated.”

Magician Da Bei nodded, “Indeed, such a possibility exists. As one of the elders of the Alchemy Guild, I can also leverage the guild's channels to facilitate negotiations with the royal family.”

“Of course, before that, it would be best if we could confirm that Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong truly left his legacy in Tranquil Hill.”

Magician Zhong Bei listened with a dazed expression.

Magician Da Bei turned to Master Cai Jing, his tone solemn. "Forgive my boldness, Master Cai Jing. I now formally invite you to join our guild in my capacity as an elder of the Alchemy Guild. Is that acceptable?"

Magician Zhong Bei's mouth fell open at these words.

He distinctly remembered: Master Cai Jing had been exiled and expelled from the Alchemy Guild. His father extending such a direct invitation amounted to speaking for the guild itself. Could this truly be permissible?

Though Magician Zhong Bei was middle-aged, he remained far more inexperienced, especially compared to Magician Da Bei and Master Cai Jing.

The latter two were seasoned veterans, each understanding what the others sought. Sometimes a single sentence could carry multiple layers of meaning. Magician Zhong Bei struggled to keep pace with their rhythm, let alone grasp their true intentions.

Master Cai Jing gave a slight nod, his eyes wide as he spoke nonsense, yet his expression remained earnest and solemn: “Truthfully, I've always held the Alchemy Guild in the highest regard. To join its ranks would be an immense honor!”

“It's just that timing-wise, things might be a bit inconvenient for me right now.”

Magician Da Bei nodded.

Hearing the two seemed to reach an agreement, Zhong Bei grew restless again. He felt his father had made an empty promise, and Master Cai Jing had taken it at face value.

In truth, he knew: the Alchemy Guild held a deeply negative view of Cai Jing, and his father was merely one elder among many within the guild, not someone powerful enough to represent the entire organization personally.

“Oh no! What if Father fails to persuade the Alchemy Guild after returning and breaks his promise to Master Cai Jing? What will we do then?”

But Magician Da Bei and Master Cai Jing were both confident the matter would be resolved.

It all came down to profit!

Master Cai Jing had previously produced counterfeit medicines for personal gain, undermining the Alchemy Guild's profits. Thus, he was ostracized and exiled.

Now, with the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong as bait, the Alchemy Guild of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom would surely set aside past grievances and welcome Master Cai Jing back to collaborate on development.

This was precisely why Master Cai Jing chose to continue collaborating with Da Bei and the others.

On one hand, he needed territory and talent to cultivate grafted magic plants on a large scale, securing stable profits. On the other hand, the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong likely lay within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom itself. He needed access to its heartland to continue unearthing that legacy.

It was time to turn enemies into allies!

Da Bei and Cai Jing exchanged glances, confirming their deepened alliance. They now found each other more agreeable.

Da Bei finally fixed his gaze on the secret door of inheritance: “Though this place is exposed, we should destroy this door to buy time.”

Cai Jing shook his head slightly: "I've tried numerous methods before. Destroying the door is unlikely. A more practical approach is to erase the inheritance information here. This information serves as a rather obvious hint for those who come after."

The magicians were no druids; they felt no empathy for the arrangements of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong. To safeguard their own interests, they planned to destroy this secret door without hesitation or reservation.

But at that very moment, an intensely powerful aura suddenly approached.

The magicians' expressions changed simultaneously.

By the time they realized an attack was incoming, it was already too late.

Boom!

A violent explosion erupted, causing Flower Skirt Island itself to tremble.

The assailant floated mid-air, surveying the smoke-filled chaos below.

When he struck, his saint level aura could no longer be concealed—it blazed forth unmistakably!

Chapter 682: Qian Xing vs Cai Jing

Rolling clouds of dust gradually dispersed, revealing a massive, hemispherical light barrier.

Master Cai Jing, Magician Da Bei, and Magician Zhong Bei gathered together, gazing upward through the translucent shield.

Though there was only one enemy, all three magicians wore expressions of profound gravity.

For the attacker's aura was unmistakably that of a saint level cultivator!

A saint level fighter.

Magician Da Bei used an amplifying spell to inquire about the intruder's identity, emphasizing his own status.

“Elder of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild?” The floating saint level fighter sneered coldly, his face brimming with contempt.

He was an elf, but his dark skin marked him not as a snow elf, but a night elf.

His hair was pulled into a high ponytail, his frame tall and slender, his fingers long and slender. Each nail was razor-sharp, gleaming with a blue radiance.

“If this were the Sheng Ming Empire's Alchemy Guild, I might show some deference.”

“But the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild? What's that worth? Even the Ice Sculpture Kingdom itself is nothing special.”

The saint level fighter was supremely arrogant, showing utter contempt for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

“I know who he is!” Master Cai Jing suddenly exclaimed, “He's Qian Xing, a member of the Fire Beard Pirates.”

Magician Da Bei and Magician Zhong Bei instantly understood, realizing the source of Qian Xing's arrogance.

The Fire Beard Pirates was a massive, legend level power. It absolutely towered over the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild—even the kingdom itself couldn't compare.

The primary reason for this dominance was simple - Fire Beard himself was a legend level fighter!

Meanwhile, the three strongest individuals currently in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom were merely at the Saint level.

Of course, the kingdom's history had seen legend level figures, though none existed in the present era. Even so, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom possessed deep foundations and undoubtedly had methods to deal with legend level threats.

However, the methods used against the dead could never be compared to those employed against living legends.

Magician Da Bei invoked his status as an elder of the Alchemy Guild, a pedigree formidable enough to intimidate most. Yet against the Fire Beard Pirates, it only invited ridicule.

Shuang Tu Clinic.

Shuang Tu activated an alchemy device, remotely surveying the southern reaches of Flower Skirt Island.

The faintly visible image showed Qian Xing, tiny as an ant, alongside a massive, hemispherical magic formation shield.

Shuang Tu's heart was tense.

“According to the latest intelligence, Cai Jing and the others have spent the longest time ever recorded in the lowest level this time.”

“It's highly likely they've already opened the secret door of inheritance and obtained what lies within.”

“Sigh. I hope Qian Xing can stir up enough chaos this time for me to seize an opportunity.”

Qian Xing's arrival was, of course, the result of Shuang Tu's covert maneuvering.

After leaving Shuang Tu's clinic, Ba Qi vanished completely.

Unable to borrow Ba Qi's strength, Shuang Tu resorted to a desperate measure: impersonating Ba Qi to seek Qian Xing's aid.

His analysis of Qian Xing proved crucial to the plan's success.

Qian Xing took Ba Qi's plea seriously.

The Fire Beard Pirates was vast and faction ridden. If Qian Xing could use this to subdue Ba Qi, it would greatly bolster his standing and influence within the fleet.

Originally, Qian Xing had only intended to visit Flower Skirt Island to search for clues left by Ba Qi's pirate crew. Unexpectedly, he stumbled upon rumors concerning the inheritance of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong.

He was a pirate, after all!

Moreover, this involved the legacy of a legend figure of a level. Perhaps Ba Qi's disappearance was orchestrated by Cai Jing and his crew.

Thus, Qian Xing acted without hesitation.

“Current developments are unfolding exactly as planned. May the next steps proceed smoothly as well!”

Shuang Tu closely monitored the standoff's progress.

His primary mission was to seize Fan Sheng Za Zhong's legacy on Flower Skirt Island.

Unfortunately, his strength was too low, only silver level.

He could only resort to this desperate measure: luring Qian Xing into the fray.

Shuang Tu hoped Qian Xing would defeat Cai Jing and the others, yet also wished they could hold out as long as possible without suffering a crushing defeat.

Should Cai Jing and the others suffer a crushing defeat, the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong would fall into Qian Xing's hands, making it exponentially harder for Shuang Tu to claim it.

Thus, this time, Shuang Tu could only gamble on luck, hoping for an opportunity to arise in the ensuing battle.

Gazing at Qian Xing in the sky, Master Cai Jing was filled with astonishment and apprehension.

First it was Ba Qi; now it was Qian Xing. He couldn't fathom why he'd drawn the ire of the Fire Beard Pirates. Keep in mind this was near the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, not the Fire Beard Pirates' usual stomping grounds.

Qian Xing's timing couldn't be more perfect!

It was right after they'd just acquired the inheritance.

This couldn't help but make Master Cai Jing suspect whether there was a mole within their own ranks. Someone who'd tipped off Qian Xing just in time.

Pondering this line of thought, Master Cai Jing soon realized the likelihood of a mole among them was quite high. He had recruited a large number of potion apprentices to accelerate the excavation of the inheritance.

But now, uncovering the mole held little significance!

After all, Qian Xing was already in front of them.

“But we must remain vigilant!” Master Cai Jing immediately instructed Magician Zhong Bei to enter the underground alchemy laboratory, oversee the potion apprentices, and lock them all inside their rooms.

Meanwhile, Master Cai Jing joined forces with Magician Da Bei to confront Qian Xing.

“Lord Qian Xing, you desire the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong... We can indeed share a portion with you. This is not beyond negotiation.”

“Please understand, we have sacrificed far too much for this legacy.”

“Provided the price is right...”

Master Cai Jing feigned negotiation, seeking to buy time.

Qian Xing snorted coldly: "You wish to negotiate with me? Sure! First, remove this protective barrier to show your sincerity."

As a seasoned pirate, Qian Xing's aura was overwhelmingly intimidating.

“Absolutely impossible.” Master Cai Jing's face remained impassive as he flatly refused.

Qian Xing laughed coldly: “Then you're only trying to stall for time!”

With that, he unleashed a saint level battle skill, launching another assault.

The battle skill struck the magic formation's light barrier. The spherical shield collided with the saint level attack, erupting in a thunderous explosion.

The potion apprentices screamed within the alchemy lab.

Jars and flasks tumbled from the shelves amid the violent tremors, shattering into shards of glass.

"Silence! Calm yourselves. We remain safe for now. Anyone who makes a move will be executed on the spot." Magician Zhong Bei issued a stern warning, his voice thick with menace.

Spies infiltrating the potion apprentices feigned panic while exchanging subtle glances.

No one dared to act!

They truly had no choice.

On one hand, this was the meticulously constructed lair of Master Cai Jing. Numerous defensive measures and magic traps were deployed throughout.

On the other hand, Master Cai Jing's arrangements were impeccable. Though not a gold level magician, Zhong Bei, as a silver level magician, was more than capable of firmly suppressing these potion apprentices.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Qian Xing unleashed a barrage of battle skills, battering the light barrier until it swayed precariously.

Yet it still held firm.

The pride on Qian Xing's face dimmed slightly: “Your formation is quite interesting.”

Master Cai Jing exhaled a breath of stale air, relaxing slightly.

This was the defensive formation he had meticulously crafted. Not only did it possess exceptional defensive capabilities, but it also channeled external attacks along the earth vein, cleverly diverting them away.

When first setting up this formation, Master Cai Jing had been filled with hesitation. The cost of deploying it was truly exorbitant.

At the time, Master Cai Jing had primarily considered the possibility of Ba Qi launching a counterattack. To ensure absolute safety, he gritted his teeth and endured the pain to construct this magic formation.

Now it seemed it was all worth it.

Although Cai Jing was just a gold level magician, he could temporarily resist Qian Xing through the defensive formation.

Magicians were such transcendents. They could harness external forces, unleashing power far exceeding their own.

Yet after several rounds of attacks, the formation had drained immense energy. At least 30% of the elemental crystals Master Cai Jing had embedded within had been consumed.

Master Cai Jing roughly calculated in his mind: “Each time it withstands an attack of a saint level, it consumes at least 300 gold coins!”

He was straining his own finances just to barely counter a saint level powerhouse.

The clash between the two sides generated intense fluctuations, naturally drawing the attention of others.

“What exactly is happening?” asked Bing Xiao.

The human ringleader replied, "It's Master Cai Jing's side. He's under attack again. This time, the enemy is even stronger than Ba Qi. He's a saint level powerhouse, though his identity remains unconfirmed."

Bing Xiao drew in a sharp breath.

Saint level!

This was a pillar of a nation.

The old gang leader's expression grew grave: “So the rumors circulating on Flower Skirt Island were true after all.”

"Cai Jing and his group were indeed excavating the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong. The previous attack by Ba Qi exposed this secret, thereby attracting the attention of a saint level entity."

“Recall all our people and prepare for evacuation at any moment!”

This was the temporary stronghold of the Bayonet Gang. Bing Xiao's primary concern was collateral damage.

If the ongoing clashes between the two sides disrupted this area and plunged it into chaos, the Bayonet Gang would likely face attacks from those with ulterior motives.

After all, the Bayonet Gang were outsiders on Flower Skirt Island. Besides them, several other silver level factions operated here.

Recently, their efforts to establish roots on the island had already triggered the vigilance and repeated probing of established powers.

“Understood.” The human ringleader immediately acknowledged the order and sprang into action.

Spring Boxer wasn't at the gang's base.

He was browsing the black market.

Periodically, Flower Skirt Island hosted a black market trade.

The scale of these transactions was considerable.

Flower Skirt Island was a neutral island near the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. This location proved highly advantageous, making it a common destination for illicit dealings.

Thus, goods unavailable in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's legitimate markets were frequently found in the black market.

Chapter 683: Spring Boxer – I am the Future Gang Leader with Dreams!

“This silver level leather armor is quite nice.” In the black market, Spring Boxer was drawn to an item and couldn't help but stop in his tracks.

But when he inquired about the price, his expression turned slightly awkward.

He didn't have enough money on hand, not even half of what the silver level leather armor cost.

Accompanying him was a burly man with a triangular head and blue skin.

It was Lan Zao.

Seeing Spring Boxer's troubled expression, Lan Zao offered, “How much more do you need? Perhaps I could lend you some.”

Having survived several wild duels together, the two had built a solid rapport.

Their shared escape had laid the foundation for their friendship.

Of course, ulterior motives played a larger role.

Lan Zao knew his mission was to infiltrate the Bayonet Gang. As one of the gang's key ringleaders, Spring Boxer was relatively straightforward, an ideal target to approach.

Spring Boxer, too, sought to cultivate a relationship with Lan Zao. Not long ago, they had received a reply from the Axe Gang confirming Hen Fa's identity and background. Bing Xiao had directly assigned the task of recruiting Lan Zao to Spring Boxer. Lan Zao's recent actions had steadily increased his value as a recruit.

Spring Boxer himself held considerable admiration for Lan Zao.

Thus, before heading to the black market, he proactively sought out Lan Zao to invite him along.

Hearing Lan Zao wanted to borrow money from him, Spring Boxer felt somewhat conflicted.

His invitation to explore the market had been an attempt to find an opportunity to fund Lan Zao as a gesture of goodwill. Unexpectedly, the situation had reversed—it was Lan Zao who primarily intended to support him.

“But the fact that he proactively offered to lend me money shows he cares about me!” Spring Boxer thought to himself.

Spring Boxer had brought a substantial amount of funds this time.

This money was mainly intended to support Lan Zao, not for his own use.

The funds for his personal use weren't enough to purchase this silver level leather armor.

Unable to voice the real reason, Spring Boxer merely shook his head, “Let's browse around first. Maybe we'll find something better?”

Noticing Spring Boxer's awkwardness, Lan Zao asked curiously, “You've participated in several wild duels now. Haven't you earned any money?”

Spring Boxer sighed and spoke plainly, “I already told you before. I joined the wild duel mainly to earn rare alchemy materials. Our gang desperately needs them."

Lan Zao's gaze sharpened slightly. He thought to himself: "Desperately needed? According to Shuang Tu's analysis, these are most likely for the journey to Tranquil Hill."

Lan Zao asked again, “So, the gang didn't give you a reward? You know how dangerous the wild duel is!"

Spring Boxer scratched his head, “The old gang leader wanted to reward me, but I refused. After all, rebuilding the gang requires a lot of funds. If that money ended up in my hands, I honestly wouldn't know what to do with it.”

Lan Zao looked at Spring Boxer with a peculiar expression. " You truly put the gang first!"

Spring Boxer let out an “Ah” with a peculiar expression. He opened his mouth to explain but ultimately held back.

In truth, after Bing Xiao declared Spring Boxer as the next leader, his perspective on the gang had shifted considerably. As the future leader, he now prioritized the gang's growth over his personal ambitions.

Spring Boxer himself didn't harbor an overly intense thirst for power; his motivation stemmed more from carrying on Teng Dong Lang's legacy.

"So, maybe Shuang Tu's analysis is right. This guy is likely to be the next leader of the Bayonet Gang!" Lan Zao thought to himself.

Shuang Tu's assistance to Lan Zao extended beyond cultivating his combat prowess; he also subtly honed Lan Zao's intelligence-gathering skills during daily routines.

Shuang Tu shared extensive intelligence and his own assessments of the Bayonet Gang with Lan Zao, helping him gain a more accurate grasp of the gang's operations.

The conjecture that Spring Boxer would become the next leader of the Bayonet Gang wasn't difficult to deduce.

Shuang Tu knew Spring Boxer was Teng Dong Lang's coffin bearer, the most explicit connection. Bing Xiao, handling gang affairs these days, had also consciously promoted and trained Spring Boxer in many areas, a clear cultivation strategy.

"Keep browsing. We might find cheaper leather armor elsewhere." Spring Boxer led the way.

Lan Zao followed at a leisurely pace.

Soon, Lan Zao lingered at a stall, showing keen interest in a particular alchemy item.

It was none other than the alchemy projectile core.

“Who knew there'd be alchemy ammunition that pairs so perfectly with the Air Cannon!” Spring Boxer strongly urged Lan Zao to purchase them.

The vendor explained, "This isn't just for pairing with the Air Cannon. In fact, this core can be assembled into any shell casing. On the battlefield, it allows flexible allocation of ammunition to counter the enemy. What do you think? Would you like to buy some, sir?"

The vendor pitched to Spring Boxer with practiced ease.

This was actually Shuang Tu's arrangement.

Lan Zao purchasing it here would perfectly explain the origin of the alchemy warheads in his possession.

Spring Boxer had invited Lan Zao to the black market himself, an opportunity Lan Zao couldn't have asked for. Now, Spring Boxer was his best witness.

Spring Boxer shook his head, “I don't need these tools.”

He still wanted to recruit Lan Zao. Competing with him for purchases now would only ruin their friendship.

Spring Boxer asked again, "So, how about it, Hen Fa? Got enough cash? I don't have enough to buy a silver level leather armor myself, but I can definitely chip in for you."

Lan Zao shook his head, “Thanks, but I've got enough.”

Spring Boxer felt a pang of regret at missing this chance to extend goodwill to Lan Zao.

“You really are lucky. I'm looking forward to seeing you use that Air Cannon battle skill in the duels.” Spring Boxer expressed his envy.

“Let's keep browsing. Maybe we'll find other good stuff.”

The black market was vast; they'd only covered a third of it so far. Spring Boxer sought another chance to sponsor Lan Zao.

Lan Zao's primary goal for this trip had been achieved. Seeing Spring Boxer's behavior, a sudden thought struck him: Could it be as Shuang Tu had speculated? Was Spring Boxer trying to sponsor me to curry favor, building goodwill to recruit me again later?

He wasn't Cang Xu, nor was he as sharp-witted as Zi Di. It took him this long to grasp the implication.

His mood grew slightly peculiar.

He desperately wanted to blurt out to Spring Boxer: “Hurry up and recruit me! I'd join the Bayonet Gang right now and head to Tranquil Hill with you!”

But Lan Zao knew rushing this matter was unwise. He forced himself to suppress his strange feelings.

Rumble...

Just as the two were about to move forward, the entire underground space suddenly shook violently.

The black market was located within an underground cavern.

A torrent of debris rained down from above.

Chaos erupted throughout the black market.

The enforcement squad sprang into action, bellowing commands as they scrambled to restore order.

Fortunately, the tremor came and went swiftly, and the cavern soon settled back into silence.

“What on earth just happened?” The crowd was understandably unsettled.

A silver level enforcer stepped forward, loudly reassuring everyone that it was merely a normal crustal tremor.

The black market transactions continued uninterrupted.

Some, however, felt uneasy and ventured outside to the surface.

Lan Zao and Spring Boxer were among them.

Those around them were discussing the situation.

“There's a battle happening!”

“That direction... isn't that where Master Cai Jing's alchemy lab is?”

"Could it be that Ba Qi has returned? We haven't heard anything about the Heroism Pirates."

"Wait, this aura?"

The crowd was stunned.

Qian Xing's saint level aura spread out with his attack. Even here in the black market, it could still be sensed.

Spring Boxer and Lan Zao's faces also showed shock.

Saint level!

This was an absolute heavyweight.

Spring Boxer gazed toward the battle's epicenter and sighed deeply, “Seems the rumors about Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong's legacy aren't false after all. Even a saint level being has been drawn here. Master Cai Jing is truly formidable, to withstand a saint level fighter!”

Lan Zao replied, "He's a gold level magician, the next highest level below saint level. Don't forget, the Alchemy Guild's Da Bei has joined his side too."

This news had already spread across Flower Skirt Island and been confirmed.

Lan Zao's eyes flickered as he thought to himself: "My bloodline has been purified, I'm certain to reach silver level someday. But that's still only silver level. Gold level, saint level... Only figures like our commander can reach such levels."

At this thought, Lan Zao's expression grew somewhat complicated. He said to Spring Boxer, "Clashes between such powerful figures are not something we can get involved in. Even spectating carries life-threatening risks. We'd be better off returning to the black market. Finding suitable gear is far more practical than lingering here in speculation."

Spring Boxer nodded in agreement, finding Lan Zao's reasoning sound.

The legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong was undoubtedly valuable, but they had no place in it. In this saga, gold level and saint level were the protagonists. Spring Boxer and his companions didn't even qualify to take the stage.

At their level, they could only look up in awe. This battle would be a rare, spectacular moment in their lives, something to reminisce about in old age or brag about to others when bored.

In truth, they merely caught a distant glimpse from afar. The actual battle scenes would only reach them through hearsay afterward.

The two descended back into the underground cavern.

Soon after, Lan Zao stopped at a stall.

The air was thick with the pungent stench of fish.

The vendor was a fishmonger, specializing in magic beast sea creatures.

He warmly welcomed Lan Zao and Spring Boxer.

After several wild duels, the pair had gained some notoriety within the circle of transcendents on Flower Skirt Island.

Lan Zao was a dark horse who had emerged unexpectedly, his physique and appearance both highly distinctive.

Spring Boxer was a silver level.

Such a transcendent was already considered elite in most circles. In the vast majority of cases. The strongest individuals they encountered in daily life were of this level.

Compared to silver level beings, gold level powerhouses were far fewer in number.

Let alone saint level beings.

The emergence of figures like Master Cai Jing, Magician Da Bei, Ba Qi, and Qian Xing on Flower Skirt Island was purely due to the legacy of Fan Sheng Za Zhong.

Under normal circumstances, a neutral island like Flower Skirt Island wouldn't even warrant a permanent gold level presence.

Lan Zao showed no interest in the dead fish being sold. His daily supply of magic beast meat came from Shuang Tu. Not only did Shuang Tu provide ample food, but they also specially designed nutritionally balanced side dishes for Lan Zao.

Most crucially, Lan Zao didn't have to pay!

Lan Zao showed no interest in either live or dead fish. Pointing at a net piled high with dead fish, he asked, “How much for this gear?”

“Lord Hen Fa, this is my livelihood, I have no intention of selling it,” replied the vendor.

It was an iron level piece of equipment.

Lan Zao, hailing from a fishing village, had spent years making his living at sea and was a master net-caster himself.

He showed keen interest in the net: "If I'm not mistaken, this is an elastic black silk fishing net. My offer already exceeds the market price by nearly double. What do you say?"

The vendor was swayed, but after hesitating, he ultimately shook his head.

Seeing this, Spring Boxer stepped in to help Lan Zao, offering to fund the purchase and driving the price up once more.

But the vendor had made up his mind—he refused to budge.

His reasoning was understandable. This was his livelihood, the gear he depended on to survive. Selling it meant he'd struggle to find a suitable replacement anytime soon.

This would severely impact his fishing efficiency. While selling it could bring in a profit, failing to find a suitable replacement could delay his fishing later and actually lead to losses.

Spring Boxer shook his head helplessly.

Lan Zao could only leave with regret.

On the way, he curiously asked Spring Boxer, " If I joined your gang, could we snatch it from him?"

Spring Boxer immediately looked troubled: “Well...”

After a moment's thought, he decided to be honest: “The Bayonet Gang is indeed a gang. Robbery and bullying? We've done our share of that.”

“But I don't participate.”

“You might not know this, but I once served in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's regular army.”

"Even though I'm a gang member now, I have my own principles. I hope you won't do this either."

“Truth is, once you start down certain paths, it's hard to turn back.”

“You might think I'm hypocritical, a gang member holding onto such principles seems ridiculous.”

“But I feel better sticking to them.”

Lan Zao quietly studied Spring Boxer.

He could sense the sincerity radiating from Spring Boxer.

Combined with Lan Zao's intelligence, he was certain Spring Boxer wasn't lying—this was genuinely his nature!

“Military life forged his foundation and character. Even after falling into gang life, he hasn't lost his integrity.”

In this regard, Lan Zao held great respect for Spring Boxer.

For in Spring Boxer, he saw the reflection of his own commander.

“Our commander is that kind of man too.”

Spring Boxer had been tense, but after speaking honestly, his mood settled.

Lan Zao chuckled, breaking the silence between them, “Lord Spring Boxer, you've truly made me look at you with new respect.”

Spring Boxer smiled back, his words equally heartfelt, “It means so much that you understand me.”

He had thoroughly investigated Hen Fa and knew that in the past, Hen Fa had served as a bodyguard and had no record of any criminal offenses. The so-called crime was merely an act of forced self-defense, an accidental killing driven by anger.

Spring Boxer surveyed the numerous vendors in the black market: “Let me put it this way, if I ever lead the Bayonet Gang, I'll uphold order here, collect protection fees from them, rather than oppressing them with my gang.”

“I believe you, Lord Spring Boxer,” Lan Zao affirmed.

But at that very moment, the black market shook violently once more.

This tremor was far more intense than before. A massive section of the ceiling collapsed, crashing down.

Countless fragments rained overhead, dust billowing as the enforcement squad descended into chaos, utterly unable to maintain control.

Panicked crowds scattered in all directions, rushing toward the exits to escape to the surface.

Amidst this chaos, a group suddenly defied the flow, running downward and bursting into the black market.

At their head stood none other than Xiong Ju!

Xiong Ju spotted Spring Boxer immediately and bellowed at him, "Quick! Someone's selling mithril in the black market. We need it urgently; we must seize it!"

Lan Zao: ?

Spring Boxer: ??!!

Xiong Ju bellowed again, "What are you standing around for? This is the old gang leader's serious order, it must be carried out immediately!"

Spring Boxer's expression darkened. Bing Xiao knew him well yet issued this order. Had something changed?

Gang affairs took precedence over personal convictions.

Without hesitation, he turned and sprinted, shouting, “Follow me! I know where the mithril stall is!”

Chapter 684: Lan Zao – Joining the Bayonet Gang Again

Chaos erupted in the black market.

Unlike last time, the tremors not only failed to subside but grew increasingly violent.

Boulders and pebbles of all sizes rained down relentlessly from above.

Casualties were mounting rapidly.

Some vendors were crushed to death, their merchandise now up for the taking.

Many seized the opportunity to act.

What began as scattered looting spread like sparks igniting oil, a wildfire consuming human greed.

More and more joined the stampede!

A horrific man-made disaster unfolded.

The enforcement squad, tasked with maintaining order, shouted themselves hoarse and intervened repeatedly, still clinging to the futile hope of restoring order to the black market.

The leader of the faction controlling this section of the black market, a silver level fighter, was forced to step forward. He acted decisively, instantly slaying two iron level looters.

This restored order around him, but the overall chaos remained beyond containment.

“Get out of my way!” Xiong Ju charged forward, his aura fierce.

“Step aside if you value your life,” Spring Boxer followed closely at his side.

The two silver levels charged forward, leaving no one standing in their path.

“Lord, look over there!” an enforcement officer alerted his leader.

But the silver level fighter merely glanced in that direction before immediately shifting his gaze. He then directed the enforcement squad to begin their redeployment, moving to suppress a nearby chaotic zone.

Across Flower Skirt Island, aside from masters of gold level like Master Cai Jing, the leaders of all factions were silver level.

Xiong Ju and Spring Boxer teamed up, while the black market had only one silver level fighter in charge. He wisely chose to turn a blind eye.

The shop dealing in mithril was one of the larger establishments in the black market. When they saw Xiong Ju, Spring Boxer, and the others charging in, their faces drained of color.

Yet they didn't simply give up resistance. The shopkeeper bellowed orders to his staff, directing each to grab a batch of goods and flee separately.

“Leave the stuff behind!” Spring Boxer shouted in panic.

“Don't run! Whoever flees dies!” Xiong Ju's eyes blazed with fury as his roar thundered.

The shop's response severely disrupted their robbery. Under these circumstances, it was nearly impossible to identify who had taken the mithril.

Xiong Ju and Spring Boxer were forced to split up, intercepting the fleeing shop assistants separately.

Once intercepted, the shopkeepers wisely abandoned their cargo, hurling it far in another direction. This tactic lured the Bayonet Gang members to retrieve it, buying the shopkeepers precious escape time.

The Bayonet Gang seized multiple shopkeepers and intercepted numerous items, yet the mithril remained elusive.

Xiong Ju charged into the shop like a starving grizzly bear.

Spring Boxer climbed high to survey the area, shouting orders for his men to intercept any stragglers he spotted.

Lan Zao watched the entire scene unfold, hesitating over whether to join the Bayonet Gang's operation.

Truthfully, robbery was something Lan Zao had never engaged in before.

Though born into poverty and having served as a merchant ship's boatswain for years, he had no history of plundering others.

“To complete the task the commander assigned me, I must infiltrate the Bayonet Gang and join their ranks.”

“Now seems like the perfect opportunity. But won't joining their operation arouse suspicion?”

Lan Zao had never robbed before, but to swiftly complete the task the commander had entrusted him with, he was willing to push beyond his limits.

The problem was, Lan Zao had no experience acting as a spy. This made him hesitant when it came to making critical decisions at crucial moments.

He didn't know how to choose, lacked confidence in taking action, and was deeply worried about things going wrong.

Just as he wavered, someone quietly approached him, hiding in a shadowy corner. Through the alchemy device, they whispered urgently: "Hen Fa, what are you standing around for? Hurry up and join them for the robbery!"

Lan Zao whipped his head around to see the reminder was none other than the vendor who’d sold him the alchemy projectile cores earlier.

He recognized the vendor's identity - a member of Shuang Tu's group.

The vendor grew anxious upon seeing him turn: “You fool! Don't look back at me!”

Lan Zao immediately averted his gaze, gritted his teeth, and prepared to dash toward the shop.

The vendor whispered urgently: "Don't go straight there. You're on the outer perimeter now. Several shop assistants from that stall are running toward our direction. Go intercept them and rob the goods they're carrying. You might just find something the Bayonet Gang wants!"

With the warning, Lan Zao sprang into action.

The shop assistants who got intercepted were already panicking. Seeing Lan Zao suddenly appear, they trembled violently, as if struck by lightning.

Lan Zao actually didn't want to get blood on his hands over a robbery. So he shouted in the name of the Bayonet Gang: “Leave the goods for the Bayonet Gang!”

The clerks, intimidated by his bluff, genuinely believed Lan Zao was part of the Bayonet Gang. With no thought of resistance, they dropped the goods and fled in panic.

They were just ordinary shop clerks, earning meager wages. There was no reason to risk their lives for the shopkeeper's merchandise.

Lan Zao thus easily seized the items from the three clerks.

Luck was on his side, among the loot was the very mithril the Bayonet Gang desperately needed!

Xiong Ju ransacked the shop but found nothing. Spring Boxer, however, spotted Lan Zao seizing the mithril and rushed over immediately.

“Hen Fa!” Spring Boxer shouted, “That mithril in your hands is exactly what we're after.”

“Here!” Lan Zao didn't hesitate, tossing the pile of mithril straight to Spring Boxer.

Spring Boxer hurriedly thanked him, his face flushing with embarrassment.

Not long ago, he had just explained his vision for governing the gang to Lan Zao. Now, in the blink of an eye, he was leading a robbery, making his earlier words seem like utter nonsense.

Xiong Ju arrived just then, catching Lan Zao tossing the mithril to Spring Boxer. He burst out laughing, "Hen Fa, well done. Bringing you out of Snow Bird Harbor was worth it after all."

Xiong Ju recognized Lan Zao too.

Lan Zao shrugged. "We're all friends here. A chance like this to strike it rich doesn't come often. We can keep working together!"

This was an unusually eloquent moment for Lan Zao.

His single sentence perfectly encapsulated his actions.

Shuang Tu would likely have been pleased to hear it. This was the fruit of his earlier efforts in secretly cultivating Lan Zao.

“Hen Fa, you...” Spring Boxer started, then trailed off. He desperately wanted to persuade Lan Zao, but knew his own recent behavior left him no standing to do so.

Xiong Ju shook his head: “We have urgent business to attend to. We must get back immediately.”

Lan Zao froze.

His experience in undercover work was woefully lacking.

For a moment, he didn't know how to respond.

Lan Zao desperately wanted to join the Bayonet Gang right then and there.

But in the end, he sighed: "That’s truly unfortunate. I’ll be off to attend to my business."

Lan Zao was about to part ways to rob other places—that was his most natural course of action.

But Spring Boxer stopped him: "Don’t go operating alone anymore. Our activities have long been under the watchful eye of the black market administrator. Only our formidable strength has kept him hesitant to act."

"They likely suspect you're part of our group. If you go it alone now, they'll almost certainly come after you."

Spring Boxer didn't want to see Lan Zao continue his robberies. Before this, he had admired Lan Zao. In a way, he didn't want to see him “fall” like this.

Still, Spring Boxer's reasoning made sense.

The silver level black market administrator had already suppressed two territories, yet he never took his eyes off the Bayonet Gang members.

Lan Zao froze again.

He couldn't help but glance at Spring Boxer, feeling a surge of surprise and delight.

He hadn't expected Spring Boxer to speak up at this critical moment. This provided Lan Zao with a perfectly suitable justification to continue aligning himself with the Bayonet Gang.

“Spring Boxer is a real good guy!” he thought to himself, nodding, “Alright, I'll go with you.”

When Spring Boxer persuaded Lan Zao, he didn’t feel very confident. But hearing him agree brought immediate relief.

Spring Boxer felt he hadn't misjudged the man and was also glad he'd successfully prevented someone from falling into ruin.

He walked up to Lan Zao and excitedly slapped the latter on the shoulder: “Stay close to me!”

Lan Zao joined the Bayonet Gang members and turned to leave the black market.

Braving the relentless rain of falling rocks and stepping on precariously swaying floor tiles, the group quickly reached the exit.

They saw the staircase leading to the surface had already collapsed halfway. Amidst a pile of rubble lay the body of a transcendent.

Lan Zao recognized the man, it was the vendor from the fish stall earlier.

He had once tried to buy the elastic black silk fishing net, only to be rejected.

His eyes lit up. He quickly jogged over, bent down, and retrieved a bundle of black fishing net from the vendor's waist.

“Who'd have thought there'd be a bonus!” Lan Zao laughed heartily, genuinely pleased.

Spring Boxer glanced at him without saying much.

“Boss, the Bayonet Gang guys all got out,” a subordinate reported.

Truthfully, he didn't need the report. The black market administrator had kept his attention fixed on Spring Boxer and his crew the entire time.

His face darkened as he stared toward the exit, "Bayonet Gang... Don't think you can just grab the goods and run. This isn't over!"

He hadn't moved against the Bayonet Gang members earlier only because his forces were outnumbered.

That didn't mean he'd simply abandon his pursuit of them.

The Bayonet Gang's actions had severely disrupted the order of Flower Skirt Island and threatened his interests!

His side might be outmatched, but he could easily rally other factions to unite against the Bayonet Gang.

The Bayonet Gang's establishment of temporary strongholds across Flower Skirt Island had already raised the vigilance of nearly every silver level faction.

Now, its members were exploiting chaos to loot the black market. Such egregious behavior constituted a grave provocation to Flower Skirt Island's other powers.

The black market administrator held the moral high ground. Even silver level factions untouched by the incident would grow deeply wary and anxious, They feared becoming the next victims.

Thus, the black market administrator had long resolved to unite with other factions afterward to confront the Bayonet Gang. Only by eliminating every last one of these damned scoundrels could he vent his fury and hatred!

Chapter 685: Cang Xu – I Still Have One Small Final Wish

The Bayonet Gang members quickly scrambled to the ground.

Spring Boxer and Lan Zao were astonished to find that the ground outside the black market showed no signs of severe damage, it seemed the earthquake had been confined solely within the black market.

“Strange. What exactly happened?”

“Did someone deliberately destroy the black market?”

Xiong Ju shook his head. "No, that's not it. The stronghold over there was destroyed too. Let's hurry back. The boss and the others need us to return immediately."

The group sprinted at full speed and soon reached the stronghold.

All the buildings near the stronghold had collapsed, turning the area into a vast expanse of rubble. The casualties were horrific, with countless people buried beneath the debris.

The scale of destruction far exceeded that of the black market, and the Bayonet Gang's temporary base lay buried within it.

Bing Xiao and the human ringleader had remained behind at the base.

Bing Xiao's expression was grim.

The moment he sensed the battle between Qian Xing and Master Cai Jing; he immediately ordered all personnel to withdraw and fortify the base defensively.

Bing Xiao feared this battle would trigger island-wide turmoil. Amidst the chaos, opportunists might exploit the situation to raid the Bayonet Gang's temporary stronghold.

This was their sole remaining base.

As the veteran old gang leader, Bing Xiao understood the Bayonet Gang's precarious position on Flower Skirt Island.

The existing silver level factions had already carved up the island. The Bayonet Gang was an outsider, and the arrival of several silver level members had severely disrupted the established order.

The Bayonet Gang had not truly integrated into Flower Skirt Island. To achieve genuine integration, the gang would inevitably face a baptism of blood and fire, needing to displace the existing powers to secure their position.

Therefore, Bing Xiao's order was exceptionally prudent.

He deeply feared that amidst the chaos, rival factions might unite to target the Bayonet Gang, disguising themselves as rioters to assault their stronghold.

The stronghold itself seemed insignificant, even if overrun, the Bayonet Gang could easily relocate to another temporary base.

Yet, in Bing Xiao's view, this stronghold was not to be lost lightly.

Three primary reasons justified this stance.

First, the basement housed the Bayonet Gang's funds, vast cultivation resources, weaponry, and equipment. Most crucially, it contained the ice coffin holding Teng Dong Lang's corpse.

Second, the Gang members had fled from Snow Bird Harbor. Despite recuperating, their morale remained low. Should they fail to defend even their sole stronghold on a place like Flower Skirt Island, it would deal a severe blow to their morale. For the current Bayonet Gang, this would be adding insult to injury.

Third, Bing Xiao desired the Bayonet Gang to establish a foothold on Flower Skirt Island. Failing to hold their only stronghold would project an image of weakness and vulnerability to other factions. Establishing a foothold later would demand greater sacrifices and bloodshed.

Bing Xiao's proactive decision to contract defenses demonstrated foresight and seasoned prudence.

Yet he never anticipated the stronghold itself would face such devastating impact. The earthquake struck with sudden, earth-shaking ferocity!

Buildings collapsed without exception, and the once level ground became uneven and scarred.

Within the Bayonet Gang's temporary stronghold, a rudimentary protective formation had been established, offering some degree of defense. Yet under such overwhelming force, this defense proved as flimsy as paper, utterly useless.

Most critically, the ice coffin hidden in the basement was also damaged.

A corner of the ice coffin shattered, nearly severing Teng Dong Lang's lower leg from his knee.

This turn of events pushed Bing Xiao to the edge of a cliff.

His journey to Tranquil Hill hinged on Teng Dong Lang's body remaining intact.

When he had retrieved the corpse from Snow Bird Harbor, he had been immensely relieved to find it undamaged.

"Teng Dong Lang's body has been damaged. Time is running out for me. The only path left is to reach Tranquil Hill as quickly as possible!"

Cruel reality had severely disrupted Bing Xiao's plans.

He had no choice but to accelerate everything, pushing forward with the Tranquil Hill operation at all costs.

Fortunately, during his time on Flower Skirt Island, his primary focus had been advancing this very plan.

Through wild duels, he had gathered most of the materials.

The only remaining key ingredient was mithril.

Thus, the entire plan was already nearing its final stage.

However, Bing Xiao required a substantial quantity of mithril, a rare metal that proved difficult to acquire through conventional channels.

Bing Xiao had previously discovered that large quantities of mithril were being sold on the black market. Unfortunately, the gang's funds were insufficient to purchase the amount he needed.

Mithril was prohibitively expensive.

Thus, Bing Xiao reluctantly ordered Xiong Ju to raid the black market and seize sufficient quantities by force.

“May their mission succeed,” Bing Xiao silently prayed, weighed down by pressure. "Otherwise, everything will be irreversibly lost. All our prior efforts will be for naught."

Spring Boxer, Xiong Ju, and the others returned with ample mithril, finally easing Bing Xiao's worries.

“Well done!” Bing Xiao praised.

Xiong Ju pointed at Lan Zao: “We also owe it to Hen Fa. He was the one who intercepted the shop assistant fleeing with the mithril.”

Bing Xiao smiled and nodded at Lan Zao.

Lan Zao chuckled. "I was just trying to get rich; I happened to snatch the mithril by accident. Since that's the case, I'll be on my way."

A flicker of concern crossed Spring Boxer's face, but he couldn't stop Lan Zao from leaving. He could only caution, "Then you'd better be careful lately. After all, they might mistake you for one of the Bayonet Gang."

Hearing this, Bing Xiao's heart stirred.

A seasoned veteran, he immediately blocked Lan Zao's path: “Hen Fa, don't rush off. You helped us this time, we can't let you face danger alone. You came to the island with us, and now you've helped seize the mithril. To others, you're clearly one of us!”

“If you leave alone now, they might not come after us immediately, but they'll definitely target you first. Though your strength has grown rapidly, you're still only iron level and alone. You'd be easy prey for ambushes and traps.”

“Here's what we'll do: stay with us for now. Once this chaos passes and their suspicions fade, you can act freely again. This is the safest course.”

"You've had it rough. Luckily, you've awakened your bloodline. Don't get overconfident and doom yourself. Your future is still bright. The view from the silver level is far prettier than iron!"

Bing Xiao delivered a heartfelt speech.

His real goal was to keep Lan Zao firmly within the Bayonet Gang.

Now was the perfect opportunity!

His words held genuine merit, not mere empty flattery.

By openly raiding the black market, the Bayonet Gang had crossed a major line. They were bound to face joint accountability from other silver level factions.

The Bayonet Gang had long been a thorn in the side of every power on Flower Skirt Island.

After all, boasting multiple silver level members, their formidable strength inspired fear and envy.
