Their latest act constituted a severe provocation against Flower Skirt Island's established order.

The island's silver level factions were known for their cooperative nature.

Faced with such a formidable adversary as the Bayonet Gang, they would undoubtedly unite to confront them.

Lan Zao had already intended to stay and join the Bayonet Gang. After hearing Bing Xiao's words, he furrowed his brow, deep in thought. Then, he finally nodded.

Bing Xiao patted Lan Zao on the shoulder, settling him in. He then ordered Xiong Ju, the human ringleader, and others to defend the perimeter of their stronghold.

Only then did he enter the basement, accompanied solely by Spring Boxer.

Upon entering the basement, Spring Boxer gasped and hurried to the damaged ice coffin.

In Spring Boxer's heart, Teng Dong Lang was like a second father. The latter had always shown him immense care and spared no effort in nurturing his growth.

The sight of Teng Dong Lang's damaged body filled Spring Boxer's face with profound sorrow.

“Alright, kid, now's not the time to wallow in sorrow,” Bing Xiao reminded him, “We need to hurry.”

"Now, do exactly as I say. Watch every step I take carefully and do your absolute best to commit them to memory. You'll likely need them yourself someday," Bing Xiao instructed.

Bing Xiao began processing the various alchemy materials stored in the basement.

Spring Boxer immediately noticed that Bing Xiao was primarily using the rare materials he had won from the wild duel.

Spring Boxer couldn't help but feel curious. While arranging the materials as Bing Xiao instructed, he asked him the reason for this action.

Without turning his head, Bing Xiao replied while working: “This way, we can go to Tranquil Hill.”

"Go to Tranquil Hill? With these?" Spring Boxer was utterly surprised.

He had always assumed they would leave Flower Skirt Island by ship, gradually approaching Tranquil Hill.

“Old gang leader, are we building a teleportation formation?” Spring Boxer asked again.

"Of course not! I'm no magician, just a fighter. We're setting up a priest's ritual."

"A ritual? What kind of ritual could transport us directly to Tranquil Hill?"

“A special ritual, naturally! I can't explain the details clearly, but you'll understand once you experience it." Bing Xiao stated.

While Spring Boxer and Bing Xiao prepared for the special ritual with intense anticipation, far away in the depths of the ocean near the Mother Nest, another group faced their own unique ritual.

The difference was that this group's mood was utterly dreadful.

Zhou Zhang observed the necromancers with keen interest as she delivered her ultimatum: “The next ritual is the teleportation blood sacrifice. Have you selected your first candidate?”

The undead, led by Jian Gui, wore expressions so grim they were frightening.

Their gazes instinctively converged on Cang Xu.

Cang Xu's expression was grim. He closed his eyes slightly before slowly opening them again. He looked at Zhou Zhang, his voice hoarse and strained as he said, “It will be me.”

“Hehehe, I hope you succeed in converting!” Zhou Zhang chuckled for a moment before strolling away leisurely.

Dead silence fell over the shack.

“Everyone... please be at ease,” Cang Xu opened his mouth, his face bitter. "According to our agreement, I'm the one with the slowest progress. I won't go back on my word. I'll be the first to participate in the blood sacrifice."

Xiao Wang Zi turned his head away, unable to bear the sight.

Qi Ling averted her gaze.

Ku Feng, however, gazed steadily at Cang Xu and declared, “I will be the second.”

This was certain.

As a fighter, his progress in forging memories could never match that of Xiao Wang Zi or Qi Ling.

And after Cang Xu, Xiao Wang Zi and Qi Ling would certainly not aid him further.

The reason was simple.

Xiao Wang Zi was already struggling to keep up, compelled to devote all his efforts to competing with Qi Ling.

As for Qi Ling, determined to hold onto her position, how could she possibly spare the energy and time to aid Ku Feng?

Cang Xu nodded.

After everyone had departed, he returned alone to the shack to see Jian Gui.

“I never imagined death would feel like this,” Cang Xu's opening words drew a deep sigh from Jian Gui.

“What is it like?” Jian Gui inquired.

“Helplessness, despair... yet perhaps because I anticipated it, I remain strangely calm,” Cang Xu replied, his expression complex.

Jian Gui gazed at him, certain lingering concerns gradually dissipating.

Facing death, Cang Xu might trade the secret of fabricating memories to Zhou Zhang for a sliver of survival.

Jian Gui had always guarded against this possibility.

“You may not die,” Jian Gui offered reassurance, “there is still hope.”

“Convert my faith?” Cang Xu smiled faintly, “Perhaps.”

“Lord Jian Gui, before I die, I have one small wish.”

“Speak.” Jian Gui sensed what was coming.

Sure enough, Cang Xu's request matched his expectations.

At life's end, Cang Xu wished to peruse the soul aspect inheritance.

“Especially that spell for detecting undead bloodlines...”

"Lord Jian Gui, forgive my audacity. Though my bloodline mutation remains incomplete, it has indeed reached its final stage. I am truly curious, what exactly is my undead bloodline?"

“Though I lack your ability to break through the seals of divine spells and magic spells for fleeting freedom...”

“Even in death, I wish to carry these memories with me.”

“Perhaps this is the lingering regret of my life, my sole regret before dying.”

"Or perhaps I truly can convert successfully. Upon my return, these spells would be my reward for enduring such peril. Lord Jian Gui, I know this is presumptuous, but would you grant me these blessings?"

Jian Gui sighed again.

Facing imminent death, Cang Xu's boldness no longer seemed presumptuous, it felt entirely justified.

Most crucially, his words struck a deep chord within Jian Gui.

For the predicament Cang Xu now faced, Jian Gui would inevitably face it too!

The present Cang Xu was the future Jian Gui.

In truth, even with fabricated memories, Jian Gui lacked the confidence to escape unscathed. Facing such precarious danger, he willingly set aside some of his steadfast principles to accept this pitiful old man's “audacity.”

After all, he had already compromised his principles before.

With the first compromise, the second came far easier.

Moreover, Jian Gui also wished to stabilize Cang Xu, ensuring the latter maintained the secret of the fabricated memories as much as possible. Though he could kill Cang Xu, it was best not to risk arousing Zhou Zhang's suspicion over it.

“Very well. I shall impart to you the entirety of the intermediate soul aspect inheritance.”

“Xu Jia, I am truly sorry.”

Jian Gui bowed his head to Cang Xu with utmost sincerity, performing a magician's salute.

Moments later, Cang Xu departed Jian Gui's hut with satisfaction.

“All my goals have been accomplished.”

“I never imagined it would happen under these circumstances, heh.”

“Now, I'm truly looking forward to meeting up with the commander.”

Cang Xu gazed distantly toward the golden altar, where vast numbers of sea beasts had been driven together. An eager anticipation welled up within him, yearning to reunite with the dragonman youngster once more.

Chapter 686: The Fishman Youngster and Cang Xu Converge

White Hair Trench.

Deep beneath the ocean.

A colossal magic formation had activated, emitting a faint hum.

Countless magic runes and divine inscriptions intertwined, illuminating the seabed like a brightly lit city.

The radiance fell upon every face of the Charming Blue believers, highlighting expressions ranging from devout reverence to fanatical fervor, even bordering on grotesque.

The necromancers stood out starkly from the crowd.

Their expressions were uniformly grave and somber, tinged with an unmistakable layer of fear.

Similar sentiments were shared by pirates like Ba Qi, Lady Sea Snake, and Ruan Tang Ren.

Qing Xin stood beside the pirates yet maintained a certain distance.

Her face was deathly pale.

Though captured alongside the necromancers, upon imprisonment she had been assigned to the human pirates' section.

Zhou Zhang employed the same strategy with these human pirates as she had with the necromancers.

Under the leadership of Jian Gui, the necromancers adopted a relatively fair policy, ultimately selecting Cang Xu as their first offering.

Among the human pirates, however, no such arrangement existed.

Qing Xin had no ties to these men. Unquestionably, she became the first to be selected.

Her only solace was that Zhou Zhang had designated Cang Xu alone for this special blood sacrifice, allowing the other captives to observe.

“After Xu Jia, the next captive to participate in the teleportation blood sacrifice will be me.” This thought weighed heavily on Qing Xin's heart, filling her with utter despair.

Truthfully, her recent experiences had been truly miserable.

Originally, Zi Di had summoned Qing Xin back to her side with genuine intentions to aid her and unite their forces.

However, the identity Zi Di revealed to Qing Xin had little connection to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, in fact, relations were rather strained.

Zi Di feared the survivors' secrets might be exposed, making it inconvenient to disclose her affiliation with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to Qing Xin just yet.

Until Qing Xin proved trustworthy, she could only delay.

This delay stretched until Qing Xin and Cang Xu joined Jian Gui.

It stretched until the battle at the Underworld River.

It stretched until the clash with Ba Qi Pirates, culminating in her capture by Zhou Zhang.

Now, Qing Xin sat imprisoned, converting being her only chance of escape.

It should be noted that Qing Xin was not merely a transcendent, but also a silver level magician. In a place like Flower Skirt Island, she could have carved out her own domain and led a minor faction.

But here, under Zhou Zhang's watchful eye, she could only struggle on the brink of life and death, utterly stripped of freedom and hope.

The blood sacrifice was underway.

Countless sea beasts vanished into nothingness upon the golden altar.

Carefully selected believers knelt in a large circle around the golden altar. Some trembled slightly, praying aloud. Others wore expressions of sheer tension—after all, this was a matter of life and death. Yet a few remained calm and composed.

Cang Xu was among them.

He was so distinct that countless eyes were fixed upon him.

“Looks like this utterly wretched necromancer is the first one they've discarded.”

“He's as good as dead!”

“Don't underestimate him. Perhaps he'll convert successfully.”

“Even if he does convert, I wouldn't want a necromancer as one of our faithful.”

The Charming Blue believers whispered among themselves.

“Xu Jia...” Xiao Wang Zi murmured, watching Cang Xu's retreating figure with deep remorse.

Standing beside him was Ku Feng. The necromancer warrior felt no guilt whatsoever, yet he admired Cang Xu's composure immensely.

Cang Xu's expression remained impassive, his composed demeanor radiating outward so distinctly it seemed he'd never participated in the blood sacrifice ritual at all.

He even had the leisure to glance around.

When his gaze fell upon Qing Xin, it lingered momentarily.

Her face was grim, exposing the despair filling the young magician's heart.

Cang Xu desperately wanted to comfort Qing Xin but ultimately refrained. Offering consolation without cause would expose a flaw, raising suspicions among Jian Gui, Zhou Zhang, and the others.

Likewise, without revealing a big secret, Qing Xin would never believe his words of comfort. If Qing Xin didn't believe him, any consolation would be utterly futile.

Cang Xu would never waste his time on futile endeavors.

Zhou Zhang stood atop the golden altar, surveying the scene below.

Her attention did not linger on Cang Xu.

She had meticulously arranged for the necromancers, yet remained remarkably composed, harboring no illusions that these people would truly convert.

After all, even if their conversion failed, they would still become sacrifices.

Zhou Zhang's true concern lay buried deep within her heart: “This blood sacrifice demands far more offerings than the last, yet the frequency of its transmission has slowed.”

"Could my Lord's injuries have worsened? Does she require greater divine power to stabilize her condition?"

The true reason was not Zhou Zhang's wild conjecture.

Rather, it lay with the divine artifact, Mermaid Fairy Tale, aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, which had been continuously siphoning divine power from the Ocean Mother Nest.

By now, the shell-like Mermaid Fairy Tale could no longer contain all the Pearl Bubbles.

Much of it had overflowed from the shell, scattering across the surrounding tabletops.

Zhou Zhang could never have imagined that the grand thief stealing divine power lurked right under her nose, concealed within the Ocean Mother Nest. Even with a saint level Snow Pear Octopus guarding it, the theft went undetected.

When the spatial fluctuations peaked, Cang Xu and the other Charming Blue believers were teleported away, vanishing from their positions.

“They're here!” The dragonman youngster aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish had long been on high alert, prepared for this moment.

The moment the Charming Blue believers teleported into the Ocean Mother Nest, he pinpointed Cang Xu's exact location.

Several Rubber Swallower-type alchemy puppets sprang into action.

These puppets had been lying in ambush nearby for quite some time.

Each was imbued with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

One Rubber Swallower opened its maw, spewing forth a torrent of magic potion. The potion spread like blood mist, instantly shrouding a vast area, preventing the believers from detecting any nearby anomalies.

Then, another Rubber Swallower expelled an alchemy puppet. This puppet bore the exact likeness of Cang Xu.

The real Cang Xu, meanwhile, was swallowed whole by a third Rubber Swallower.

The Charming Blue believers, teleported to the scene, hadn't even registered their surroundings before being engulfed by the blood mist.

The mist quickly dissipated. The group frantically scanned their surroundings, utterly unprepared for the fact that the nearby Cang Xu had been replaced. Their gazes were swiftly drawn to the saint level sea beast, eliciting gasps of astonishment from many.

The Rubber Swallower that had devoured Cang Xu submerged into the sea, swimming downstream until it entered the Deep Sea Monster Fish vessel.

Shortly after, Cang Xu emerged from the landing pod and encountered the dragonman youngster.

“Commander!” Cang Xu bowed deeply to the dragonman youngster, his expression filled with excitement.

The dragonman youngster laughed heartily at Cang Xu, a flash of crimson light erupting from his body as he shifted from dragonman form to fishman form.

The youngster introduced himself proactively, "This is my newest form. My previous dragonman form was simply too disadvantageous for naval combat. Time is extremely limited. Let's talk as we walk."

Cang Xu knew of the Bloodcore. During previous communications with the youngster, he had also learned of the latter's fishman form.

Cang Xu followed closely behind the dragonman youngster into the metallic passageway.

Along the way, they reached the chamber housing the divine artifact, the Mermaid Fairy Tale.

He witnessed it firsthand: the shell overflowing with Pearl Bubbles, much of it scattered across the tabletop.

The old magician exclaimed sincerely, "I've never seen such an abundance of Pearl Bubbles! This single discovery alone makes our journey worthwhile!"

The fishman youngster remained composed: “None of that matters until we're safely out of here.”

For now, they'd confirmed the Deceptive Disguise spell still worked against Zhou Zhang.

But it wasn't foolproof.

They'd deceived Zhou Zhang in her normal state.

If she sensed something amiss and used investigative divine magic, she could still break through the Deceptive Disguise spell.

During their covert communication, the fishman youngster and Cang Xu reached a consensus.

Take this Ocean Mother Nest, for instance.

It was an exceptional source for replenishing Pearl Bubbles!

In fact, it was the only source the survivors had unexpectedly discovered as of now.

If the fishman youngster destroyed the nest outright to secure their escape, it would not only be risky but also a tremendous waste.

Cang Xu believed: the Ocean Mother Nest should be preserved as much as possible. The survivors' heavy reliance on the Deceptive Disguise spell meant that the Pearl Bubbles, the god level material, was an absolute necessity!

The survivors still didn't know how long the plan to atone to the Empire's higher-ups would take, or if it would ultimately succeed.

Therefore, more Pearl Bubbles were always better.

This meant that preserving the Ocean Mother Nest was highly advantageous for the survivors.

Under these circumstances, the fishman youngster did not rashly destroy the Ocean Mother Nest. Instead, he patiently waited for Cang Xu, placing his greatest hope for escape on the latter.

The two made their way forward until they reached a compartment.

The metal hatch slid open to the side, and they entered the chamber.

This was the place where the fishman youngster had long prepared for Cang Xu.

The memory fabrication device lay within, awaiting Cang Xu's operation.

"I have already tested this alchemy apparatus. Using the inheritance content you passed on, I can fabricate memories with this device. The fabricated memories we've designed are now complete." Though merely a fighter, the fishman youngster could efficiently forge memories with this apparatus.

Indeed, this was precisely the purpose of alchemy devices.

Like spell scrolls or enchanted weapons, these devices allowed fighters to wield methods typically reserved for magicians.

The memory fabrication device, invented by You Shen, proved immensely helpful in fabricating memories. Even a gold level magician like Jian Gui couldn't stop thinking about it, once sighing: “If only I could use such a device, why would I need to collaborate with others to forge memories!”

Chapter 687: One Point Star

Cang Xu attempted to operate the memory fabrication device.

After inspecting it, he nodded and smiled, “The fabricated memories are flawless. Commander, you've been a tremendous help this time!”

The fishman youngster shrugged. "This alchemy device is the real hero here, while the undead inheritance you acquired was the key. With these two elements, anyone could have succeeded. If Zhong Yong had done it, he'd have been even more efficient than me."

“Zhong Yong, huh... Too bad I can't meet him just yet.” Cang Xu's expression softened with a hint of regret.

He'd learned of Zhong Yong's existence long ago through his conversations with the fishman youngster.

As the ship's alchemist magician, Zhong Yong was a permanent crew member aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish. Neither the fishman youngster's return with Cang Xu nor Cang Xu's boarding had been revealed to Zhong Yong.

After all, Cang Xu was a necromancer. Zhong Yong needed time to mentally prepare before he could fully accept Cang Xu as a comrade.

Cang Xu quickly mastered the operation of the memory fabrication device.

On one hand, he experimented with the alchemy device multiple times after leaving Mystifying Monster Island. On the other hand, after becoming a captive, he had repeatedly studied the contents of the necromancer inheritance.

The memory fabrication device was incredibly simple to operate, so straightforward even a fool could use it.

Otherwise, the fishman youngster wouldn't have succeeded so easily in fabricating a memory.

“Next, I must set up the Memory Switching Spell and employ the Subconscious Reminder Spell!” Cang Xu crossed his legs and began meditating.

These two spells were the true test for him.

Only by combining them could he awaken independently after using the fabricated memory and reclaim his true identity.

While awaiting the teleportation blood sacrifice, Cang Xu had spent considerable time secretly studying these two spells, becoming thoroughly familiar with them.

But familiarity was one thing; actually casting the spells was another.

The greatest difficulty lay in Cang Xu still being bound by dual restrictions, both magical and divine.

These seals originated from Zhou Zhang and were of the saint level.

Cang Xu naturally couldn't break them, and the fishman youngster was powerless to help.

Whether it was memory forgery, or the Memory Switching and Subconscious Reminder spells, they all acted upon the soul. Though they would be disrupted by the dual seals, the latter couldn't completely prevent them.

Before this, necromancers had always been able to forge memories in secret. Now that Cang Xu had retreated into the Deep Sea Monster Fish, he could operate freely.

Seeing Cang Xu giving his all, the fishman youngster tactfully withdrew from the cabin and closed the hatch.

“Cang Xu, it's all up to you now. Hope everything goes smoothly!”

The fishman youngster silently offered his blessing before immediately returning to the cockpit to monitor the situation at the Ocean Mother Nest.

The saint level sea beast, Snow Pear Octopus, feasted voraciously on the teleported sea creatures.

As before, the Charming Blue believers cowered in every corner of the Mother Nest. Having quickly adapted, they now prayed frantically, consumed by their own fears.

Flower Skirt Island.

Qian Xing stared at the hemispherical light barrier beneath his feet, his eyes flickering with growing murderous intent.

He had unleashed multiple battle skills, yet the barrier remained intact.

However, he had discerned the mechanism: “So this formation can dissipate energy. The earth vein serves as its dissipation channel, that explains the violent earthquakes occurring elsewhere on Flower Skirt Island.”

Though not a magician, Qian Xing possessed broad knowledge.

Moreover, the seismic activity across Flower Skirt Island was undeniably intense, making it impossible to ignore.

"Cai Jing, you are truly cruel and ruthless. I wonder how the others on the island would react if they knew you used them as sacrificial lambs. What would they think?" Qian Xing sneered, attempting to undermine his opponent's morale with words.

"A bunch of weaklings. Their feelings are irrelevant. Besides, Flower Skirt Island was unclaimed territory to begin with. What business is it of theirs if I use the earth vein? Casualties? Just bad luck. They happened to be standing on the nodes of the earth vein." Master Cai Jing spoke coldly, unmoved.

“Heh, well said.” Qian Xing sneered, “Now I'd rather pierce your skull and watch your brain matter ooze out.”

As he spoke, Qian Xing extended his right index finger, pointing it toward the hemispherical light barrier below.

He drew a deep breath, channeling his battle qi, pouring it in with all his might. A torrent of battle qi concentrated on the nail of his right index finger, compressing relentlessly, compressing further.

The aura of a saint level cultivator surged steadily.

Master Cai Jing and Magician Da Bei wore solemn expressions, tension mounting within them.

Throughout the entire battle, they had never witnessed Qian Xing mobilize such immense force or adopt such a formidable stance to gather power.

Magician Da Bei hastily cast his spell.

But Qian Xing, having resolved to build his momentum, would not be so easily disrupted.

He immediately evaded, continuously employing his flight battle skill. With extraordinary agility, he dodged every magic attack.

Battle skill - One Point Star!

Like a droplet of water, a blue starlight fell from the nail of Qian Xing's right index finger.

The starlight had been contained within him, but the moment it left his fingernail, it burst forth with boundless brilliance.

Blindingly bright, it reached its target in an instant, colliding with the hemispherical light barrier at an unimaginable speed!

Unlike previous attacks, the One Point Star pierced straight through the barrier, then shot toward Master Cai Jing with lightning speed.

Struck by the starlight, Master Cai Jing's head was pierced clean through from front to back. His entire form dissolved into a fading shadow, slowly dissipating.

This was not Master Cai Jing's true form!

A magician's true body was always their weak point, and even a gold level magician like Cai Jing possessed this characteristic.

Unlike Da Bei, his combat prowess was formidable. Long before his public appearance, he had concealed his true form, revealing only a deceptive illusion as bait.

Qian Xing struck the enemy, yet his effort proved futile, prompting a cold snort.

He hadn't seen through Cai Jing's illusion because of the effect of the latter's formation.

But it didn't matter. The battle skill “One Point Star” pierced through the surface, something a hemispherical light shield couldn't block.

Qian Xing continued to unleash this battle skill.

This time, however, he extended the middle finger of his right hand.

The nail on his index finger had grown dull and dark, as if petrified, unable to sustain the release cost of the One Point Star battle skill repeatedly in the short term.

“Damn it, I can't let him attack so freely!” Master Cai Jing cursed inwardly, feeling immense pressure.

He exerted all his strength to control the formation, forcing it to unleash its maximum defensive power.

Though this action pushed the formation's consumption of elemental crystals to its absolute limit.

Yet, as long as it could defend against Qian Xing, it would be a success for Cai Jing.

Using this formation burned resources at an alarming rate. But converting that expenditure into combat power was infinitely preferable to wasting it!

Simultaneously, Master Cai Jing mentally commanded numerous magic trumpet flowers to burst from the ground.

Nearly a hundred giant trumpet flowers appeared, encircling Master Cai Jing's alchemy chamber.

During the battle against Ba Qi, the trumpet flowers performed remarkably well.

This prompted Master Cai Jing to cultivate more trumpet flowers after the battle, bolstering his defenses.

The trumpet flowers formed a floral formation, continuously spewing streams of potions.

"Multicolored potions?!" Qian Xing's pupils contracted sharply as he dodged swiftly.

Shuang Tu had provided intelligence on the multicolored potion while summoning Qian Xing for aid in the name of Ba Qi.

Thus, Qian Xing knew that contact with this potion would alter the target's battle qi into another type.

Conflict between different battle qi existed, and Qian Xing had no desire to find himself in such a predicament.

As a saint level fighter, any transformed battle qi would also be of saint level. This posed a significant internal threat to him.

Qian Xing dodged nimbly through the air, evading every splash of the multicolored potions.

When evasion was impossible, he instantly condensed his gold level battle qi into shields, like umbrellas or walls, to block the splashes in advance.

Yet the gold level battle qi quickly disintegrated upon contact with the potions, fragmenting into clusters of foreign battle qi.

Though Qian Xing could manipulate these foreign energies, their control proved limited and difficult to harness effectively.

The trumpet flower formation and multicolored potions indeed caused considerable disruption for Qian Xing.

Still, this struggle did little to help Cai Jing, Da Bei, and the others reverse their disadvantage.

Qian Xing repeatedly unleashed his One Point Star battle skill, successively destroying multiple nodes of the light shield formation.

Master Cai Jing wanted to repair them but dared not.

If he revealed his true form, he would struggle to survive under the One Point Star battle skill.

Ultimately, after multiple nodes were destroyed, Qian Xing located the formation's core. With an incredibly precise One Point Star, he breached the formation's core.

An intense spatial fluctuation occurred.

Master Cai Jing, Magician Da Bei, and Magician Zhong Bei evacuated Flower Skirt Island at the last moment via the teleportation formation.

Qian Xing's expression darkened.

He had gone to great lengths to breach the formation's light barrier, only to let these magicians escape.

Magicians possessed a wealth of techniques, and when prepared, they were indeed highly skilled at preserving their lives.

Master Cai Jing paid a tremendous price to lay multiple magic formations here and cultivate nearly a hundred magic plants. It was precisely these preparations that allowed him to withstand Qian Xing's assault for a short time and ultimately escape successfully.

Had he constructed an exceptional mage tower here, he could have even relied on his gold level strength to resist a Saint level opponent for an extended period.

Qian Xing charged down into the underground chambers, where he discovered the open secret door of inheritance.

Approaching the door, he received the message left behind by the late Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong.

“Cai Jing and Da Bei have obtained the inheritance!” Qian Xing's face turned ashen.

Filled with resentment, he turned his attention back to the teleportation formation.

He found it had already self-destructed. With his abilities alone, it was nearly impossible to deduce Cai Jing and the others' teleportation coordinates from the wreckage.

"Damn it! Those rats actually got away." Seething with resentment, Qian Xing shot skyward, randomly choosing a direction. Like a meteor piercing the heavens, he left Flower Skirt Island behind.

He refused to give up, determined to try his luck elsewhere.

The potion apprentices scattered in near-panic.

Terrified, they'd nearly believed their end was near.

But Qian Xing paid them no mind; they'd merely scraped by with their lives.

Some apprentices remained behind, securing the area and swiftly contacting Shuang Tu.

Shuang Tu had been monitoring the situation all along, receiving firsthand intelligence almost immediately.

“So, in the end, the legacy of Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong was taken by Cai Jing and his crew...” Shuang Tu sighed, utterly powerless. Luring Qian Xing here was already the limit of what he could achieve.

But reality offered him no further opportunities. The chance he'd painstakingly waited for, to take advantage of the chaos, never materialized.

Shuang Tu could only resign himself to the situation.

Almost simultaneously, another report from his subordinate caught his attention: “Lan Zao and the Bayonet Gang have joined forces?”

“They've seized a large quantity of mithril. Are they preparing to hold a teleportation ritual?”

“My primary mission has failed, but this Tranquil Hill operation seems to have made a breakthrough!”

Realizing this, Shuang Tu immediately resolved to push forward aggressively, aiding Lan Zao's entry into Tranquil Hill.

Chapter 688: Cang Xu – I am a Devout Charming Blue Believer

Deep Sea Monster Fish.

In a specially arranged cabin, Cang Xu slowly opened his eyes.

“Where am I?” He looked around, his gaze unfocused.

But soon, waves of memories flooded his mind, making him understand his situation.

“My name is Duo Yu. I'm a fisherman.”

“From childhood, I have worshiped the Charming Blue Goddess, a devout believer in my Lord.”

“During a shipwreck, I was captured by an evil necromancer. He turned me into his test subject, ultimately transforming me into an undead being with his wicked magic!”

"Though I am no longer truly alive, I still yearn to return home. My wife must be waiting for me. My son, daughter-in-law, and my newborn grandson..."

“My Lord, I beseech Your mercy. Please rescue me.”

The cabin contained only a single bed and a statue.

The statue depicted a mermaid holding a staff, her fishtail standing amidst the waves. Her features were exquisite, and her gaze fixed on the horizon, radiating an aura of untouchable majesty.

It was a statue of Charming Blue Goddess.

Cang Xu knelt on both knees, his face filled with sincerity as he began to pray.

“My Lord, mighty Charming Blue Goddess!”

“You are the powerful Sea Goddess, possessing infinite wisdom and strength.”

“I beseech You to rescue me, to have mercy on me, to help me break the seal upon my body, to restore my freedom, and to return me to safety.”

“As Your sheep, I deeply feel Your love and grace. I trust You will guide me and grant me strength to rise and bravely face all challenges in my destiny.”

“I shall serve You for life. Even though I have become an undead monster, it does not diminish my faithful devotion to You!”

Cang Xu prayed over and over.

After several repetitions, a surge of divine power descended from the void, entering his body. It soothed his weary spirit, restoring him to peak condition!

Cang Xu's heart surged with shock and joy, tears welling in his eyes.

“The goddess has noticed me!” he nearly roared in excitement.

Remembering his confinement, he forced himself to suppress his overwhelming emotion and bowed his head to continue praying.

“Time is running out. I must reach the alchemy chamber, or that wicked necromancer will surely grow suspicious.” The fabricated memory urged Cang Xu, forcing him to reluctantly end his prayers.

He rose, took a few steps, and approached the hatch.

The instant he opened it, his pupils contracted to pinpricks, and he froze.

A tidal wave of genuine memories surged forth, rendering the fabricated memories suspicious in comparison.

Cang Xu stood frozen for nearly ten seconds before regaining his composure.

The collision of two memories sent sharp pains through his head. A wave of intense dizziness overwhelmed him, forcing him to bend over and kneel, retching uncontrollably.

At first, he was utterly skeptical, convinced an evil necromancer or magician had tampered with his memories to transform him into someone else.

But then, a powerful subconscious voice deep within him began insisting that the second set of memories was the genuine, unaltered truth.

Finally, when he recalled his capture, his reunion with the fishman youngster aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, and the plan to desecrate the goddess using fabricated memories, everything clicked into place.

“I'm awake!” Cang Xu trembled, gripping the metal wall as he pulled himself to his feet.

He shouted, “Commander!”

The fishman youngster remained stationed in the cockpit. The moment Cang Xu emerged, he noticed.

But the fishman youngster, exercising caution, did not reveal himself immediately. Instead, he used the intercom to ask Cang Xu several questions.

These questions had been deliberately prepared beforehand to verify the state of Cang Xu's memory recovery.

Cang Xu answered with remarkable accuracy and speed.

Only then did the fishman youngster truly reveal himself, inquiring with concern about the matter of the prayer.

Cang Xu declared, “I succeeded.”

"The hardest part was ‘awakening’, restoring my true self. In this phase, both the Memory Switching Spell and Subconscious Reminder Spell played crucial roles."

Cang Xu had designed the memory switching to be triggered by the action of “opening the hatch and stepping out of the chamber.”

Thus, the moment he left the chamber, his true memories began to resurface.

Two streams of memory clashed relentlessly, the real memories striving to overwhelm the fabricated ones, plunging Cang Xu's mind into chaos. At this critical juncture, his potent subconscious voice helped Cang Xu identify which memories were genuine, drastically shortening his recovery time.

This was precisely the power of the Subconscious Reminder Spell!

Cang Xu looked at the fishman youngster, his voice brimming with excitement: "Our fabricated memories have withstood the test. They hold up perfectly. After my prayer, I have also received divine blessing. My spirit, once exhausted and at its lowest ebb from studying and casting two spells, is now fully restored and at its peak."

The fishman youngster nodded, exhaling a cloud of murky breath.

Cang Xu's prayer had succeeded. The first trial was a resounding success, revealing several truths.

First, Charming Blue Goddess was indeed gravely wounded and had fallen into a deep slumber. Thus, the fabricated memory had passed inspection.

Second, though the Deep Sea Monster Fish was imbued with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, it could not hide from the Charming Blue Goddess. Even in her slumber, she could still bestow divine blessing upon Cang Xu.

Third, Cang Xu had not gained the goddess's attention.

The reason was simple: despite praying many times, Cang Xu had only recovered his spirit.

Compared to other believers, their gains were far greater. Some had elevated their transcendent ranks, while others had gained vast combat experience.

However, Cang Xu and the fishman youngster had anticipated this outcome in their secret discussions.

“It's likely your undead status caused the deity to treat you differently,” the fishman youngster sighed.

No deity would ever favor a necromancer.

Cang Xu shook his head, it was an inescapable reality.

Crucially, they currently had no solution for this predicament.

Using the Deceptive Disguise spell might be a solution, but based on the first trial, this method was highly likely to fail.

Still, it was worth a shot.

After all, the divine artifact overflowed with Pearl Bubbles.

The second prayer trial.

Cang Xu awoke alone in the cabin.

The cabin contained a single bed, an image of the Charming Blue Goddess, and a mirror.

In the mirror, Cang Xu saw his own reflection - a merman.

To enhance the prayer's effectiveness, the fishman youngster had directly altered Cang Xu's appearance into that of a merman, aligning it most closely with the image of the Charming Blue Goddess.

To complement this new form, the fishman youngster also used the alchemy device to slightly adjust the fabricated memories.

“I am a merman named Duo Yu.”

“From childhood, I have worshiped the Charming Blue Goddess as a devout believer of my Lord!”

"I was persecuted! The priest of the Sea Abyss Church framed me and imprisoned me here. Only if I renounce my faith will he spare me."

"But how could I ever do that!"

“I will never renounce my faith, even if that damned priest has placed a deadly curse upon me that will kill me instantly when the time comes.”

“Damn it! He's sealed my supernatural abilities. I can't escape on my own!”

Cang Xu gritted his teeth silently.

Within the fabricated memories, he had already attempted numerous escape plans, all ending in failure.

“Smash this statue, and I'll release you. I'll extend your time limit and delay your sudden death.” The priest of the Sea Abyss Church's words echoed in his ears.

Cang Xu refused to do so. Gritting his teeth, he knelt before the statue of the Charming Blue Goddess and began his prayer.

“My Lord, goddess I worship with my entire being.”

“You are the Sea Goddess, ruling over the oceans and all sea creatures.”

“You are the Goddess of Deception and Disguise, capable of deceiving foes and masquerading as all things.”

"I deeply feel Your power and majesty and shall follow Your path for all my days. Grant me strength to break free from my bonds, to avert the peril of sudden death!"

"Protect me, let Your radiance illuminate my life. My God!"

Cang Xu's prayer originated from his fabricated memories.

In reality, it was meticulously crafted through discussions with the fishman youngster.

Both the first and second rounds of prayer addressed needs like “breaking bonds” and “averting mortal peril.” The purpose was clear: to harness the divine blessing of the Charming Blue Goddess to resolve the seal planted by Zhou Zhang and the undead trap set by Jian Gui.

This time, though Cang Xu again received divine blessing, it bypassed the saint level seal and the undead threat. Instead, it granted him a divine job from thin air, elevating him to the rank of monk.

Along with this monk job, Cang Xu acquired the doctrines of the Charming Blue Church, its cultivation practices, and the protocols for prayer.

After opening the door, Cang Xu regained his memories once more.

When he turned to look at the mirror in the cabin, his merman form vanished without a trace, restoring his true appearance.

Such was the nature of the Deceptive Disguise spell.

Cang Xu's true memories knew this truth, so upon awakening, the spell held no power over him.

After conversing with the fishman youngster, he sighed, “The sample size is too small. We can't determine the extent of the divine blessing between the two instances.”

Cang Xu replied, "Regardless, the mermaid's disguise isn't very convincing. Just as we suspected, the Deceptive Disguise spell may fool Zhou Zhang, but it certainly cannot deceive the Charming Blue Goddess!"

"We've wasted enough time. I see no need to test the Deceptive Disguise spell again. It's time for me to guide you, Commander, into meditation."

A flicker of hesitation crossed the fishman youngster's face, but he eventually nodded. Together with Cang Xu, they departed and entered the meditation chamber.

Divine bestowal, commonly referred to as the divine blessing, was fundamentally tailored to the recipient.

The primary considerations were twofold: the depth of faith and the innate aptitude of the believer.

After two prayer trials, they concluded the Deceptive Disguise spell was highly unlikely to conceal anything from the Charming Blue Goddess.

Therefore, from the perspectives of the fishman youngster and Cang Xu, the most effective way to enhance divine blessing was to improve innate aptitude!

For anyone else, this method would be nothing short of a fairy tale.

But the fishman youngster, possessing a Bloodcore, was exceptional. Cang Xu, who could benefit from the Bloodline Purification formation, was also exceptional.

Chapter 689: Fishman Youngster – I am a Necromancer

The Bloodline Purification formation resided within the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Lan Zao and Zi Di (her body) had already benefited.

However, the conditions for Cang Xu to purify his bloodline were not yet ripe.

He still lacked two prerequisites.

The first condition was that he had to understand the true nature of his undead bloodline. Especially after undergoing bloodline mutation.

The second condition was that the fishman youngster had to collect the corresponding undead bloodline in his Bloodcore. Only when the corresponding Bloodcore magic was infused into the Bloodline Purification formation could the purification process commence.

Regarding the first condition, Cang Xu had already fulfilled it through his own efforts. In the midst of the crisis, he had skillfully intervened to obtain the intermediate soul aspect inheritance, granting him the spell to detect undead bloodlines.

However...

He was still unable to cast it.

The reason? He was currently bound by both divine and magic restraints imposed by Zhou Zhang!

Therefore, to cast the spell detecting undead aptitude, the fishman youngster must be the one to do it.

As a silver level magician, the fishman Zhong Yong was also capable of doing it. Yet, neither the fishman youngster nor Cang Xu considered him an option.

On one hand, the survivors had no intention of revealing secrets to Zhong Yong; on the other, Zhong Yong himself was incapable of casting the spell to detect undead bloodlines.

The latter was a pure undead spell, requiring negative energy to be cast.

This was an irreplaceable key element.

Therefore, only the fishman youngster could do it!

But the fishman youngster was a fighter, not a magician.

Thus, with Cang Xu's prayer proving ineffective, they immediately adopted an alternative plan - Cang Xu would protect and guide the fishman youngster through crucial meditation.

Upon entering the meditation chamber, the fishman youngster sat cross-legged, assuming the posture for meditation.

“Commander, which meditation method have you chosen?” Cang Xu inquired.

“It's called the Tidal Sound meditation method. Take a look.” The fishman youngster handed Cang Xu the detailed meditation method.

Cultivating battle skill required battle qi manuals, while refining magic power demanded meditation methods.

Of course, simple meditation was possible, but its efficiency paled in comparison to dedicated meditation methods.

The meditation method the fishman youngster presented naturally originated from the War Merchant's database.

Since emerging from Mystifying Monster Island, the survivors had continually reaped the benefits of the island's spoils. This remains true even now.

Cang Xu skimmed through the meditation method before nodding to the fishman youngster, “We can begin.”

He crossed his legs beside the fishman youngster, linking his consciousness to the youngster through the alchemy apparatus using his own spirit.

The fishman youngster drew a quiet breath, slowly closed his eyes, and commenced this crucial meditation.

Having absorbed Hai Ping's saint corpse, he possessed the Surging Wave fishman bloodline. Due to its mere 11% concentration, vital information was lacking, and the fishman youngster remained unaware it was a god level bloodline.

His Flaming Dragon King bloodline reached 35% concentration, though legend level, its higher potency made it outperform the Surging Wave fishman bloodline in cultivation.

Even so, the 11% concentration of Surging Wave fishman bloodline was more than sufficient to sustain the fishman youngster's early cultivation in magic spells.

The fishman youngster's meditation proceeded exceptionally smoothly.

In truth, he had already invested considerable effort in mastering the Tidal Sound meditation method beforehand, practicing it repeatedly.

This meditation was crucial, and out of caution, the youngster deliberately waited until reuniting with Cang Xu before beginning.

In truth, many magic apprentices had their mentors present to safeguard them during their first steps. Cang Xu was performing precisely that role now. Should the fishman youngster falter, Cang Xu could intervene immediately to rescue him from peril.

Yet, from the moment the fishman youngster slowly opened his eyes until now, Cang Xu had found no opportunity to act.

Though the Surging Wave bloodline leaned toward a clerical role among the fishman's three god level bloodlines, it also proved remarkably effective for cultivating magic and battle qi.

Bolstered by his god level bloodline, the fishman youngster achieved successful meditation without difficulty, formally opening his personal magic pool.

“Congratulations, Commander. You are now a bronze level magician,” Cang Xu smiled.

Currently, the fishman youngster's life level stood at silver level, still some distance from reaching its peak.

His fighter level was likewise silver level, though in dragon-form, with carefully selected battle skills and equipment, he could wield gold level combat power.

Having just begun his magic cultivation, the magician level was currently at the lowest bronze level.

Cang Xu added: "Commander, your fishman bloodline is not simple. In just this short period of opening your magic pool, you've already converted half a pool of water-attribute magic power. I advise you to expel this magic power immediately."

The fishman youngster nodded.

Here in the deep sea, within the Ocean Mother Nest, water elements were abundantly plentiful.

Yet without exceptional water elemental talent, achieving what the youngster had done would be utterly impossible.

He channeled his spirit to direct the water magic power within his pool, swiftly draining it dry.

The pool lay utterly empty, devoid of any magic.

Immediately afterward, the fishman youngster focused his attention on the Bloodcore at his heart, beginning to channel its magic.

Blood light flickered across the youngster's skin before fading. When it vanished, the fishman youngster's scales had turned blackish gray, their patterns warped into two eerie grimaces, one on his back, one on his chest.

This was the bloodline trait of the Ghostface Fish.

The Ghostface Fish resembled a flounder in appearance, with an extremely flat body. Adult specimens exhibited bilateral asymmetry, with both eyes clustered on one side and a curved mouth. Like flatfish, Ghostface Fish permanently rested on their side on the ocean floor.

Even ordinary Ghostface Fish reached the size of a carriage. When attacking, they launched themselves upward like soaring birds, capable of leaping over ship railings to dive onto decks.

Crucially, these beasts possess both water and undead attributes. Their scales were prime undead material.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps collected numerous Ghostface Fish carcasses among their materials.

As this was a rare undead material, they had deliberately gathered a large quantity during their time on Two Eyes Island.

Unexpectedly, this undead material ultimately wasn't used on Zi Di or Cang Xu but instead consumed by the fishman youngster.

Bloodcore - Hybrid Transformation!

The fishman youngster now possessed two bloodlines. One was the Surging Wave fishman bloodline, the other was the Ghostface Fish bloodline.

This temporarily transformed him into a life form possessing both water and undead attributes.

However, the Ghostface Fish bloodline wasn't that of an intelligent species, but pure magic beast bloodline. It couldn't coexist harmoniously with the Surging Wave fishman bloodline.

Thus, this life form was unstable, constantly draining the Bloodcore's magic to sustain itself.

Having reached this stage, the dragonman youngster could now absorb negative energy.

The undead materials on Cang Xu's body were unusable. Instead, he consumed part of the Deep Sea Monster Fish's undead material reserves, decomposing them to generate negative energy.

Zi Di had always employed this method to temporarily modify her meditation environment and enhance her meditation efficiency.

Now, the fishman youngster had to resort to the same approach.

The reason was simple: not a trace of negative energy existed within the Ocean Mother Nest. He had no choice but to create it artificially.

The fishman youngster began his meditation. He absorbed external negative energy, transforming it into undead magic power that accumulated within his personal magic pool.

Only with such undead magic power could he cast necromancy magic!

A new problem arose.

The spell to detect undead bloodlines was recorded in the intermediate soul aspect inheritance. This spell was beyond the grasp of a novice; its casting difficulty was extremely high.

“Commander, for the time being, you'll likely have to practice spellcasting alone.”

“Fortunately, you can practice with other basic water spells. Zhong Yong can assist you,” Cang Xu said.

“What? You truly wish to continue taking the risk?” The fishman youngster's expression turned solemn.

This was a topic they'd discussed earlier - whether Cang Xu would teleport back after their reunion.

The fishman youngster would rather have Cang Xu stay.

It was the safest option.

Cang Xu shook his head slightly, “I bear a double seal and the deadly hidden trap set by Jian Gui. Staying here, I'm constrained and can't be of much use."

“But if I return, it proves my faith. Having gained the approval of the Charming Blue Goddess, Zhou Zhang won't trouble me, she might even lift my seals voluntarily.”

“After that, I can continue communicating with Jian Gui. Perhaps I can pry out other undead legacies.”

“We need more undead materials, don't we?”

Cang Xu was right.

The fishman youngster required vast quantities of undead materials to generate negative energy, which he would then absorb and convert into undead magic power.

Only with sufficient magic power could he practice extensively and ultimately succeed in casting undead spells.

On the other hand, Cang Xu needed to purify his bloodline, requiring corresponding bloodline magic energy. This required the Bloodcore to absorb vast amounts of undead life.

But how could the fishman youngster obtain undead life within the Ocean Mother Nest?

Zhou Zhang possessed it!

She had captured Jian Gui and others, sweeping up every zombie, bone soldier, and ghost from the battlefield, even that ghost ship.

The fishman youngster gazed at Cang Xu with concern: “But to teleport back, you'll face the saint level octopus's trial. It might kill you outright.”

Cang Xu smiled: “I believe the risk here is minimal, small enough for me to take the chance.”

“Falsifying memories isn't an issue. I succeeded in both previous prayers.”

"Most crucially, I've already received the divine blessing. Based on your observations, Commander, not a single believer who received divine blessing has ever been killed by the octopus, correct?"

The fishman youngster sighed, “Even so, once you meet Zhou Zhang, there's no guarantee she won't cast some divine spell to detect your true faith.”

“We have the Deceptive Disguise spell,” Cang Xu declared, his voice brimming with confidence. "If this spell can deceive the Charming Blue Goddess, surely it can fool Zhou Zhang? Didn't we already prove that?"

“There's still a risk. We haven't uncovered all of Zhou Zhang's methods...”

“Lord Commander!” Cang Xu interrupted the fishman youngster with rare urgency, his expression solemn and earnest, “I already bear the fatal hidden trap set by Jian Gui and Zhou Zhang's double seal.”

“Let me take this one risk!”

“Only then might my heart find some peace. When I face you next time, it will be a little easier...”

The fishman youngster paused, startled.

It was the first time he'd seen such an expression on Cang Xu's face.

After a moment, the corners of his mouth lifted into a gentle smile.

He reached out and patted Cang Xu's shoulder lightly, “Then... let's do that.”

Chapter 690: Cang Xu Returns

White Hair Trench.

Ocean Mother Nest.

The grand formation hummed as it operated, the golden altar flickering with a faint glow.

Countless Charming Blue believers surrounded the altar, kneeling in prayer. They maintained a certain distance from the altar at all times.

“The time is almost upon us, isn't it?”

“Yes. Judging by the previous teleportation blood sacrifice, they should be returning soon.”

“My son... you must come back alive.”

Xiao Wang Zi whispered to Jian Gui, “What are the chances Xu Jia will return alive?”

Jian Gui remained silent, merely shaking his head slightly, his gaze solemn.

The believers looked up expectantly, even Zhou Zhang filled with anticipation.

Those Charming Blue believers who returned alive had undoubtedly undergone rigorous selection, gained enhanced strength, and deepened their faith.

These individuals would surely become the elite, even the pillars, of the Charming Blue Church!

Spatial fluctuations rippled through the air.

Before the gathered crowd, the believers flashed back to their original kneeling positions.

As with previous blood sacrifices, some were missing.

“I'm back! I passed the trial!” a returning believer shouted excitedly.

“My son! Where is my son?” another frantically scanned the crowd.

“Quite a few casualties this time,” Zhou Zhang noted impassively, silently tallying the losses. Her gaze suddenly halted, settling on one figure.

It was none other than Cang Xu!

"Oh? Something interesting has happened.” The gold level fighter Zhang Yu Wan, standing nearby, also spotted Cang Xu.

More and more eyes turned toward Cang Xu. It was impossible not to notice him; he was too conspicuous.

He was the sole necromancer.

“Did I see that correctly?”

“That necromancer... he... he... he actually returned?”

“Cang Xu! It really is Cang Xu! He survived!” Xiao Wang Zi was the most agitated.

He had chosen to aid Ku Feng, indirectly eliminating Cang Xu, and had harbored guilt ever since. Seeing Cang Xu return now, he was likely the happiest person present after Qing Xin.

Countless gazes converged on Cang Xu. Some eyes widened in shock, others furrowed in disbelief, while some fell into deep contemplation.

More murmured in astonishment, discussing how an undead could gain divine recognition, utterly baffling.

“No, impossible!”

“My son worshiped our Lord most devoutly, from childhood till his death. He believed in no other god.”

"He didn’t return alive, yet you, an undead, have come back?"

“Something's fishy! You must have cheated!”

The father who'd searched in vain for his son shouted in despair, pointing at Cang Xu and openly accusing him.

The crowd's expressions varied.

Cang Xu sneered coldly, retorting sharply, “I worship our Lord, the great Charming Blue Goddess. The Lord is so forgiving and merciful that She has already granted me divine blessing. Who are you? You dare question my Lord's will?"

“Yes!” Jian Gui stepped forward, echoing immediately, "We undead are life forms too, existing only through negative energy. Why can't we convert to our Lord and become Her believers? Your racial prejudice is one thing, but you gravely underestimate our Lord's magnanimity! You are guilty!"

Cang Xu's appearance shocked Jian Gui immensely. He had believed Cang Xu was dead.

But after the shock, Jian Gui rejoiced.

After all, Cang Xu was one of his own, and he had planted a fatal trap within him.

Cang Xu nodded to Jian Gui. Though the latter was utterly hypocritical, being the first to stand up for him was precisely what Cang Xu needed now.

In contrast, Qi Ling beside Jian Gui stared at Cang Xu in disbelief, her expression darkening.

Jian Gui's endorsement immediately drew loud approval from Xiao Wang Zi, Ku Feng, and others.

On the pirate side, Qing Xin also shouted in support of Cang Xu.

The elderly believer whose son had perished paid no heed. He took several running steps before collapsing to his knees before the altar. Gazing up at Zhou Zhang, he wailed, "Archbishop Zhou Zhang, I implore you! Expose the true form of this wicked undead! He's a fake! He must be a fake! How could he possibly survive, while my son...?"

All eyes instantly focused on Zhou Zhang.

Her face expressionless, Zhou Zhang suddenly raised a hand. The elderly believer's cries abruptly ceased.

He struggled to shout, yet no sound emerged.

Then Zhou Zhang beckoned faintly to Cang Xu.

Cang Xu suddenly felt an immense, invisible force grip his entire body, lifting him off the ground and carrying him into the air before setting him down upon the golden altar.

“The most crucial test has arrived!” Cang Xu felt a chill run through him, though outwardly he remained composed as he met Zhou Zhang's gaze.

She cast a divine spell, scanning Cang Xu's entire body, but found nothing.

Zhou Zhang then asked Cang Xu several questions in a gentle tone, to which Cang Xu answered cautiously.

When asked, “What is your divine blessing?” he replied that he had become a monk, receiving a series of doctrines, prayer procedures, rituals, and so forth.

"Oh? You've actually attained a clerical position?" Zhou Zhang's expression softened further.

“A monk... counts as a clerical position?” Cang Xu was puzzled.

By clerical position, he meant a role within the church.

Zhou Zhang nodded slightly: "A believer isn't considered a member of the clergy, but a monk can be seen as a preparatory position. Take one more step, and you could become a missionary for our Lord."

“Continue striving diligently.”

Zhou Zhang then publicly declared that Cang Xu was now a member of the church, a fellow believer, one who had renounced darkness for the light!

“What did she say?”

“It’s much easier than I imagined!”

Standing upon the altar, Cang Xu gazed at Zhou Zhang to his right front, then scanned the diverse expressions of the Charming Blue believers below. A slight sense of relief settled in his heart.

Flower Skirt Island.

"So, this is how we get to Tranquil Hill? It was way too easy." Spring Boxer voiced a similar astonishment to Cang Xu's.

Bing Xiao nodded then shook his head: “This is merely the ritual's sequence and arrangement. Memorize it carefully, it'll make recreating it easier later.”

“But that alone won't suffice.”

“The success of this special ritual hinges on the people.”

“More precisely, on me and my comrade in the ice coffin.”

“Both Teng Dong Lang and I are holy warriors of Tranquil Hill!”

Spring Boxer widened his eyes slightly: “Holy warriors?”

Bing Xiao explained: "It's a clerical position, similar to a templar knight. Only a ritual conducted by a holy warrior will succeed, allowing us to reach Tranquil Hill safely."

“Now kneel beside me. Repeat my prayer words and join me in praying.”

As Bing Xiao knelt, the sounds of explosions and fierce gunfire erupted outside.

“It's the other silver factions! They've united, using our recent black market raid as justification to storm the stronghold!”

“There are too many silver level enemies! We can barely hold them off!” someone reported.

Spring Boxer's expression hardened. He started to rise, intending to rush to the defense, but Bing Xiao grabbed his arm.

“There's no time! Our top priority now is reaching Tranquil Hill!”

“But what about the others?”

"This isn't the time to think about them!”

“If they fall in battle, the Bayonet Gang will be left with only us...”

"What we must do now outweighs the Bayonet Gang's importance! Kneel. Pray with me." Bing Xiao's expression at that moment was terrifying.

Spring Boxer gritted his teeth and knelt, obeying Bing Xiao's command.

The special ritual succeeded swiftly.

Suddenly, snow swirled through the basement. A strange space unfolded like a scroll before Spring Boxer.

“Carry Teng Dong Lang's ice coffin. Let's go,” Bing Xiao ordered.

Spring Boxer hurriedly protested, “Wait! We need to gather the others. We should leave together.”

Bing Xiao refused outright: "Absolutely not! A place as crucial as Tranquil Hill isn't somewhere outsiders can enter!"

But before his words faded, the snowy space like a painting abruptly expanded, spreading out to engulf the entire base.

Bing Xiao's expression changed drastically.

The snowy expanse engulfed every member of the Bayonet Gang, even including the few silver level foes who had charged to the forefront.

The next instant, the snowy space violently contracted, vanishing entirely along with everyone it had enveloped, leaving only an empty stronghold behind.

"It worked! It really worked!" Shuang Tu, the mastermind behind the operation, was overcome with excitement at the sight.

Though his primary mission had failed, he had successfully aided both the human ringleader and Lan Zao into Tranquil Hill.

This was an absolute breakthrough!

Of course, he had paid a tremendous price for it.

“What comes next isn't solely my responsibility.”

“I must relay this news immediately. The organization will undoubtedly spare no effort to assist them both!”

Chapter 691: Entering Tranquil Hill

The night sea breeze fluttered the naval flag of the Sheng Ming Empire.

A gold level magic ship cut through the waves.

This vessel was clearly not a standard Imperial Navy warship. Its bow was thick and sturdy, with a bridge positioned squarely at the center of the deck—a perfect cube, equal in length, width, and height.

The surface of this cube was covered in intricate, layered magic formations. Embedded within these formations were countless gems: sapphires the size of fists, diamonds as large as human heads, and topazes as big as carriages.

It was the Treasure Chest.

The captain, Tan Mo, a gold level fighter and Rear Admiral of the Sheng Ming Empire's Navy, stood within the bridge.

He gripped the wheel with both hands, concentrating intently on navigating the flagship through the night.

The first mate approached, standing ramrod straight as he reported: “Lord, an urgent transmission from Channel Three!”

Tan Mo's brow furrowed instantly. Communication from Channel Three carried immense significance and often brought immense trouble.

“What's going on?” “Channel Three only relayed orders to me not long ago. Why now?”

Tan Mo tossed the wheel to the first mate, “Steer carefully. These waters aren't safe, especially at night.”

The first mate hurriedly acknowledged the order, though he inwardly rolled his eyes.

Normally, Tan Mo rarely came up to the bridge, preferring to stay in his cabin enjoying the company of beautiful women and indulging in fine wines and delicacies.

This version of Tan Mo was exceedingly rare.

The first mate knew the reason - it was that person aboard the Treasure Chest before departure!

Tan Mo entered the communications room and received the new orders from Channel Three.

“Another important figure wants to board my ship?” Tan Mo felt troubled.

The Treasure Chest had already sailed far from port, but according to Channel Three’s instructions, Tan Mo only needed to provide his precise coordinates.

Tan Mo's dilemma, of course, wasn't about providing coordinates. It was that his ship already carried someone he absolutely dared not provoke. Now another important figure was being sent aboard, giving him a headache.

But there was no choice - it was Channel Three!

"Before proceeding, I must report to that person of importance. Otherwise, misunderstandings could lead to trouble." Tan Mo steeled himself, entered the Treasure Chest, and relayed the situation.

The big shot gave him a thorough dressing-down. Tan Mo bowed and scraped his way out, struggling to maintain his composure.

He hurried back to the deck without delay.

By then, the Treasure Chest had come to a slow halt in the ocean.

Using the active sea chart, Tan Mo pinpointed the ship's current coordinates. He then ordered his subordinates to calculate the position using alternative methods, yielding another set of coordinates.

The two sets of coordinates matched perfectly.

Only then did Tan Mo transmit this critical data via Channel Three.

About ten minutes later, violent spatial fluctuations erupted directly above the Treasure Chest.

From the turbulence, a brilliant blue light coalesced.

The light condensed into a circular doorway.

A gold level clergyman stepped through the doorway, bearing a door-shaped emblem on his chest, and ascended to hover above the Treasure Chest.

Another figure followed closely behind him.

This latter individual was a towering, imposing figure, carrying a massive shield as tall as a man. Clad in heavy, full-body holy armor that concealed his features, he radiated an aura unmistakably of the saint level.

"A gold level clergyman of the Secret Gate Church, and a saint level shield guardian!" Tan Mo's heart skipped a beat as he hurriedly greeted them.

Though he himself was gold level, he lacked flight battle skill and could only look up.

The Secret Gate Bishop and the saint level shield guardian slowly descended, landing on the deck to formally meet Tan Mo.

Tan Mo displayed warmth, the saint level shield guardian remained silent, while the Secret Gate Bishop adopted an arrogant demeanor.

“Prepare the finest quarters for me. I require rest!” commanded the Secret Gate Bishop.

“Lord, that may prove difficult,” Tan Mo replied with a troubled expression, “You are unaware of the circumstances. There is a prominent figure currently aboard my vessel!”

“Important figure?” The Secret Gate Bishop scoffed in disbelief, “What legend level VIP could possibly be aboard your little vessel?”

Tan Mo hurriedly explained, “This guest is only gold level, but…”

The Secret Gate Bishop waved him off, cutting him off, “A mere gold level? What kind of VIP is that?”

"Is that so? Could you repeat that statement?" The voice echoed as someone stepped onto the deck.

The Secret Gate Bishop froze upon recognizing the speaker, his expression shifting to sheer terror, “I... I...”

Even the saint level shield guardian behind him hurriedly bowed in reverence.

The “important figure” was indeed only of gold level, but his uniform featured a black base adorned with crimson patterns.

This revealed his identity - he was a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution!

"Lord Tu Ji, why have you come up? Is there something you require? Please state your command, and I shall fulfill it to the best of my ability." The imposing captain, Rear Admiral Tan Mo, now groveled like a lowly servant.

Tu Ji of the Blood Light Punishment Institution replied coldly, "You've already wasted too much of my time retrieving these two. This time, I'm heading to Two Eyes Island to investigate undead traces. The longer this drags on, the harder the investigation becomes! Should my mission be delayed, you will bear responsibility!"

"M-My Lord, this humble servant cannot shoulder such responsibility! I shall set sail immediately, departing at full speed to save every moment! I guarantee we shall reach the target waters within seven days!" Tan Mo hastily pledged.

“You had better keep your word.” Tu Ji finished, flicked his sleeve, and returned to the cabin.

The Secret Gate Bishop remained behind, glaring reproachfully at Tan Mo. The unspoken accusation hung heavily: How did a lunatic from the Blood Light Punishment Institution end up aboard your vessel? Why didn't you warn me sooner?

But seeing the bitter expression on Tan Mo's face, the Secret Gate Bishop ultimately only gave a cold snort, “Take us to the cabin. We need to rest.”

"No, not the hold below deck. Your forecastle is quite nice. Are there rooms there?"

"Yes, of course. Lords, this way please." Tan Mo bowed low, leading the way.

There was no night in Tranquil Hill.

Here, snow blanketed everything, the cold piercing to the bone.

Crimson blood and corpses littered the snow.

Lan Zao panted heavily as Xiong Ju and the others cleaned up the battlefield.

They had been unexpectedly swept into the fray during the fierce battle, entering Tranquil Hill together.

This turn of events surprised both sides.

But with reinforcements from Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer, the Bayonet Gang quickly gained the upper hand.

Their adversaries were the leaders of Flower Skirt Island's factions, including three silver level fighters.

Under Xiong Ju's relentless assault, their formation collapsed.

Spring Boxer seized the opportunity to unleash his battle skill, grabbing one of them and battering him into critical condition.

Xiong Ju executed his Savage Charge battle skill, immediately following with a Bear Pounce, killing one silver level fighter.

Lan Zao employed the alchemy projectile cores, coordinating with the human ringleader to pin down the third silver level opponent.

Thanks to Xiong Ju's relentless slaughter, the Bayonet Gang successfully broke through the stalemate. They ultimately eliminated the remaining two silver level foes, securing their victory.

"So, where exactly is this place? How do we get back?" Lan Zao asked.

“This is Tranquil Hill,” Spring Boxer answered, glancing at Bing Xiao.

Bing Xiao's face turned ashen, “None of you can return for now.”

Though victorious and having successfully brought the ice coffin into Tranquil Hill, Bing Xiao couldn't muster a smile.

The sudden expansion of the divine domain at the last moment was highly unusual.

Especially since Bing Xiao knew there had always been a mole within the Bayonet Gang.

“This is likely a conspiracy!”

“Perhaps our escape from Snow Bird Harbor was just the beginning of it.”

“The fact that I obtained Teng Dong Lang's complete body is highly suspicious.”

“The crucial question now is: who is the mole?”

"Is it Xiong Ju? The human ringleader? Including Hen Fa... even though his background check came clean. But we can't fully trust him yet!"

Lan Zao pressed urgently, his face etched with anxiety: "Why can't we go back? How can we get back?"

Bing Xiao swept a glance over the surrounding gang members, then fixed his gaze on Lan Zao: “What? You want to go back and face an ambush?”

Lan Zao: “Uh, that's not what I meant.”

“Alright, follow my lead from here on out,” Bing Xiao said, “Who knows? This place might grant you a massive boost that'll leave a lasting impression for the rest of your lives!”

Bing Xiao dared not let the gang members roam freely, so the best course was to keep them all under his watchful eye.

“Tranquil Hill?” The human ringleader murmured, confusion mingled with faint excitement. "Did I hear correctly, Lord Gang Leader? Is this the legendary Tranquil Hill?"

In an instant, the rest of the gang members turned their gaze toward Bing Xiao.

Chapter 692: The Dueling God’s Divine Domain

The name Tranquil Hill was known to every soul present.

Facing the human ringleader’s inquiry, Bing Xiao remained impassive, nodding slightly as he acknowledged outright, “Indeed, it is that very Tranquil Hill.”

A collective gasp erupted, faces brimming with excitement and disbelief.

Legends of Tranquil Hill’s heroic duels were household tales throughout the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

“Who would’ve thought I could enter Tranquil Hill? To forge a connection with such a legendary land!” murmured the human ringleader, his feigned expression flawless, truly the work of a seasoned mole.

“This is incredible!” Xiong Ju roared with excitement.

He revered the heroic duel. During his battle with the dragonman youngster, the demon hybrid ringleader had secretly exerted power from outside the arena, attempting to assassinate the youngster. Xiong Ju detected it and crushed the demon's eye with a single squeeze.

Lan Zao's mouth hung slightly open, his face frozen in shock.

He wasn't skilled at concealing his emotions, and he too sought entry into Tranquil Hill. But this time, entering Tranquil Hill, he hadn't received Shuang Tu's warning.

He'd simply been swept into the fray during the battle.

When the fighting ended and he finally registered what had happened, he realized he was already inside!

Before this, he'd shared the same plan as Spring Boxer: to sail all the way to Tranquil Hill. He never imagined entering this way.

"Damn it. This means I have no clue how to get to Tranquil Hill. Even if I return to the mercenary group later, I won't be able to report back to the commander."

Bing Xiao looked at Spring Boxer. Though the latter knew the truth, his expression remained agitated.

Bing Xiao: “You are Teng Dong Lang's coffin bearer. Therefore, you need only carry the ice coffin and follow closely behind me.”

He gave Spring Boxer a brief instruction, then addressed the others: “As for the rest of you, lacking Spring Boxer's status, you must precisely tread in my footsteps. Not a single step may be missed if you wish to keep pace.”

“Tranquil Hill is safe. Achieving this isn't difficult.”

“Whoever makes a mistake is a hidden enemy. The rest of you must kill them immediately, leaving no trace behind!"

A chill ran through the group, and they obeyed without delay.

Tranquil Hill lay blanketed in snow, and Bing Xiao's footprints were clearly visible as he walked.

Following his footsteps in a single file, each person kept close behind, wary of one another, yet not a single one fell behind.

Spring Boxer faced no such constraints. Carrying the ice coffin, with every step he took, the scenery around him changed dramatically.

The distant ice peaks, visible only a few steps away, rapidly expanded within their field of vision.

Another five or six steps later, they had traversed tens of thousands of meters, arriving at the foot of the ice peaks.

Bing Xiao stepped onto the ice peaks. Following his footprints, the others took only two or three steps to reach the summit.

The mountaintop was a flat expanse dotted with numerous tombstones.

Behind each monument lay a rectangular burial pit.

A sharp gleam flashed in the human ringleader's eyes as he swiftly counted the monuments, 38 in total.

His heart surged with excitement: “This signifies at least 38 gold level powerhouses!”

Bing Xiao's expression grew grave, “Now, you need not follow my footsteps.”

“But you must all stay put. Anyone who dares move freely becomes my mortal enemy!”

Then, he nodded to Spring Boxer: “Follow me.”

Xiong Ju and the others remained frozen in place, not daring to stir. They watched silently as Spring Boxer carried Teng Dong Lang's ice coffin to his tombstone.

The translucent ice monument bore Teng Dong Lang's name and portrait.

The likeness was strikingly lifelike.

“Why is a third of the boss's tombstone missing?” Spring Boxer inquired.

Bing Xiao crouched, wiped the monument, and sighed. "It signifies the extent of the corpse's disfigurement. If the time limit is exceeded, the tombstone will vanish completely. By then, even if you bring the ice coffin, there'll be nowhere to bury it!"

Following Bing Xiao's instructions, Spring Boxer then placed Teng Dong Lang's ice coffin into the corresponding burial pit.

"Old gang leader, will the Boss truly be resurrected? When will he come back to life?" Spring Boxer asked, his voice brimming with hope.

The others held their breath; their eyes fixed intently on the scene.

Resurrection was difficult, yet not entirely impossible.

This was a world where gods exist.

Many had successfully revived the dead using divine resurrection techniques.

And according to legend, Tranquil Hill was a mystical place capable of bringing people back to life.

Bing Xiao sighed. "Resurrection... it's only possible. Even the most intact corpse isn't guaranteed a 100% chance of revival."

“But burying Teng Dong Lang here will surely yield tremendous rewards.”

Spring Boxer froze. "Resurrection isn't guaranteed? What are the actual odds of revival?"

Bing Xiao didn't answer. Instead, he beckoned. "Now is not the time for that discussion. Come to me. Kneel. Pray with me."

Spring Boxer could only turn, confusion lingering, and walk toward Bing Xiao.

Midway, his footsteps abruptly halted. He spotted the Axe Gang leader's tombstone and exclaimed in surprise, "The bald uncle has a tombstone here too? So he was also a holy warrior of Tranquil Hill?"

“Strange... there are so many cracks on Uncle's tombstone.”

"What?!" Bing Xiao's pupils contracted sharply. His heart skipped a beat as he stared at the Axe Gang leader's tombstone.

He understood the meaning of those cracks.

“The Axe Gang leader... is dead?”

“Wait, Jia Bing, Lin... they're all dead?”

Bing Xiao had been focused solely on Teng Dong Lang's tombstone. Now, scanning the area, he noticed cracks on all three monuments.

These cracks were subtle, visible only upon close inspection.

But Bing Xiao knew that even the slightest crack signified the death of the holy warrior it represented. Over time, these cracks would multiply until the entire tombstone shattered.

“What in the world is going on?!”

"These three were exceptionally close during their service. Judging by the state of the cracks, they likely perished simultaneously!"

"This suggests they probably fell on the same battlefield. Who could have struck such a blow?"

“These were three genuine gold levels, battle-hardened gold level experts at that!”

Bing Xiao suppressed his shock, forcing a composed expression, “Now, place your hands upon the tombstone and recite the prayer with me.”

"Great Dueling God, we praise You, we praise this most sacred land of Tranquil Hill. Today we come to Your divine domain to bring back Your holy warrior."

“We beseech You: grant the fallen holy warrior true peace and tranquility.”

“Watch over our new warriors. Bestow upon us their steadfast and courageous spirits, that we may learn from the successes and mistakes of our predecessors, drawing wisdom from their legacy.”

"In Your name, we pray for greater strength and wisdom to advance, to understand the trials endured by those who came before us, and to reflect upon them as our own knowledge. Grant us courage, strength, and wisdom to succeed in life's journey."
