"Guide us toward the path of peace. We praise the exalted and sacred nature of Tranquil Hill, journeying together toward light and tranquility."

“Endless praise be to You, exalted Dueling God, sacred and wondrous land of Tranquil Hill!”

As the prayers of Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer spread, a solemn and sacred atmosphere swiftly enveloped the space. The others watched intently, their hearts filled with awe and growing curiosity.

"Dueling God? Does the Ice Sculpture Kingdom have such a deity?"

"Tranquil Hill... is actually the divine domain of the Dueling God?

"Was the special ritual on Flower Skirt Island to summon the divine domain? This is absurd, I've actually stepped into a domain of the gods! "

Lan Zao felt even more troubled: "So the truth behind Tranquil Hill's resurrection is the Dueling God? Does that mean to revive Miss Zi Di, we must at least convert her into a holy warrior of the Dueling God?"

“Th-that sounds incredibly difficult...”

The human ringleader, acting as an excellent mole, focused intently on the names engraved on the tombstone, trying to memorize them.

“Long Meng... The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's top gladiator was also a holy warrior of the Dueling God?”

“Mei Lin—is that the mermaid magician from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's First Navy?”

"Bao Li Gen... Who is this? Never heard of them."

The human ringleader recognized many familiar names but also encountered unfamiliar ones.

He didn't know the meaning behind the cracks in the tombstones, but he made an effort to memorize every name on them.

“Regardless of anything else, this intelligence alone is invaluable!” the human ringleader thought, his heart racing.

Thud.

After finishing his prayer, Spring Boxer was about to rise when his eyes suddenly rolled back, and he collapsed unconscious for no apparent reason.

As the others looked on in astonishment, Bing Xiao picked up Spring Boxer. "Don't worry, this is normal. Now follow me down the mountain. We have a temporary shelter at the base."

Chapter 693: Experience Inheritor

A small stream flowed gently past the brown-and-yellow wooden cabin, its waters clear enough to see the bottom.

A rainbow of seven colors hung high in the sky.

Tranquil Hill had become a distant snow-capped peak.

The Bayonet Gang members had entered the wooden cabin.

Here, Spring Boxer slowly opened his eyes, gradually regaining consciousness, “Where am I?”

Bing Xiao stood by his bedside and answered him.

He then asked Spring Boxer, “How do you feel?”

Spring Boxer still looked dazed: “I think I had a dream. In it, I was part of an army fighting a pack of ice plains wolves.”

“Strangely, the battle qi I used was the ice battle skill of our gang leader.”

Bing Xiao nodded: "That's Teng Dong Lang's battle experience. You became his coffin bearer, and now you've placed his ice coffin within the Tranquil Hill. In the days ahead, you'll gradually receive much of his combat wisdom—how he used battle skill, how he cultivated himself, how he led armies into battle, and more."

“This is the true meaning behind his choosing you as coffin bearer.”

Spring Boxer's eyes widened in astonishment at this revelation.

Soon, a sorrowful expression spread across his face. He missed Teng Dong Lang deeply. The latter had shown him immense kindness, and even in death, Spring Boxer continued to receive Teng Dong Lang's care.

"Live well with Teng Dong Lang's experience. Strive to become stronger. Only then will you live up to his earnest hopes for you." Bing Xiao encouraged Spring Boxer.

Spring Boxer pressed, “When will the gang leader be revived?”

Bing Xiao hesitated, but ultimately chose honesty: "Regrettably, he may never be revived again. When you began absorbing Teng Dong Lang's lifetime experience, it signaled his soul had already begun dissipating within Tranquil Hill. These precious combat insights stem from his memories, which reside within the soul itself. A soul stripped of memory forgets who it once was."

Spring Boxer's mouth fell open, “Then I'd rather not have this combat experience! I want the leader revived!”

Bing Xiao shook his head. "This isn't something we can simply choose to do. Truthfully, I've never heard of anyone I know being resurrected in Tranquil Hill. To me, Tranquil Hill's true purpose is preserving our ancestors' combat wisdom and passing it to the next generation of successors. You are Teng Dong Lang's chosen coffin bearer, his successor. Therefore, the better you live and the stronger you become in the future, the more it proves Teng Dong Lang's choice was correct. Do you understand what I mean?"

Spring Boxer silently shed tears. After a moment, he nodded heavily, accepting this harsh reality.

Bing Xiao sighed, patted his shoulder, and led him out of the small cabin onto the green grass in front of it.

There, Bing Xiao gathered the others and informed them that “Spring Boxer had just acquired Teng Dong Lang's lifetime combat experience.”

This elicited gasps from the group.

“Is such a miraculous thing even possible?”

“Sharing one person's combat experience with another saves so much time.”

"The successor can dedicate all their time to training. Such exceptional combat knowledge could transform a battlefield novice into a seasoned veteran or even surpass that level—in an instant."

Everyone was talking about it.

Lan Zao felt a pang of suspicion. He recalled the memory fabrication device aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

As one of the survivors, Lan Zao understood the function of memory crystals.

He thought to himself: “Compared to memory crystals, Tranquil Hill's role is more like the Grand Arena Medal in the commander's hands.”

Lan Zao naturally drew some connections.

In truth, the essence of all three was tied to the soul.

The survivors had obtained the memory fabrication device from the War Merchant and speculated that this alchemy apparatus was likely acquired from the undead faction.

The memory fabrication device originated from necromancers, while Tranquil Hill = the divine domain of the Dueling God, and the Grand Arena Medal traced their roots to the Demon God Serene Fury.

Whether necromancers or deities, all possess the ability to unearth secrets from souls. Extracting combat experience from souls is not uncommon.

After detailing the events surrounding the Spring Boxer, Bing Xiao swept a meaningful glance across the assembled crowd and declared, "You can achieve this too."

This statement astonished the assembly.

The human ringleader urgently pressed, “Lord Bing Xiao, what do you mean?”

Bing Xiao elaborated, "Though only 38 tombstones stand in Tranquil Hill, countless souls have been laid to rest here throughout history. Only when both their bodies and souls completely dissipate do their tombstones vanish."

“It's hard to fathom how much battle wisdom lies within Tranquil Hill.”

"Unlike elemental crystals, which are utterly depleted after use, this knowledge persists like the pages of a book, enduring here."

“We, standing within the divine domain, can also draw upon this knowledge and experience.”

“Yet there is only one prerequisite to achieve this.”

“That is - to convert, to embrace the faith of the Dueling God!”

The Bayonet Gang members fell silent.

“Convert my faith...”

“How could that possibly be done?”

Conversion was exceedingly difficult.

Some present had worshiped a single deity since childhood, their faith deeply ingrained.

Others, however, held only a casual belief, indifferent to adding another deity to their pantheon.

The human ringleader appeared outwardly tempted, yet inwardly grasped Bing Xiao’s true intent in gathering them.

“He seeks to use faith to screen talent and guard against potential traitors.”

“Though I successfully infiltrated Tranquil Hill, my current position is likely becoming precarious!”

The human ringleader and Shuang Tu belonged to the same organization. However, due to the urgency of the situation at the time, the human ringleader had remained within the Bayonet Gang's stronghold. This prevented effective communication between the two.

As the mastermind behind the scenes, Shuang Tu had united the vast majority of silver level factions to besiege the Bayonet Gang. At the critical moment, he spared no expense, deploying a costly artifact that amplified the divine power of Tranquil Hill upon its summoning. This surge engulfed not only the interior of the Bayonet Gang's stronghold but also everyone within a significant radius outside.

Thus, entering Tranquil Hill had been an unexpected turn of events for the human ringleader.

"This is the enemy's crucial secret stronghold. Getting in was difficult; getting out will likely be even harder!" The human ringleader had already come to terms with this.

Bing Xiao smiled as he surveyed the Bayonet Gang members, his tone gentle, “I'm not forcing you to convert. I'm merely reminding you: we'll be living here for a while.”

"During this time, even if you don't worship the Dueling God, your presence within the divine domain of Tranquil Hill will grant you some infusion of combat experience. When these occur, do not be overly astonished."

"The key point is this: if you worship the Dueling God, the efficiency of this experience infusion will increase significantly. The deeper your faith, the more combat wisdom you draw from Tranquil Hill, and the greater your rewards."

“It is even possible that you might become a holy warrior of the Dueling God, earning the privilege of being buried within Tranquil Hill after death.”

“You know the legend, though the probability is low, warriors buried here may indeed be resurrected!”

Silence fell over the crowd.

Everyone actively processed and absorbed the meaning of these words.

After speaking, Bing Xiao dismissed the assembly.

Members of the Bayonet Gang eagerly began training around the wooden hut.

After inspecting the perimeter, Bing Xiao returned to the hut.

Inside, facing the ice-capped peaks beyond the window, he knelt in prayer, recounting the cracks appearing on the tombstones of the Axe Gang leader and the other two.

“Perhaps the others have already noticed this situation.”

"Whatever happens, we must recover the bodies of the Axe Gang leader and the others at all costs. But this is no longer a matter I can interfere with."

Ice Sculpture Kingdom, Snow Rabbit City, inside an upscale inn.

Zi Di regarded the three corpses before her with a complex expression: the Axe Gang leader, Lin, and Jia Bing.

All three bodies were gold level materials.

When Zong Ge brought them back, Zi Di had already performed emergency processing. During the initial handling, she had already noticed some peculiar and unusual aspects.

Upon arriving in Snow Rabbit City, Zi Di thoroughly processed the three corpses and confirmed the presence of abnormalities in each.

"Typically, after death, a soul is guided away by the deity it worships. Even if not guided, it would plunge into the abyss. In any case, souls swiftly separate from their bodies."

"But these three were different. Traces of divine magic were present on their bodies, binding their souls within the corpses. Though their souls are fading, the process is exceedingly slow."

“Perhaps Teng Dong Lang's situation is similar to theirs.”

Zi Di naturally thought of Teng Dong Lang.

She too had witnessed the battle at Snow Bird Harbor's docks.

Back then, a rocket assault boat had run aground midway. To salvage Teng Dong Lang's ice coffin from that vessel, Bing Xiao and the others, who had already reached Feng Lian's pirate ship, turned back. They didn't rest until they retrieved the coffin.

The ice coffin contained Teng Dong Lang's sealed corpse. Though dead, Teng Dong Lang still held immense significance for the Bayonet Gang.

Connecting the dots, Zi Di suddenly surmised: Teng Dong Lang's situation was likely similar to these three!

“So, the reason we were attacked was because the captain killed Teng Dong Lang?”

“Teng Dong Lang may appear merely as the leader of the Bayonet Gang, but behind the scenes, he was likely a member of another major organization.”

“And the members of this organization must share a common faith!”

As for what that faith specifically entailed, Zi Di couldn't be certain.

She felt the most likely candidate was the Snowfield War God.

This deity was one of the principal gods of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Though holding the divine authority of the War God, he focused more intently on warfare within snowy terrain.

Chapter 694: Zong Ge vs Zi Di

Stepping out of the inn room, Zi Di relayed her discovery to Zong Ge: the three gold level corpses, including the Axe Gang leader, could temporarily preserve their souls.

Beyond that, she also shared her conjecture regarding the Snowfield War God.

Zong Ge's brow furrowed deeply.

For Zi Di's discovery and conjecture implied that the scale of the enemy they faced far exceeded previous estimates, it was highly likely a colossal force!

Zong Ge's previous battle had been a resounding success, instantly eliminating three gold level enemies. This was undoubtedly a major blow, a crippling setback that would surely instill profound fear!

But if the enemy's forces were vast, this intimidation wouldn't be the final outcome - far from it.

Retaliation was inevitable!

Zong Ge stated, “They don't know how we eliminated the three gold level members.”

“Therefore, the most likely course of action is gathering intelligence and conducting full-scale reconnaissance.”

“Once they figure it out, or grow impatient, the retaliation we face will be terrifying, like thunder crashing down from the heavens.”

The half-beastman sighed, “Such hatred is impossible to resolve.”

“For now, we can only focus on strengthening ourselves, adapting to any changes.”

Zi Di shook her head slightly. "A faction this large must have numerous connections. They lurk in the shadows, likely unable to operate openly. Perhaps we could expose them, then leverage the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's connections, the Li Jian Clan, or even the official channels of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to deal with them."

Zong Ge paused, startled. Her words struck a chord.

As a merchant, Zi Di naturally prioritized leveraging existing forces. This time, she had anticipated Zong Ge's thoughts.

Inspired, Zong Ge acknowledged, "You're right. They must be a clandestine faction. The Bayonet Gang was wiped out without ever revealing their connection or receiving support from the Axe Gang."

"The Axe Gang is based in Blood Kin City, closest to Snow Bird Harbor. They had every capability to provide backup. Yet when the Bayonet Gang fell, they didn't lift a finger."

"If this was because the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord acted too swiftly, then why didn't the Axe Gang intervene when Bing Xiao and the others escaped Snow Bird Harbor?"

“The Axe Gang leader sought revenge against us for Teng Dong Lang's death, yet he's always acted in the shadows, never openly deploying the Axe Gang's strength.”

“From the ambush in Cold Wind Valley to our counter-ambush in the blizzard last time, they've always tried to eliminate us secretly.”

“If they could act openly, why go to such lengths?”

“Right! They have their own teleportation formation!”

Back then, the Axe Gang leader and Lin used a hidden teleportation formation to take a shortcut, joining Jia Bing ahead of schedule to ambush Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their group midway.

Zong Ge's tone grew urgent as he continued, "They intercepted us early because they must have secretly constructed that teleportation formation. That violates the laws of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!"

“Exposing them and leveraging the Kingdom's official channels could turn their own tactics against them.”

“But we must choose our approach carefully.”

“Their ability to grow in secret suggests powerful patrons shielding them from above.”

Zi Di felt a chill run down her spine, fully agreeing with Zong Ge's assessment.

Regardless of other factors, their military status alone formed an incredibly tight-knit network. The influence of their connections behind the scenes could not be underestimated.

“For now, let's avoid involving the Li Jian Clan.” Zi Di's primary concern was that the Li Jian Clan commanded the kingdom's Second Navy, boasting overwhelming military backing.

It was possible that members of the Li Jian Clan were aligned with figures like the Axe Gang leader and Teng Dong Lang.

“Perhaps we could seek assistance from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.”

"I believe it's still safe for now. We shouldn't casually reveal our strength in slaying three gold level opponents...but informing him about our ambush in Cold Wind Valley might be feasible."

After discussing it for a while, the two headed to the dueling arena.

Snow Rabbit City boasts dozens of dueling arenas of varying sizes.

This particular arena was small and unremarkable, utterly inconspicuous.

The two entered without any spectators present.

This was a private duel.

After a thorough check to confirm no observers were monitoring them, Zi Di and Zong Ge stood facing each other, ready to begin.

Having proven themselves in combat, the snake shadow ghost and rat eye ghost fully demonstrated the strengths of the new undead faction in reconnaissance, they were reliable allies.

Without further ado, the duel commenced.

Zong Ge, hands empty, took a stride forward, charging toward Zi Di.

Zi Di gently waved her Soaring Flame Staff, casting a Flight spell upon herself to step back.

Zong Ge activated his battle skill: Rapid Advance, outpacing Zi Di to close the distance.

Unfazed, Zi Di tapped her gold level belt.

A spatial ripple erupted, and three silver level ice gargoyles abruptly materialized to block Zong Ge.

One bore fearsome claws, another sported broad wings and astonishing speed, while the last blasted icy beams from its eyes, specializing in ranged attacks.

The clawed ice gargoyle primarily entangled Zong Ge, while the winged ice gargoyle provided flanking support, sealing gaps in the defense whenever Zong Ge attempted to break through.

In an instant, the three ice gargoyles encircled Zong Ge at the center. Their movements were calculated, their coordination seamless, creating a fierce and chaotic battle.

Though clearly a master of weapons, Zong Ge deliberately fought unarmed.

He channeled his gold battle qi, conjuring clusters of ranged attacks from thin air. Like a human artillery platform, he relentlessly pounded the ice gargoyles.

The ice gargoyles possessed exceptional defense; even under continuous assault, they remained unshaken.

Zong Ge grew increasingly adept as the battle progressed.

Having recently ascended to gold level, he desperately needed such combat experience to grasp the unique properties of gold level battle qi.

His previous battle against the Axe Gang leader and two others had been fought at full strength, consuming vast amounts of pearl bubbles, leaving no room for this kind of practical training.

Zi Di's eyes flashed with intense focus.

Controlling the ice gargoyles proved immensely beneficial for her as well. Many facts could only be deeply understood through actual combat. Practical experience was something that couldn't be gained through ordinary cultivation.

Though she was only at the iron level, she was a ghost by nature, possessing mental prowess. Combined with the Burden Alleviation Spell and Karma Removal Spell, Zi Di possessed mental strength not inferior to that of a silver level.

This made controlling the silver level ice gargoyles effortless for her.

Three ice gargoyles gradually showed signs of exhaustion. Once their injuries reached a certain threshold, Zi Di proactively replaced them.

Soon, the three ice gargoyles were replaced and her defenses were watertight again.

Zi Di obtained a total of twelve ice gargoyles from Jia Bing's laboratory, each with their own differences and characteristics. Among them was even a gold level ice gargoyle, though it remained unfinished.

She rotated them constantly, familiarizing herself with each of the twelve ice gargoyles' differences and characteristics.

Zong Ge displayed astonishing warrior talent. He began condensing his gold battle qi into various weapons, wielding them with his hands.

As a weapons master, Zong Ge's combat effectiveness was greatly affected by whether he had weapons.

Now armed, even if only with gold level battle qi, his combat power surged dramatically. This forced Zi Di to simultaneously control six ice gargoyles just to barely hold the line.

But this strained her mental energy rapidly, and she gradually struggled to keep up.

However, Zong Ge's combat strength declined even faster than hers.

Suddenly, a flash of crimson light erupted from Zong Ge's body.

He rolled backward in a hasty evasion, dodging the ice gargoyle's claw strike. Retreating in disarray, he shifted from offense to defense.

“Blood Blade Enemy has activated again!” Zi Di understood, yet she did not pause her assault. Instead, she intensified her command over the ice gargoyles, launching a full-scale offensive.

In their previous battle, Zong Ge had been struck by the Axe Gang leader's death throes counterattack.

The power of the Blood Vengeance Axe had seeped deep into his bones, proving nearly impossible to purge. Periodically, it would flare up, striking at his weak points.

Zi Di attacked with all her might, yet she couldn't detect the very source where this power had taken root. However, through subsequent investigations and purchased intelligence, they had uncovered many secrets of the Blood Vengeance Axe, this unique gold level weapon.

Blood Blade Enemy was an inherent attack ability of the Blood Vengeance Axe. The energy it emitted could penetrate deep into an enemy's body, draining their strength to fuel its own growth. Once it reached a certain threshold, this energy would shift from a dormant state into an aggressive one.

When embedded within an enemy, it would immediately unleash brutal attacks both inside and out.

If the enemy failed to react in time, they would suffer severe injuries, or even death.

While engaging in a training battle with Zi Di, Zong Ge remained constantly vigilant about his physical condition. The moment a Blood Blade activated, he immediately mobilized his internal battle qi to defend.

The Blood Blade's assault directly disrupted his combat rhythm, forcing him to shift from offense to defense.

Facing the ice gargoyle's fierce attack, Zong Ge blocked left and right, ultimately relying solely on a makeshift weapon forged from gold level battle qi to withstand the blow.

This wasn't their first training battle. Zi Di could clearly sense Zong Ge's progress.

"Zong Ge's combat talent is truly remarkable! After just a few training sessions, his improvement is practically visible to the naked eye. Meanwhile, I've mostly been getting familiar with the ice gargoyles. Sigh..."

Zi Di's thoughts drifted to the commander.

Zong Ge's promotion to gold level, coupled with such combat talent, would undoubtedly pose a formidable challenge to the youngster's current leadership position!

Before, Zi Di had hoped the dragonman youngster would appear soon to join them.

Now, she wished he would arrive later, buying her more time to avoid an early confrontation with Zong Ge.

“Battle qi cultivation cannot be rushed.”

“Even if we buy some time, it'll be difficult for the commander to bridge the gap between him and Zong Ge.”

“After all, he's only at the silver level, still far from the peak.”

“However, there are other ways to enhance personal combat strength.”

“Like cultivating magic alongside it.”

“The bloodline of the Flame Dragon King is legend level, granting exceptional aptitude for fire magic.”

Zi Di pondered and worried for the youngster, unaware that the young commander had already taken such a path.

Yet it differed from Zi Di's expectations—he cultivated magic not in his dragonman form, but as a fishman.

Spell - Spring Well.

After chanting the incantation, the fishman youngster tapped a finger against the metal wall. Instantly, a spring welled up, releasing a trickle of clear water.

Zhong Yong, standing nearby, looked stunned, “Lord, you succeeded on your very first attempt! You possess extraordinary magic talent!”

Chapter 695: Zhong Yong – My Lord, You are a Genius!

Fishman Zhong Yong was a silver level magician who had practiced magic for years, yet he had never encountered a case like that of the fishman youngster.

After the fishman youngster began his magic training under Zhong Yong's guidance, the innate talent he displayed left the latter deeply astonished.

“What level of bloodline did I absorb from Hai Ping's saint corpse this time?” the fishman youngster wondered inwardly.

He had not practiced any magic while in his dragonman form, and the concentration of the Flame Dragon King's bloodline differed from that of the fishman bloodline he had absorbed this time.

Thus, he had no point of reference.

Yet the fishman youngster was certain: the fishman bloodline he had absorbed must be of exceptionally high level!

He had felt this distinctly the very first time he attempted to cast a spell.

When memorizing the magic model for Spring Well, it felt remarkably simple. After one look, he remembered it with no possibility of forgetting.

Before casting it for the first time, the fishman Zhong Yong shared various insights, experiences, and tricks with him. When it came time to actually cast the spell, it flowed as naturally as breathing, so effortless that those insights and tricks felt superfluous!

“Spring Well is merely the lowest level spell to learn.”

“Let us continue our training.”

Facing Zhong Yong's effusive praise, the fishman youngster remained remarkably composed.

Impressed by the youngster's poise, Zhong Yong silently admired him and proceeded to teach him other fundamental water attribute spells.

The magic models for these were all remarkably straightforward.

The fishman youngster glanced over them briefly before committing them all to memory.

Just like Spring Well, his first attempts at casting these spells were all successful. Not a single failure, not even a hint of one.

“These basic spells hold no challenge for your Lordship.”

“We can try some higher level spells now.” Zhong Yong suggested proactively.

His enthusiasm for teaching the youngster grew ever stronger. This was his first encounter with someone of such innate talent, and he was deeply curious to discover the limits of the fishman youngster's abilities.

“Speaking of which,” the fishman youngster inquired, “what exactly defines the classification of spell levels?”

This seemingly basic question caused Zhong Yong to pause slightly, reaffirming his realization that the fishman youngster before him was indeed a complete novice in magic.

Zhong Yong replied, “There are many methods for classifying spells.”

“Generally speaking, spell levels align with the levels of transcendents. From lowest to highest: bronze level, iron level, silver level, gold level... legend level.”

“This is the most common classification system, based on the casting capabilities of beings at corresponding levels. For instance, bronze level spells are spells most bronze level magicians can cast. The same applies to gold level spells and so forth."

The fishman youngster inquired again, “But I've heard of tactical-class, battlefield-class, and strategic-class spells?”

Zhong Yong nodded and explained, “That classification is based on a spell's actual power.”

“Generally, some lower level spells are classified as tactical-class.”

“Battlefield-class spells are at least gold level.”

“Most renowned strategic-class spells are essentially legend level, with extremely stringent casting requirements.”

The fishman youngster nodded repeatedly, grasping the distinctions and connections.

Zhong Yong continued, “These are merely classification methods for spells and aren't entirely precise.”

“Extremely rare silver level spells can also be rated as battlefield-class.”

"In the most common classification, if a caster possesses exceptional talent, they can fully unleash silver level spells at the bronze level. This is commonly known as casting beyond one's level."

“Take you, for instance, my lord. Though you've only just begun your cultivation and hold the lowest bronze level magician title, your displayed talent is exceptionally strong. You could easily cast iron level spells beyond your level.”

"My Lord, forgive my boldness, but what exactly is your bloodline? I'm truly curious!"

The fishman youngster shook his head, his face filled with regret, “I too yearn to know the answer to that question. Truthfully, I had hoped to glean some clues from you.”

Zhong Yong sighed, “My Lord, though I am a fishman, I grew up in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. While I possess knowledge of fishmen, it is almost entirely derived from books.”

"Your bloodline traits are not pronounced, making it difficult to assess from outward appearance alone. I cannot discern the truth of your lineage."

The fishman youngster felt no surprise at Zhong Yong's response.

He silently lamented: "If only Zi Di were here. She could use a bloodline-testing potion to uncover the truth of my fishman bloodline."

At this stage, without Zi Di present, the fishman youngster had no means to conduct a bloodline test.

Though the tower spirit remained by his side, its current capabilities were limited to calculations, such as refining potion formulas or perfecting alchemy diagrams.

Conducting a complete potion blood test from start to finish, however, was beyond the tower spirit's capacity.

Too many unpredictable variables existed in such a process. Each test result necessitates continuous hypothesis refinement while simultaneously invalidating numerous prior assumptions.

Especially in the early stages, the magician had to intuitively select a testing path from an overwhelming sea of possibilities.

This was a test of spiritual insight.

The tower spirit itself was damaged and required specialized treatment for any hope of recovery.

After obtaining the soul crystals, Cang Xu attempted to use it to heal the tower spirit, but it proved ineffective.

This was precisely why Cang Xu has persistently sought the ship spirit restoration scroll held by Jian Gui.

Unfortunately, Jian Gui intended to submit this scroll as crucial evidence and showed no willingness to trade it.

Given Zhong Yong's status and skill as an alchemist, he was actually qualified to conduct the bloodline potion test.

However, his loyalty had yet to be fully verified, and he was far from being trusted with core secrets.

In truth, assigning Zhong Yong to the Deep Sea Monster Fish was already a considerable risk.

After all, he was a silver level magician. Despite the meticulously crafted magic contract, the survivors still needed to observe him over the long term.

No matter what, from the fishman youngster's terrifying learning speed, his fishman bloodline was definitely high level.

But this was not enough.

The fishman youngster also needed the most precise answers.

With those answers, he could break through based on his bloodline, specifically select certain marine life, absorb their bloodline essence, and effectively supplement his own fishman bloodline.

Once the concentration of his fishman bloodline increased, the youngster's innate talents would grow even more.

“With Zi Di absent, the tower spirit and Zhong Yong are unusable. The only remaining option is to rely on Cang Xu.”

“Cang Xu will inquire through prayer to obtain the answer from the Charming Blue Goddess.”

“But Cang Xu's current level of faith and position within the church are insufficient.”

“To gain the answer for my own fishman bloodline, I must first secure the answer for his bloodline.”

That was the basic plan.

The fishman youngster began his magic training, leveraging his superior bloodline and astonishing talent to cast spells beyond his level.

Once he mastered the Undead Bloodline Detection Spell, he could use it on Cang Xu.

By then, Cang Xu's bloodline mutation would surely have stabilized completely.

After obtaining Cang Xu's bloodline answers, they could lie in wait at the Ocean Mother Nest.

Whenever Zhou Zhang presided over blood sacrifices outside, the fishman youngster would strike first whenever the sacrificed undead were transported into the Mother Nest.

He would hunt down these undead, extract their bloodline essence using the Bloodcore, then purify Cang Xu's bloodline concentration through the Bloodline Purification Formation.

As Cang Xu's bloodline concentration increased, his aptitude would rise, and his ability to forge memories would become more proficient. Thus, in subsequent prayers, he could garner greater favor and receive more divine blessings.

Through this process, his position within the church would also advance.

As his rank within the church rises, Cang Xu would gain greater involvement in the grand Charming Blue Ritual, securing better divine blessings, creating a positive feedback loop.

Upon reaching a certain rank, Cang Xu could preside over rituals himself.

He would master divine spells, enabling survivors to wield the pearl bubbles more effectively—much like the naga priests wielding divine spells during the Two Eyes Island battle.

Moreover, Cang Xu might even command the divine artifact, Mermaid Fairy Tale.

Reaching this level represents the optimal outcome.

According to the fishman youngster's prior speculation, his recent teleportation, along with his ship, into the Ocean Mother Nest was caused by Charming Blue Goddess' divine artifact, Mermaid Fairy Tale.

If Cang Xu could manipulate the Mermaid Fairy Tale, there existed a possibility of teleporting the Deep Sea Monster Fish out once more.

This way, the fishman youngster and Cang Xu could safely leave the ocean trench without anyone noticing, keeping Zhou Zhang completely in the dark from start to finish.

Achieving this step would mean a major victory for the survivors!

With the Ocean Mother Nest preserved and Zhou Zhang harboring no suspicions, the fishman youngster and Cang Xu could teleport back in whenever she held another blood sacrifice. Right under her nose, they could effortlessly replenish the divine artifact's power and mass-produce pearl bubbles.

Achieving this would render both the dual seals binding Cang Xu and the fatal hidden mechanism of Jian Gui irrelevant.

Divine blessings could easily resolve these complications.

His cultivation progress accelerated rapidly.

The fishman youngster swiftly mastered basic water attribute spells. After a brief glance, he memorized all iron level spells.

His success rate in casting spells beyond his level was 100%!

Zhong Yong was once again stunned.

Though he had surmised the youngster could cast spells beyond his level, he hadn't anticipated such effortless mastery.

“Lord, where exactly lies your limit?” Zhong Yong's gaze toward the fishman youngster now held unmistakable reverence.

Admiring strength was one of the fundamental traits shared by nearly all sentient beings.

However, when practicing silver level spells, the fishman youngster encountered trouble.

Though he could use his spirit to accurately map out the magic model, his magic power was completely insufficient.

Zhong Yong declared, "Lord, it's only your magician level holding you back! With your talent, you'll reach iron level in no time."

He was even more excited than the fishman youngster.

Chapter 696: Cultivating Necromancy Again

“Iron level...” The fishman youngster nodded, a sense of urgency welling up within him.

According to the plan he and Cang Xu had devised, time was a critical factor.

Cultivation couldn't be rushed, yet the dragonman youngster needed to elevate his magician level as swiftly as possible at this juncture.

Much like a fighter's cultivation, advancing a fighter's level primarily relied on battle qi techniques. Magicians, however, depended on meditation methods.

“What do you think of this meditation method?” The fishman youngster handed the Tidal Sound meditation method to Zhong Yong for review.

Zhong Yong had only read a third of it before his face lit up with admiration, “Lord Hou Lang, this meditation method is truly exceptional. It suits your fishman bloodline perfectly. It far surpasses my own meditation method!"

“That's quite rare.”

“To be honest, my meditation method was exchanged from the Li Jian Clan - a high level item beyond the ordinary.”

The fishman youngster laughed heartily: “In that case, I'll gift this meditation method to you.”

Zhong Yong was stunned: “Lord, this is far too valuable...”

“Consider it a personal token of my gratitude for your teachings.” With that, the fishman youngster patted Zhong Yong on the shoulder, “Besides, fishmen are rare in the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. As your senior, it's only right to mentor the younger generation.”

Zhong Yong felt even more grateful.

The youngster concealed his true identity as Commander Long Fu, presenting himself only as “Senior Hou Lang.”

Combined with the aura of kinship bestowed by their shared bloodline, Zhong Yong felt incredibly fortunate. Having just joined this unfamiliar organization, he'd already encountered a benefactor who favored and sought to mentor him!

Consequently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps itself grew much more welcoming in his eyes.

The fishman youngster offered Zhong Yong a few more words of encouragement before dismissing him to continue his alchemy practice.

Armed with the Tidal Sound meditation method, Zhong Yong left the meditation chamber brimming with motivation.

The room was once again empty except for the fishman youngster.

Rather than meditating immediately, the youngster activated his Bloodcore to undergo a hybrid transformation.

In the next instant, his appearance underwent a drastic transformation.

His scales turned dark gray, twisted patterns forming grotesque faces on his chest and back.

This was the fishman youngster's activation of the Ghostface Fish transformation.

Only with this layer of bloodline enhancement did he gain the qualification to absorb negative energy, convert it into undead magic power, and practice necromancy magic.

This demonstrated that bloodline formed the foundation.

Without the corresponding lineage, many kinds of transcendent cultivation proved inaccessible even at the most basic level.

Earlier, the fishman youngster had already depleted all water attribute magic power from his magic pool.

This allowed him to directly absorb negative energy and convert it into undead magic power.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish's meditation chamber was specifically designed for War Merchant. Its sophisticated facilities could directly consume corresponding materials from the storage room, absorb energy, and create an environment suitable for a magician's meditation.

This consumption method was vastly superior and more efficient than Zi Di crushing undead materials. A single batch of identical materials could extract more negative energy.

It was worth mentioning that these undead materials were mostly of the Ghostface Fish.

Ghostface Fish possess both water and undead attributes.

They could be sold as water attribute materials in the market while also skirting the line of undead.

Consequently, some regions prohibited the sale of such materials, while others, like Two Eyes Island, allowed open trade.

Since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps began acquiring undead materials, all obtainable specimens on the market had been Ghostface Fish carcasses.

Previously, the dragonman youngster attempted to transform into a ghost but failed precisely because these materials couldn't convert him into a ghost-type undead lifeform.

At best, it could only transform into a physical entity like the Ghostface Fish.

The fishman youngster refined water attribute magic power using the Tidal Sound meditation method. Now, to refine undead magic power, he employed the Death Qi meditation method.

The former technique was selected from the War Merchant's database. The latter originated from the orthodox soul aspect necromancer inheritance, obtained by Cang Xu.

“The efficiency of extracting undead magic power pales in comparison to extracting water magic power.”

“After all, the Ghostface Fish is merely a silver level magic beast, and this Ghostface Fish bloodline is only a silver level bloodline.”

This was already the best choice available to the fishman youngster, even though the Deep Sea Monster Fish's storage held undead materials bordering on gold level.

Absorbing such materials could grant the fishman youngster a gold level undead bloodline, but it proved less useful than the Ghostface Fish bloodline.

Because the quantity of the gold level undead material paled in comparison to the Ghostface Fish carcass.

Having absorbed vast quantities of the Ghostface Fish carcass, the fishman youngster had already pushed his bloodline concentration to its absolute limit.

A 100% silver level Ghostface Fish bloodline versus less than 5% gold level undead bloodline, the former clearly held the advantage.

The fishman youngster began teaching himself necromancy magic.

Drawing on his prior experience learning water magic, he swiftly mastered the most fundamental necromancer incantations.

Yet he distinctly sensed this learning process was more arduous than before, demanding active effort.

His first attempts at casting these basic necromancy magic spells weren't entirely successful. Nor did he experience that effortless, almost breathing-like naturalness he'd felt before.

Fortunately, the foundation provided by his fishman bloodline remained, allowing him to grasp new concepts by analogy to a large extent.

His formidable spirit also made it effortless for him to construct the models for necromancy magic.

After mastering the foundational necromancy magic, the fishman youngster wasted no time advancing toward iron level spells.

During this period, the tower spirit handled external surveillance.

Its primary focus was Cang Xu.

Should Cang Xu face mortal danger, the youngster would shatter the Mother Nest to launch an emergency rescue!

In the youngster's heart, Cang Xu's life held far greater value than the entire Ocean Mother Nest.

For now, Cang Xu's venture appeared successful.

Though he had temporarily passed Zhou Zhang's inspection, the latter had not lifted the dual seals binding Cang Xu.

As for Jian Gui, that was even less likely.

Before this teleportation blood sacrifice, the cunning Jian Gui had hinted to Cang Xu that Doctor Sui Shi still held means over him. This was a message: Though you are bound to me, the true culprit is not me, but Doctor Sui Shi.

Should Cang Xu later discover a fatal trap planted within him, this excuse could be immediately offered - Doctor Sui Shi was the culprit.

Cang Xu arrived at Jian Gui's dwelling.

Accompanying him were Xiao Wang Zi, Qi Ling, and Ku Feng.

Just as they had done when sharing experiences about forging memories, they sat cross-legged in a circle upon entering the room.

“Cang Xu, it's such a relief you made it back alive!” Jian Gui exclaimed warmly.

The others wore varied expressions.

Xiao Wang Zi was actually the happiest. These past days he had been tormented by guilt.

Ku Feng's gaze was open and honest. In his view, competition where everyone fought for survival using every means necessary was perfectly justified!

Qi Ling's expression was gloomy.

She never imagined Cang Xu would return alive.

Her previous schemes and plots against Cang Xu had now become his legitimate grounds for dealing with her.

Chapter 697: Jian Gui – My Situation is Getting Better

Cang Xu's return was most detrimental to Qi Ling.

At this very moment, within Jian Gui's quarters, Qi Ling observed Cang Xu with veiled scrutiny, her emotions in turmoil: “Xu Jia has actually converted his faith!”

“Is conversion really that easy?”

“What kind of god is Charming Blue Goddess? She accepted an undead as her believer so readily?”

Qi Ling couldn't help but inwardly scoff: "Charming Blue Goddess has fallen from grace, she now accepts any believer, how pathetic!"

Cang Xu sighed bitterly: “I too am astonished that this conversion succeeded.”

"Now seeing you all again feels like a dream. Recalling our ordeal in the Ocean Mother Nest, it was perilous beyond measure. The fear still lingers.

Cang Xu's performance was astonishing, and he steered the conversation toward a topic that piqued everyone's interest.

How could they not be interested?

The Ocean Mother Nest concerned their very survival.

Thus, the group pressed Cang Xu for details.

Cang Xu began slowly, recounting the meticulously crafted tale he and the fishman youngster had woven.

He particularly emphasized the scene where the saint level sea beast, Snow Pear Octopus, tested the faithful, painting it vividly with dramatic flair.

“When the octopus's tentacles closed in on me, I was almost certain my end had come.”

"In that moment, the sensation of death descending upon me was suffocating, yet it also seemed to completely transform my faith!”

“To survive, I truly began to worship Charming Blue Goddess.”

“As the octopus's tentacles gradually retreated from me, I was still in a daze.”

…

The group listened with rapt attention. For a time, only Cang Xu's voice echoed within the shack.

Cang Xu was confident his account held no flaws.

Not only had he and the fishman youngster meticulously planned and reviewed it, but crucially, this wasn't a fabrication.

During their time aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the fishman youngster had witnessed multiple teleportation blood sacrifices. Magic recordings documented countless such instances.

All were genuine evidence, authentic material extracted from actual events.

Thus, when Jian Gui and others heard it, none doubted its truth.

Finally, Cang Xu sighed deeply, his face bitter. "Perhaps I should thank that saint level sea beast more. Without its coercion, I wouldn't have faced such immense pressure of death, unable to transform my faith!"

After Cang Xu finished speaking, the shack fell into silence.

He lowered his head, his gaze resting on the sandy seabed, his mood tense.

He, too, was undergoing a trial now.

During his teleportation back, he had faced Zhou Zhang's test. Now, to reintegrate into Jian Gui's group, he needed the necromancers to recognize him anew.

His new identity as a believer of Charming Blue Goddess made the magicians both envious of his experiences and wary of him.

Qi Ling displayed this wariness most clearly.

Throughout Cang Xu's account, she kept glancing at Jian Gui. Her gaze seemed to ask: “Can Xu Jia truly be trusted?”

"Xu Jia's faith has shifted; he now worships the enemy's deity. For the sake of that faith, betraying us is entirely possible."

But Jian Gui seemed oblivious to Qi Ling's glances. Not only did he actively invite Cang Xu to this necromancer gathering, but after Cang Xu finished speaking, he declared: "Xu Jia, you performed brilliantly. Frankly, it surpassed my expectations."

“I owe you my gratitude.”

"Your success proves that even if we fail to forge memories, there remains a way out. You have given us immense confidence and hope."

"Changing allegiance on the fly is incredibly difficult, especially for us necromancers. But you did it!"

“What comforts me most is that even after switching sides, you haven't betrayed us. You're still willing to act alongside us.”

“I didn't gift that undead inheritance in vain!”

Jian Gui spoke with skill, referencing how he had previously “broken his own rules” to grant Cang Xu the intermediate soul aspect inheritance.

Upon hearing this, Cang Xu immediately expressed gratitude while seizing the moment to declare his loyalty, emphasizing the nature of faith: “Though I worship Charming Blue Goddess, it doesn't mean I'll abandon or betray you all.”

“That's entirely different!”

“Besides, the one truly responsible for our captivity and suffering is Zhou Zhang.”

"I worship the Charming Blue Goddess, but I do not acknowledge Zhou Zhang. My grudge against her remains unsettled!"

Cang Xu's words somewhat eased the necromancers' worries and reservations.

Qi Ling's gaze was somber, her expression gloomy.

Xiao Wang Zi averted his eyes, unable to meet Cang Xu's stare, still feeling awkward.

Ku Feng, however, approached Cang Xu directly, inquiring about the specifics of conversion.

For him, after Cang Xu, if the human pirates weren't an option, he would be next. By then, the memory forgery would surely remain incomplete.

Thus, conversion was crucial, perhaps his only path to survival.

Cang Xu didn't reject Ku Feng's invitation. Instead, he agreed to speak with him privately after the gathering to explain everything in detail. What everyone should focus on next was sharing experiences and adjusting their collaboration on the memory fabrication.

Cang Xu noticed significant progress in the memory fabrication process.

He wasn't surprised. Calculating the time, he had missed one gathering session due to participating in the teleportation blood sacrifice.

On the surface, the group discussed prayers and conversion rituals. In reality, they secretly transmitted messages, plotting to fabricate memories and blaspheme the goddess.

The difference this time was that Cang Xu became the ostensible speaker. He shared the divine revelations he had received after his own prayers. Specifically, how a monk of the Charming Blue Church should pray, interpret its doctrines, and so forth.

After the gathering concluded, Cang Xu was personally retained by Jian Gui.

The two conversed privately.

Cang Xu expressed gratitude for Jian Gui's earlier breach of protocol in bestowing upon him the intermediate soul aspect inheritance. The magnanimity and demeanor Jian Gui displayed in this matter had won Cang Xu's deep respect. Cang Xu pledged his full cooperation in forging memories for Jian Gui. Finally, Cang Xu subtly hinted: his interest in other inheritances remained undiminished!

Jian Gui roared with laughter, his chuckles echoing loudly.

“From our first meeting, I recognized you as an exceptional magician.”

“A voracious thirst for knowledge is one of a magician's finest qualities."

“Doctor Sui Shi chose you to learn the Flying Head Spell, likely recognizing your exceptional qualities.”

That's utter nonsense!

Doctor Sui Shi taught the Flying Head Spell only to use Cang Xu as an experimental subject. While ostensibly compensation, imparting the technique was merely to keep Cang Xu as his test subject.

By invoking Doctor Sui Shi, Jian Gui primarily sought to remind Cang Xu of his formidable background, a tactic of intimidation.

Then, shifting his tone, Jian Gui adopted a troubled expression, explaining that while he possessed both intermediate inheritances of the corpse aspect and bone aspect, such sharing was strictly forbidden by principle.

Upon hearing this, Cang Xu knew instantly that he had a chance!

Having dealt with Jian Gui for so long, he understood all too well the nature of his so-called “principles”.

Cang Xu hastily pledged his loyalty and expressed his intense desire for the undead inheritance.

Sure enough, after some evasive talk, Jian Gui hinted: if Cang Xu made a significant contribution—like rescuing a legitimate member of the undead faction (meaning himself), granting him the intermediate inheritance would be entirely within the bounds of principle.

Satisfied with this answer, Cang Xu took his leave.

Watching Cang Xu's retreating figure, Jian Gui narrowed his eyes, equally satisfied.

“Though Xu Jia has embraced the faith of the Charming Blue Goddess, he remains no zealous believer. For the time being, he remains trustworthy and poses no threat.”

"The persona I've cultivated, my leadership, the personal charisma I've maintained, all are working to exert a positive influence on Xu Jia!"

"The hidden trap is confirmed to still be in place. Should Xu Jia betray me, I can kill him in an instant."

But doing so would inevitably trigger a massive investigation by Zhou Zhang. The consequences would be too much for me to bear."

“Keep Xu Jia steady. His desire for the inheritances is perfect, let them be the carrot dangling before the donkey, compelling him to keep fabricating memories for me.”

“Speaking of which, his successful conversion greatly benefits my situation.”

"On one hand, he'll continue contributing to the memory fabrication plan. On the other hand, Zhou Zhang and the others will gain greater confidence in the undead conversion, which will improve their attitude toward us."

"Don't panic, don't rush. Though I'm now a prisoner, my chances of escape have actually grown. That's right, if things continue this way, I'll definitely break free from here. It might even turn out to be a blessing in disguise."

Jian Gui couldn't help but envision the future.

Chapter 698: Cang Xu Socializes

“Jian Gui is temporarily pacified,” Cang Xu murmured to himself on the way back.

For Cang Xu, his current situation was complex.

He had to maintain contact with both Zhou Zhang's faction and Jian Gui, playing both sides while secretly plotting, gaining benefits for his true allegiance and faction.

Cang Xu excelled in such situations. Despite bearing the marks of both factions' schemes, his confidence only grew.

“It all stems from the commander. He's the one who made me feel increasingly at ease.”

Recalling the events on Mystifying Monster Island and the perilous ordeal this time, Cang Xu found himself teetering on the edge of betrayal.

The young commander's timely arrival had been triggered by the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale]'s teleportation effect.

“This is destiny!”

Understanding the chain of events filled Cang Xu with profound reverence for destiny.

He returned to his dwelling - still the same crude shack.

Ku Feng had already arrived.

Cang Xu welcomed him inside and spoke with him privately for quite some time.

Ku Feng, desperate to survive, and pressed for time, moved with decisive urgency.

He asked numerous questions, to which Cang Xu responded with well-reasoned answers, consistent, logically sound, and rich in detail.

Of course, on certain points, Cang Xu deliberately gave vague answers, revealing the state of tension, fear, and utter helplessness he had felt at the time.

This greatly enlightened Ku Feng declared: Cang Xu's psychological state formed the strongest foundation for his successful conversion.

Especially the immense pressure brought by death, in such circumstances, it became an aid.

After their exchange, Ku Feng felt deeply enriched and grew grateful toward Cang Xu.

“If I survive this, I must repay him!”

With this resolve, Ku Feng sought to offer timely feedback: "I walk the path of the corpse aspect. Regarding its inheritance, I'm ashamed to say my knowledge might not even match yours. But as an undead ronin, I possess extensive experience in wielding undead battle qi."

"My cultivation experience is the only thing I can repay you with."

"No need," Cang Xu waved his hand and rejected.

He spoke with genuine sincerity: "It's not that I look down on you, I'm interested in all knowledge. But for you, Ku Feng, my companion, you need to treasure your time more now. There's no need to waste it here. Save it for yourself. Who knows? In a critical moment, an extra minute—or even a second—could mean the difference between life and death."

These words deeply moved Ku Feng.

After all, Ku Feng had previously targeted Cang Xu with the help of Qi Ling and Xiao Wang Zi.

Before seeking Cang Xu's aid, Ku Feng had braced himself for the worst.

Yet from start to finish, Cang Xu remained gentle, not merely putting on a show before Jian Gui, but genuinely considering Ku Feng's well-being.

Cang Xu continued, "What happened before doesn't amount to much. If I were in your place, I'd have done the same. It's just about survival."

“Of course, if we make it out alive, or even regain our freedom, you'll owe me big time!”

Ku Feng was deeply moved.

He took a deep breath, lowering his voice: “I'll remember this favor. I'll repay it someday.”

Ku Feng wasn't one for flowery words, but Cang Xu could sense his sincerity and resolve in that simple reply.

“This debt of yours won't be easily repaid,” Cang Xu chuckled inwardly.

“Come to think of it, taking this risky teleportation back was absolutely the right call.”

“I've completely turned the tables on Jian Gui's crew!”

Based on Cang Xu's understanding of Ku Feng and his own political acumen, Ku Feng was now deeply indebted. He would surely be useful to Cang Xu in the future.

“This means I can ask the commander to intervene and protect Ku Feng as much as possible.”

To Ku Feng, his situation seemed precarious.

But to Cang Xu, it presented an entirely different scenario.

Life or death could now be arranged entirely through the hands of the young commander!

“Qi Ling previously schemed against me... but my survival has actually given me an opportunity to strengthen bonds and break through barriers.”

Cang Xu quickly regained his composure.

This was merely a minor advancement, too insignificant within the grand scheme of the entire plan.

"I am a necromancer, though I have received divine blessing and passed Zhou Zhang's trial, the other believers still regard me with apprehension, wariness, and aloofness."

Cang Xu gazed through the cracks of his makeshift hut toward the golden altar.

Recalling his teleportation back and the inspection by Zhou Zhang, his brow furrowed slightly.

He had passed Zhou Zhang's verification, a process far easier than he had anticipated. Yet it was precisely this ease that left him feeling unsettled, uneasy.

“Does Zhou Zhang truly trust me?”

“If she truly trusts me, why hasn't she proactively lifted my seal?”

“Perhaps I should seek an audience with her myself and request the seal's removal?”

A thought suddenly struck Cang Xu.

It was a form of probing.

The next moment, he dismissed this dangerous notion of testing the waters.

Too reckless, it was not his style.

Most importantly, there was the plan he'd discussed with the young commander! Even sealed, he could still advance that plan.

“No need for unnecessary risks.”

“Look at it another way, this seal on me is the best way to gain Zhou Zhang's trust.”

Cang Xu secretly speculated that perhaps, ever since his teleportation, every move he made had been under Zhou Zhang's strict surveillance and scrutiny.

“Hmm, I must be even more cautious!”

Cang Xu lingered in the shack for a short while before pushing open the door and stepping out.

The area where necromancers gathered had always been isolated as a separate zone by other Charming Blue believers.

Now, Cang Xu voluntarily left this isolated zone and ventured into other areas.

Along the way, he encountered numerous Charming Blue believers. These intelligent sea creatures all regarded him with looks of suspicion, wariness, and warning. Many pointed at him from corners, whispering among themselves, a decidedly unfriendly atmosphere.

Having witnessed Cang Xu's departure, they continued their discussion.

“Who would have thought a necromancer would become one of us?”

“I absolutely won't accept him!”

“This is all the will of the gods. I believe that since he's been granted divine blessing and teleported back, it proves his true heart.”

“Even so, what does that matter? Does sharing the same faith mean we can't be enemies? That poor son of mine never came back. Waaah, waaah, waaah! That vile undead actually returned!"

…

Cang Xu left his lodgings of his own accord, seeking Qing Xin.

He knew Qing Xin's location, among the human pirates.

"Come to think of it, Qing Xin was captured aboard the ghost ship. Yet during imprisonment, Zhou Zhang had her transferred to the human pirates. Looking at how she forced us to select participants for the teleportation blood sacrifice, this Charming Blue Archbishop Zhou Zhang truly has a twisted sense of humor."

Cang Xu felt Zhou Zhang treated their group of captives as casually as a child playing with ants.

"Relying on her mastery of both divine and magic spells, especially her deception magic, Zhou Zhang certainly has reason to look down on us. But might her nature offer some exploitable weakness?"

Lost in thought, Cang Xu unexpectedly encountered Qing Xin halfway through his journey.

Zhou Zhang did not restrict the necromancers' movements, nor did she concern herself with the human prisoners. After all, these captives bore dual seals; Zhou Zhang had no fear they could cause any disturbance.

Qing Xin's face lit up with delight upon seeing Cang Xu, “Magician Xu Jia, I was just looking for you!” she exclaimed.

Like Ku Feng, her motive for seeking Cang Xu was to learn from his successful conversion.

In truth, Qing Xin bore even heavier psychological pressure than Ku Feng.

Under the current rules, the second blood sacrifice ritual would almost certainly target her.

Cang Xu smiled and nodded, “My success this time was sheer luck. I was actually looking for you too, as I believe my experience might be of some help.”

Qing Xin instantly looked deeply moved, nodding vigorously, her eyes reddening.

This period had been incredibly difficult for her.

Had she known things would turn out this way, she never would have agreed to leave Two Eyes Island with Zi Di!

To Qing Xin, Cang Xu was her lifeline.

But in truth, she needn't have worried at all.

Among the captives, she was likely the safest. The young commander was hiding aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish. For the sake of his beloved Zi Di, he would never allow anything to happen to Qing Xin!

As for Cang Xu, he bore not only dual seals but also the deadly trap set by Jian Gui. Though he had seen the fishman youngster, he was still walking a tightrope.

The explanation Cang Xu gave Qing Xin was nearly identical to what he told Ku Feng.

He treated them equally primarily because he knew Zhou Zhang and others were secretly monitoring him. By acting impartially, he demonstrated he had done everything possible.

This act of helping his comrades highlighted his loyalty and emphasized the common ground between the undead and the living. Raising the opinion of Zhou Zhang and the others would also increase their trust in him.

After parting ways with Qing Xin, Cang Xu returned to his quarters. Outwardly, he began his prayers, but secretly, he was forging memories.

The reason he didn't proceed with the operation was that Cang Xu still had to consider Jian Gui's feelings.

Earlier, Cang Xu had assured Jian Gui that he would spare no effort in forging the memories. But after leaving, he'd been wandering about, negotiating with others—what was that about?

This would undoubtedly damage his image in Jian Gui's eyes.

"Besides, Xiao Wang Zi won't see me. He was tormented by guilt before; now, even looking at me makes him uncomfortable."

"Qi Ling... What happened earlier has already severely damaged our relationship. Even if I reach out, she'll be suspicious."

“Truthfully, her existence serves another purpose.”

“I can fabricate a conflict between us, making it explicit. This will reassure Jian Gui, making him believe everything is under control.”

“Using the teleportation blood sacrifice, the commander can capture these undead, even Jian Gui himself.”

“Once my rank advances and I can command the artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] to teleport away, the power of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance will command Jian Gui's respect and force the undead orthodoxy to take notice.”

“My future relations with Jian Gui's faction will improve tremendously!”

Cang Xu's vision was far-reaching; he was already strategizing and acting for future alliances.

The web of relationships surrounding Cang Xu was exceedingly complex.

He had to navigate two distinct roles: Charming Blue believer and necromancer.

He needed to maintain a delicate equilibrium between these identities, constantly maneuvering between the two factions while advancing his own true agenda.

Among the survivors, no one was better suited for this arduous task than him.

Chapter 699: Only by Becoming a Holy Warrior will You be Free

Tranquil Hill.

Snow-capped peaks towered overhead, and the frigid air descended upon this valley of green grass and crimson flowers, leaving only a refreshing chill.

Around the brownish-yellow wooden house, several makeshift shelters had been hastily erected.

These shelters were constructed using local materials, trunks and branches trimmed flat, then roughly assembled.

The Bayonet Gang members resided within these shelters.

The brown wooden house they guarded housed only two individuals: Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer.

No one questioned this arrangement. Even Lan Zao understood that Bing Xiao was the Bayonet Gang's former leader, and since Spring Boxer served as Teng Dong Lang's coffin bearer, he was naturally destined to be the next leader.

Spring Boxer's days were far from easy.

Periodically, waves of overwhelming memories would surge into his mind, sending him into dizzying unconsciousness.

After lying in bed for a while, he would slowly regain consciousness.

He'd stay lucid for a short time before slipping back into unconsciousness.

Through this endless cycle, he absorbed vast amounts of Teng Dong Lang's memories.

Spring Boxer was growing at an astonishing rate!

Bing Xiao took on the responsibility of tending to him day and night.

Suspecting possible traitors among the Bayonet Gang members, Bing Xiao trusted no one else, not even Lan Zao, or rather Hen Fa to them.

The bedridden Spring Boxer stirred once more.

The first thing he saw upon opening his eyes was the old gang leader.

Bing Xiao stood by the window, reading a book in the bright daylight streaming through.

Tranquil Hill was the divine domain of the Dueling God, where night held no meaning, 24 hours of unbroken, radiant light.

Seeing Bing Xiao, Spring Boxer felt an overwhelming sense of security and gratitude surge within him.

His journey to this day owed much to Teng Dong Lang's cultivation, but the old gang leader Bing Xiao's assistance had been equally immense.

Noticing Spring Boxer had awakened, Bing Xiao immediately approached the bedside, “Seems your gains this time were substantial.”

Spring Boxer nodded, “This time, I gained Lord Teng Dong Lang's combat experience. He fought a fierce battle against a ferocious rabbitman warrior atop a snowy mountain. His comrades fell one by one. The original hundred-strong squad was nearly wiped out, leaving only him and three allies. Lord Teng Dong Lang was clinging to life by a thread. It was only through the selfless sacrifice of his comrades that his life was saved."

Bing Xiao realized: "You mean that battle. That rabbitman warrior was called Qing Hong Yan. Though his temper was explosively volatile, he was a persecuted, ill-fated snow rabbitman. You may encounter him in the future."

Spring Boxer paused, his tone tinged with resentment and grievance: “In my memory, it was Teng Dong Lang who defeated him, leaving the rabbitman unconscious. But did that rabbitman giant not die? Why didn't Lord Teng Dong Lang kill him?"

"There must have been explicit military orders at the time. As for the full story, you'll understand it in time." Bing Xiao declined to elaborate.

Spring Boxer lowered his head, a pensive look crossing his face, “No wonder when I experienced that memory, I felt Lord Teng Dong Lang's complex emotions back then, anger, hatred, responsibility, sympathy, and more.”

Spring Boxer had gained access to Teng Dong Lang's memories, effectively reliving some of the pivotal moments from his remarkable life.

By immersing in these memories, he could not only obtain experience but also savor the emotions that Teng Dong Lang felt at that time.

Spring Boxer placed a hand over his heart, his expression complex, “Receiving Lord Teng Dong Lang's experiences feels like he's come back to life, teaching me through a strange kind of guidance. Lord Bing Xiao, sometimes I have this illusion, when I wake up, I feel like Lord Teng Dong Lang is right beside my bed.

Saying this, Spring Boxer's eyes turned red.

Bing Xiao sighed, stepping before Spring Boxer. He grasped the fighter's wrist and pulled him up, “Don't wallow in sentimentality. You've inherited Teng Dong Lang's legacy, now you must strive even harder.”

“This period of absorbing his experience is crucial for you.”

"Stop wasting time. Go eat and drink. You must maintain your physical condition—you didn't eat while unconscious."

Spring Boxer composed himself, got out of bed, and approached the dining table, devouring his meal in large bites.

The table was perpetually laden with abundant food.

This sustenance wasn't hunted from valley beasts or gathered berries by the Bayonet Gang, it was a divine blessing granted through Bing Xiao's prayers!

This was the divine domain, a place of wonder.

As a holy warrior of the Dueling God, Bing Xiao's prayers received instant answers, yielding exquisite delicacies.

While eating and drinking, Spring Boxer's ears twitched slightly, picking up the sounds of the Bayonet Gang members training outside the wooden hut.

He couldn't help but look thoughtful as he addressed Bing Xiao: “Gang Leader, what should we do about those guys like Xiong Ju and Hen Fa?”

Upon hearing this, Bing Xiao's eyes lit up. He smiled with satisfaction, “Indeed. After absorbing Teng Dong Lang's experience, you've truly matured considerably.”

People would change.

A person's maturity stemmed primarily from experience, not age.

The more experiences one accumulated, the more mature they became.

Spring Boxer had lived Teng Dong Lang's life, which was filled with grueling battles, brushes with death, and memories of strategizing, weighing every option, and ultimately commanding armies to victory.

He was maturing.

Crucially, he had gained Teng Dong Lang's memories of being recognized by Tranquil Hill and becoming a holy warrior, deeply understanding Tranquil Hill's significance.

At the critical moment on Flower Skirt Island, he even requested Bing Xiao to bring in Xiong Ju and the others.

Now, however, he began contemplating the most prudent way to handle Xiong Ju and his group. After all, there was a strong possibility of a mole among them!

Bing Xiao stated, “I've already devised a plan for this matter.”

“Their successful conversion in faith will prove their loyalty.”

"After we depart Tranquil Hill, we'll take the newly initiated holy warriors with us. As for the rest, they'll have to be temporarily imprisoned."

Spring Boxer frowned slightly, “Though I'm not a clergyman, I know faith alone can't determine loyalty.”

Bing Xiao nodded slightly, “To be precise, it works like this.”

“Faith can be divided into several levels: casual, ordinary, devout, and fanatical.”

"Casual believers possess some knowledge and understanding of a faith but lack deep study or practice. They profess belief verbally yet do not strictly adhere to its principles in action."

"Ordinary believers are traditional adherents of a religion or faith. They follow its doctrines and rules without extreme zeal. Their understanding and practice vary but generally hold a sense of devotion and reverence toward their religion or belief."

"Devout believers possess a strong sense of identification with their faith and actively practice it in daily life. They strictly adhere to the doctrines and regulations of their faith in their daily lives, ensuring they receive the blessings and protection of their faith."

"Fanatical believers pursue their faith with excessive extremism. Fanatical believers believe only absolute faith can bring salvation or blessings, disregarding all rational thought and action. They may resort to extreme behaviors, often harming themselves or others."

“You've also gained memories of Teng Dong Lang being chosen as a holy warrior, yet the selection criteria remain unclear.”

"To become a holy warrior of the Dueling God, transcendent level is secondary. The most crucial criterion is faith level. Only those of the devout level can become holy warriors."

“So that's how it is.” Spring Boxer realized, but more questions immediately followed, “But even if all holy warriors are devout level, strictly adhering to the Dueling God's doctrines and rules, it doesn't necessarily mean a traitor would defect to us. Faith alone doesn't determine friend or foe.”

Bing Xiao roared with laughter, thoroughly delighted.

He couldn't resist slapping Spring Boxer's shoulder repeatedly, praising, “Young man, your ability to reason like this is truly remarkable.”

“This is the turning point in your life.”

“The true value lies in the wisdom imparted by Teng Dong Lang, not mere combat experience.”

After a pause, Bing Xiao continued, “You're right. Even among holy warriors of the Dueling God, we could be adversaries. Killing one another wouldn't violate His doctrine.”

"If a traitor lurks among us, they might still attain faith and become a holy warrior. Even if we bring them out, it won't stop them from opposing us."

"The crucial point is this: once we become holy warriors, our bodies undergo divine transformation, like hidden mechanisms being activated. Should a traitor ever strike, their very identity as a holy warrior becomes a scythe of death!"

Spring Boxer nodded repeatedly, understanding fully.

Bing Xiao didn't force anyone to convert, but in practice, he was already guiding them.

Taking those who become holy warriors with them, even if there's a mole among them, was nothing to fear. It was because the fate of the mole was no longer in his own hands.

Of course, there were many possibilities, a shift in allegiance could see the mole defecting to their side.

As for the others, they could not prove their loyalty, had entered Tranquil Hill, knew too many secrets, and remained unworthy of becoming holy warriors. With no leverage over them, imprisonment was the only option.

At this thought, Spring Boxer couldn't help but glance out the window, observing the training scenes of some gang members. He silently cheered them on: “Xiong Ju, Hen Fa, everyone... I hope your conversion succeeds. Only by becoming holy warriors can you regain your freedom.”

Lan Zao stood before the wooden hut's main entrance, facing the stream, and suddenly opened his mouth to spit.

A small Air Cannon projectile shot from his mouth, striking the stream. It didn't explode immediately but sank beneath the surface.

Then, with a soft pop, it detonated, sending droplets flying everywhere.

“Interesting.”

“Who would've thought someone in Tranquil Hill had trained in Air Cannon too.”

While practicing this battle skill, Lan Zao had experienced a kind of resonance, gaining valuable insights.

With these insights, he quickly mastered the force control of the Air Cannon.

Now, he could precisely compress air and control the timing of the explosion. For instance, whether it detonated upon hitting the water's surface or after submerging beneath it.

“Too bad I don't worship the Dueling God.”

“If I had converted, the experience I gained wouldn't be so fragmented, like scattered pieces.”

"Come to think of it, having a little faith isn't such a bad thing. At least, it benefits me."

Lan Zao mused inwardly.

He was unaware of the arrangements Bing Xiao had made for their group. Fortunately, his faith had always been rather scattered. He worshipped the Sea God, the Wind God, and even the Fishnet Goddess. Thus, adding a new deity, the Dueling God, didn't feel like a contradiction.

However, such superficial devotion could never reach the devout level.

Without becoming a holy warrior, he faced the prospect of imprisonment.

Chapter 700: Eliminating the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps

The snow elf warrior Gu Dong knelt on both knees, offering a prayer.

“Before the deities we revere, worship, and honor, we present our sincere and exalted supplication.”

“Dueling God, you are the immortal champion, invincible yet eternally vigorous.”

"In the divine domain known as Tranquil Hill, the duelists rest in peace. When the Lord summons them, they shall rise again, taking up arms once more!"

“Dueling God, hear our prayers. Let our hearts' springs gush forth, let our strength converge into a mighty torrent.”

"Hear our confessions, let myriad voices merge into one vast ocean, joining hands amidst the surging currents. Under your supreme majestic power, grant us the exchange of spirit, let our messages traverse the void, reaching distant lands."

“Praise be to You, the tireless champion who marches onward, the sacred radiance. May You forever walk beside us, filling us with the warrior spirit to fight for You, for Your faith shines with glorious light!”

Gu Dong closed his eyes tightly, his devotion profound.

As a holy warrior of the Dueling God, he possessed great authority. This being a special prayer, his spirit was swiftly drawn into the divine domain of Tranquil Hill.

Gu Dong's spirit ascended, rising higher and higher until it reached a vast expanse of heaven and earth.

The sky was white, the ground gray.

Snowflakes as soft as goose feathers drifted silently down.

His spirit descended upon the summit of a snow-capped peak.

The mountaintop appeared as if carved by a blade, forming an exceptionally flat platform.

Scattered across the platform stood numerous tombstones, arranged in a staggered pattern.

Were the Bayonet Gang members to witness this scene, they would surely be astonished. For the positioning of these tombstones mirrored that of Tranquil Hill exactly.

Gu Dong's spirit remained focused, concentrated within his own tombstone.

Upon his arrival, several tombstones had already begun to glow.

After a brief wait, nearly twenty tombstones emitted a faint light.

Each tombstone represented a gold holy warrior. They had offered prayers, just as Gu Dong had done. At this very moment, these holy warriors were scattered across the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Yet, through their similar prayers, they had temporarily gained the ability to communicate across distances.

This was no ordinary prayer.

Such long-distance communication would consume a vast amount of divine favor from the holy warriors.

Under normal circumstances, they would never resort to this method to contact one another.

But this matter was of grave importance. Upon learning of it through Bing Xiao's prayer, many sprang into action immediately.

From one of the faintly glowing tombstones, a mental wave emanated, carrying a message tinged with sorrow: “As expected, Jia Bing is already dead. Two other holy warriors have also perished.”

Gu Dong knew who this mental transmission belonged to.

Her name was Xue Tuan Zi, a snow elf magician.

Xue Tuan Zi was unfamiliar with the Axe Gang leader or Lin. But as an alchemist magician, she had collaborated with Jia Bing on several occasions.

The glow on another tombstone brightened, sending forth a clear mental wave: “Since becoming a holy warrior, I've never witnessed so many die simultaneously.”

Gu Dong recognized this spirit as well.

His name was Zhu Gan, an elf ranger. Though also an elf, he wasn't a native snow elf of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, but a wood elf.

Gu Dong had met him once. Zhu Gan liked to wear a bamboo-woven conical hat and drape a cloak over his shoulders. His favorite pastime was fishing.

"Hmph, that's just your narrow-mindedness talking. Didn't several holy warriors perish during that Duel Festival over a decade ago?" A coarse, rugged spiritual fluctuation conveyed the words.

This was the holy warrior Mo Gui Ji.

Another holy warrior, He Gai Tou, held a different view: “The two situations are not the same. Dying in the Duel Festival is a worthy end for a holy warrior, a warrior of the Duel God.”
